    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT — Planetary exterior artwork
 * Original, self-contained Canvas illustrations; deterministic, prerendered once per world.
 * Draws ONLY the negative space behind the station. Rooms, corridors, doors, collision,
 * floors and furniture are drawn on top by LunaMap and remain unchanged.
 * 1700x1200 offscreen atlas gives smooth camera scrolling without per-frame terrain generation.
 */
class MoonshiftTerrain {
  constructor() {
    this.width=1700; this.height=1200;
    this.worldWidth=3400; this.worldHeight=2400;
    this.canvases=new Map();
    this.colors={ moon:'#79808f', mars:'#b95b41', orbit:'#071321' };
  }
  rng(seed) {let x=seed>>>0;return () => {x^=x<<13;x^=x>>>17;x^=x<<5;return (x>>>0)/4294967296;};}
  ellipse(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,fill,stroke,strokeWidth=1,angle=0){
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,rx,ry,angle,0,Math.PI*2);
    if(fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();}
    if(stroke){ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=strokeWidth;ctx.stroke();}
  }
  colorStops(ctx,colors,w=this.width,h=this.height){
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,w,h);
    colors.forEach(([p,c])=>g.addColorStop(p,c));
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
  }
  // Hand-drawn elliptical craters, soft rim on sunward side and a shaded interior.
  crater(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,moon=true,angle=0){
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(angle);
    const rim=moon?'rgba(204,215,225,.32)':'rgba(249,163,123,.31)';
    const shade=moon?'rgba(42,53,72,.24)':'rgba(83,33,33,.27)';
    const inner=moon?'rgba(77,86,103,.30)':'rgba(119,43,37,.30)';
    this.ellipse(ctx,3,5,rx*1.08,ry*1.12,shade);
    this.ellipse(ctx,0,0,rx*1.08,ry*1.06,rim);
    this.ellipse(ctx,1,2,rx*.89,ry*.83,inner);
    this.ellipse(ctx,1,4,rx*.66,ry*.57,moon?'rgba(70,80,99,.12)':'rgba(102,40,36,.14)');
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,rx,ry,0,Math.PI*1.1,Math.PI*1.91);
    ctx.strokeStyle=moon?'rgba(226,232,239,.58)':'rgba(255,178,135,.60)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.8,rx*.075);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(1,3,rx*.89,ry*.82,0,0,Math.PI*.93);
    ctx.strokeStyle=shade;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,rx*.045);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  rock(ctx,x,y,r,moon,rng){
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate((rng()-.5)*.6);
    const dark=moon?'rgba(48,61,78,.24)':'rgba(92,39,38,.20)';
    const light=moon?'rgba(193,207,217,.42)':'rgba(250,164,123,.38)';
    this.ellipse(ctx,r*.4,r*.75,r*1.35,r*.55,dark);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-r,0);ctx.lineTo(-r*.42,-r*.85);ctx.lineTo(r*.42,-r*.95);ctx.lineTo(r*.95,-r*.1);ctx.lineTo(r*.62,r*.55);ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle=moon?'rgba(102,118,138,.52)':'rgba(162,72,55,.49)';ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-r,0);ctx.lineTo(-r*.42,-r*.85);ctx.lineTo(r*.42,-r*.95);ctx.lineTo(-r*.15,-r*.17);ctx.closePath();ctx.fillStyle=light;ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  dune(ctx,w,h,moon,rng){
    // Curvilinear elevation bands, no obvious repeated tiled ground grid.
    for(let i=0;i<13;i++){
      const y=(i+.25)*h/12 +(rng()-.5)*74;
      const height=20+rng()*45;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-150,y);
      let x=-150;
      while(x<w+150){const len=190+rng()*200;ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+len*.25,y-height,x+len*.72,y+height*.65,x+len,y+(rng()-.5)*34);x+=len;}
      ctx.lineTo(w+150,h);ctx.lineTo(-150,h);ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle=moon?(i%3===0?'rgba(222,229,236,.032)':'rgba(48,63,82,.027)'):(i%3===0?'rgba(255,187,135,.047)':'rgba(97,37,36,.037)');ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  paintMoon(ctx){
    const w=this.width,h=this.height,rng=this.rng(17713);
    this.colorStops(ctx,[[0,'#586879'],[.45,'#4f5e70'],[1,'#415265']]);
    this.dune(ctx,w,h,true,rng);
    // Sparse, sculpted crater groups: visually distinct, but quiet under the room artwork.
    const main=[
      [130,120,75,56,-.28],[430,80,44,32,.15],[830,150,64,48,-.2],
      [1360,120,100,68,-.12],[1570,390,70,49,.25],[350,470,63,45,.08],
      [980,555,88,58,-.15],[1225,880,58,44,.26],[1510,1045,94,67,-.18],
      [240,1010,105,68,.1],[710,980,49,38,-.05]
    ];
    main.forEach(([x,y,rx,ry,a])=>this.crater(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,true,a));
    for(let i=0;i<27;i++){
      let x=65+rng()*(w-130),y=65+rng()*(h-130),r=8+rng()*18;
      this.crater(ctx,x,y,r,r*(.50+rng()*.20),true,(rng()-.5)*.65);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<126;i++)this.rock(ctx,15+rng()*(w-30),15+rng()*(h-30),1.5+rng()*6.5,true,rng);
    // Very subtle flattened ridgelines for a premium hand-painted 2D surface.
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
      const x=rng()*w,y=rng()*h;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-50,y+9);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x,y-10-rng()*18,x+65,y+6);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(214,225,231,.09)';ctx.lineWidth=1.6;ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  paintMars(ctx){
    const w=this.width,h=this.height,rng=this.rng(27977);
    this.colorStops(ctx,[[0,'#c56547'],[.5,'#b4533c'],[1,'#a1493b']]);
    // Long rounded sediment layers from low sun; matches the soft cartoony references.
    this.dune(ctx,w,h,false,rng);
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
      let x=(i*.31*w)%w,y=(i*.21*h)%h,span=210+i*17;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-span,y+50);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-span*.55,y-40,x+span*.1,y-65,x+span,y+18);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+span*.55,y+35,x+span*.2,y+40,x-span,y+50);
      ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'rgba(240,146,100,.052)':'rgba(91,37,32,.045)';ctx.fill();
    }
    const main=[
      [145,105,66,44,.2],[420,270,58,40,-.1],[780,135,78,52,-.2],
      [1285,150,94,64,.07],[1580,460,69,48,-.2],[1120,570,82,50,.13],
      [370,815,64,38,.11],[905,1035,55,39,-.2],[1420,980,86,58,-.05]
    ];
    main.forEach(([x,y,rx,ry,a])=>this.crater(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,false,a));
    for(let i=0;i<20;i++){
      const x=20+rng()*(w-40),y=25+rng()*(h-50),r=9+rng()*17;
      this.crater(ctx,x,y,r,r*(.53+rng()*.15),false,(rng()-.5)*.8);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<114;i++)this.rock(ctx,20+rng()*(w-40),20+rng()*(h-40),2+rng()*7.8,false,rng);
  }
  paintOrbit(ctx){
    const w=this.width,h=this.height,rng=this.rng(9013);
    this.colorStops(ctx,[[0,'#061321'],[.45,'#091729'],[1,'#07111f']]);
    // Quiet star field, not an artificial grid; all detail cached once.
    for(let i=0;i<220;i++){
      const x=rng()*w,y=rng()*h,r=rng()>.93?1.6:0.55+rng()*.7;
      this.ellipse(ctx,x,y,r,r,`rgba(220,239,255,${.2+rng()*.67})`);
    }
    // The exterior is quiet open space. Do not show an enormous, flat Earth
    // behind every room: the real observation happens inside the cupola.
    // A distant illuminated atmospheric arc is glimpsed at the far edge only.
    ctx.save();
    ctx.lineWidth=3;
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(83,155,203,.19)';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(1700,1020,240,-Math.PI*.98,-Math.PI*.42);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth=20;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(33,93,136,.04)';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(1700,1020,251,-Math.PI*.98,-Math.PI*.42);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    // Small, sparse orbital research satellite far away.
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(1320,165);ctx.rotate(.24);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(126,155,193,.58)';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(200,229,248,.68)';ctx.lineWidth=2;
    ctx.fillRect(-48,-9,30,18);ctx.fillRect(20,-9,30,18);ctx.fillRect(-14,-5,26,10);
    ctx.strokeRect(-48,-9,30,18);ctx.strokeRect(20,-9,30,18);
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-48+10*i,-9);ctx.lineTo(-48+10*i,9);ctx.moveTo(20+10*i,-9);ctx.lineTo(20+10*i,9);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  get(world){
    const type=this.colors[world]?world:'moon';
    if(this.canvases.has(type))return this.canvases.get(type);
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=this.width;canvas.height=this.height;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});
    if(type==='moon')this.paintMoon(ctx);else if(type==='mars')this.paintMars(ctx);else this.paintOrbit(ctx);
    this.canvases.set(type,canvas);
    return canvas;
  }
  render(ctx,camera,world){
    const type=this.colors[world]?world:'moon';
    const image=this.get(type);
    const sx=this.width/this.worldWidth,sy=this.height/this.worldHeight;
    const left=Math.max(0,camera.x),top=Math.max(0,camera.y);
    const right=Math.min(this.worldWidth,camera.x+camera.w),bottom=Math.min(this.worldHeight,camera.y+camera.h);
    ctx.fillStyle=this.colors[type];ctx.fillRect(camera.x-3,camera.y-3,camera.w+6,camera.h+6);
    if(right<=left||bottom<=top)return;
    ctx.drawImage(image,left*sx,top*sy,(right-left)*sx,(bottom-top)*sy,left,top,right-left,bottom-top);
  }
}
window.WorldTerrain=new MoonshiftTerrain();

</script>

            {

            },
            {
                id: 'terminal_hab_holo',
                roomId: 'centralHab',

                shortLabel: 'NOVA Globe',

                icon: '🌐',

            },
            {

                roomId: 'quarters',

                x: 1120, y: 525, radius: 40,

        // Animated environmental details (fans, holographic beam, LEDs, hydroponic sprinklers)
        this.fanAngle = 0;
        this.holoRotation = 0;
        this.pulsePhase = 0;
        this.roomFloorCache = {}; // prerender detailed floor art once, not 60x per second
        this.backdropTexture = null;
    }

    update(dt, playerX, playerY) {
        this.fanAngle = (this.fanAngle + dt * 5.0) % (Math.PI * 2);
        this.holoRotation = (this.holoRotation + dt * 0.8) % (Math.PI * 2);
        this.pulsePhase = (this.pulsePhase + dt * 2.5) % (Math.PI * 2);
        this.playerX = playerX;
        this.playerY = playerY;

        // Functional, proximity-triggered airlock doors. The motion is synchronized with
        // player collision: shut doors block, open doors are physically traversable.
        // The separate opening/closing distances avoid jitter at the sensor boundary.
        for (const door of this.doors) {
            const nearestX = Math.max(door.x, Math.min(playerX, door.x + door.w));
            const nearestY = Math.max(door.y, Math.min(playerY, door.y + door.h));
            const distance = Math.hypot(playerX - nearestX, playerY - nearestY);
            const sensorRange = door.targetOpen ? 218 : 175;
            const shouldOpen = !door.locked && distance < sensorRange;
            if (shouldOpen && !door.targetOpen) {
                // At most one subtle pneumatic cue while approaching neighboring doors.
                const clock = performance.now();
                if (!this._lastDoorChime || clock - this._lastDoorChime > 950) {
                    window.Audio?.doorOpen?.();
                    this._lastDoorChime = clock;
                }
            }
            door.targetOpen = shouldOpen ? 1 : 0;
            door.open += (door.targetOpen - door.open) * Math.min(1, dt * 9);
            if (Math.abs(door.targetOpen - door.open) < .008) door.open = door.targetOpen;
        }
    }

    cubicPoint(b, t) {
        const u=1-t, u2=u*u, t2=t*t;
        const k0=u2*u, k1=3*u2*t, k2=3*u*t2, k3=t2*t;
        return {x:k0*b.p0[0]+k1*b.p1[0]+k2*b.p2[0]+k3*b.p3[0],
                y:k0*b.p0[1]+k1*b.p1[1]+k2*b.p2[1]+k3*b.p3[1]};
    }
    cubicSamples(b, count=36) {
        const pts=[];
        for(let i=0;i<=count;i++)pts.push(this.cubicPoint(b,i/count));
        return pts;
    }
    // Geometry-based walkability follows the same path used to paint the curved
    // connector, rather than the old square bounding boxes.
    isPointInCorridor(c, px, py) {
        if(!c.bend)return px>=c.x&&px<=c.x+c.w&&py>=c.y&&py<=c.y+c.h;
        if(!c._samples)c._samples=this.cubicSamples(c.bend);
        const threshold=(c.bend.width/2)**2;
        for(let i=1;i<c._samples.length;i++){
            const a=c._samples[i-1],b=c._samples[i];
            const dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;
            const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((px-a.x)*dx+(py-a.y)*dy)/(dx*dx+dy*dy)));
            const qx=a.x+t*dx,qy=a.y+t*dy;
            if((px-qx)**2+(py-qy)**2<=threshold)return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Determine which room a point is in
    getRoomAt(x, y) {
        for (const key in this.rooms) {
            const r = this.rooms[key].bounds;
            if (x >= r.x && x <= r.x + r.w && y >= r.y && y <= r.y + r.h) {
                return this.rooms[key];
            }
        }
        for (const c of this.corridors) {
            if (this.isPointInCorridor(c, x, y)) {
                return { id: 'corridor', name: 'Corridor', description: 'Pressurized transit conduit.' };
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    // Check if a point is within any room or corridor interior
    isPointWalkable(px, py) {
        // 1. Station Rooms (with small 8px margin for outer walls)
        for (const key in this.rooms) {
            const r = this.rooms[key].bounds;
            if (px >= r.x + 8 && px <= r.x + r.w - 8 &&
                py >= r.y + 8 && py <= r.y + r.h - 8) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        // 2. Pressurized Corridors (seamless overlap with rooms)
        for (const c of this.corridors) {
            if (this.isPointInCorridor(c, px, py)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Check collision with station walls and locked doors
    isPassable(x, y, radius = 20) {
        // Test cardinal points on player circumference
        const angles = [0, Math.PI / 2, Math.PI, (3 * Math.PI) / 2];
        for (let i = 0; i < angles.length; i++) {
            const tx = x + Math.cos(angles[i]) * radius;
            const ty = y + Math.sin(angles[i]) * radius;
            if (!this.isPointWalkable(tx, ty)) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        // Sliding door collision tracks the visible shutter animation.
        // A partially-open slit is not yet large enough for the astronaut's suit.
        for (const door of this.doors) {
            if (door.locked || door.open < .76) {
                const dx = door.x - radius;
                const dy = door.y - radius;
                const dw = door.w + radius * 2;
                const dh = door.h + radius * 2;
                if (x >= dx && x <= dx + dw && y >= dy && y <= dy + dh) return false;
            }
        }

        // Check central holo-table in Central Hab (Moon-only fallback; Orbit/Mars use world-specific colliders)
        if ((this.currentWorld || 'moon') === 'moon') {
            const habCenter = { x: 1275, y: 1225, radius: 46 };
            if (Math.hypot(x - habCenter.x, y - habCenter.y) < (radius + habCenter.radius)) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    // Get the closest interactable item, not merely the first match in array order.
    getNearbyInteractable(x, y, range = 72) {
        let nearest = null;
        let nearestDist = Infinity;
        for (const item of this.interactables) {
            const dist = Math.hypot(x - item.x, y - item.y);
            const threshold = range + (item.radius || 0);
            if (dist <= threshold && dist < nearestDist) {
                nearest = item;
                nearestDist = dist;
            }
        }
        return nearest;
    }

    // Corridor geometry is always open at its ends. Rounded joints, never
    // the closed capsule outline that made doorways look like floating obstacles.
    corridorPathData(c) {
        if(c.bend){
            const b=c.bend;
            return `M ${b.p0.join(' ')} C ${b.p1.join(' ')}, ${b.p2.join(' ')}, ${b.p3.join(' ')}`;
        }
        return `M ${c.x} ${c.y} H ${c.x+c.w} V ${c.y+c.h} H ${c.x} Z`;
    }
    roomPortalOpenings(room) {
        const b = room.bounds;
        const margin = 30;
        const openings = [];
        const add = (side, start, end) => {
            start = Math.max(side === 'top' || side === 'bottom' ? b.x + margin : b.y + margin, start);
            end = Math.min(side === 'top' || side === 'bottom' ? b.x + b.w - margin : b.y + b.h - margin, end);
            if (end - start < 26) return;
            openings.push({ side, start, end });
        };
        const touch = 26;
        for (const c of this.corridors) {
            if (c.bend) {
                const width = (c.bend.width || Math.min(c.w || 120, c.h || 120)) * 0.72;
                for (const pt of [c.bend.p0, c.bend.p3]) {
                    const [px, py] = pt;
                    if (Math.abs(py - b.y) <= touch && px >= b.x - touch && px <= b.x + b.w + touch) add('top', px - width / 2, px + width / 2);
                    if (Math.abs(py - (b.y + b.h)) <= touch && px >= b.x - touch && px <= b.x + b.w + touch) add('bottom', px - width / 2, px + width / 2);
                    if (Math.abs(px - b.x) <= touch && py >= b.y - touch && py <= b.y + b.h + touch) add('left', py - width / 2, py + width / 2);
                    if (Math.abs(px - (b.x + b.w)) <= touch && py >= b.y - touch && py <= b.y + b.h + touch) add('right', py - width / 2, py + width / 2);
                }
                if(c.link?.includes(room.id)) {
                    const inside=(p)=>p.x>=b.x&&p.x<=b.x+b.w&&p.y>=b.y&&p.y<=b.y+b.h;
                    let previous=this.cubicPoint(c.bend,0);
                    for(let step=1;step<=160;step++){
                        const current=this.cubicPoint(c.bend,step/160);
                        if(inside(previous)!==inside(current)) {
                            const point={x:(previous.x+current.x)/2,y:(previous.y+current.y)/2};
                            const distances={top:Math.abs(point.y-b.y),bottom:Math.abs(point.y-(b.y+b.h)),left:Math.abs(point.x-b.x),right:Math.abs(point.x-(b.x+b.w))};
                            const side=Object.keys(distances).sort((a,z)=>distances[a]-distances[z])[0];
                            const center=(side==='top'||side==='bottom')?point.x:point.y;
                            add(side,center-width/2,center+width/2);
                        }
                        previous=current;
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }
            const overlapX0 = Math.max(c.x, b.x + margin), overlapX1 = Math.min(c.x + c.w, b.x + b.w - margin);
            const overlapY0 = Math.max(c.y, b.y + margin), overlapY1 = Math.min(c.y + c.h, b.y + b.h - margin);
            if (overlapX1 > overlapX0) {
                if (c.y <= b.y + touch && c.y + c.h >= b.y - touch) add('top', overlapX0, overlapX1);
                if (c.y <= b.y + b.h + touch && c.y + c.h >= b.y + b.h - touch) add('bottom', overlapX0, overlapX1);
            }
            if (overlapY1 > overlapY0) {
                if (c.x <= b.x + touch && c.x + c.w >= b.x - touch) add('left', overlapY0, overlapY1);
                if (c.x <= b.x + b.w + touch && c.x + c.w >= b.x + b.w - touch) add('right', overlapY0, overlapY1);
            }
        }
        const bySide = { top: [], bottom: [], left: [], right: [] };
        for (const item of openings) bySide[item.side].push(item);
        const merged = [];
        for (const side of Object.keys(bySide)) {
            const list = bySide[side].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
            for (const item of list) {
                const last = merged.length && merged[merged.length - 1].side === side ? merged[merged.length - 1] : null;
                if (last && item.start <= last.end + 12) last.end = Math.max(last.end, item.end);
                else merged.push({ ...item });
            }
        }
        return merged;
    }

    renderRoomPortals(ctx, room) {
        // A doorway is an EMPTY opening in the wall — NOT another transverse gate.
        // Floor is restored through the wall; only small jambs remain at the ends.
        const b=room.bounds;
        for (const op of this.roomPortalOpenings(room)) {
            const s=op.start, size=op.end-op.start;
            if (size < 30) continue;
            ctx.save();
            const horizontal=op.side==='top'||op.side==='bottom';
            const bx=horizontal?s:(op.side==='left'?b.x-9:b.x+b.w-11);
            const by=horizontal?(op.side==='top'?b.y-11:b.y+b.h-11):s;
            const w=horizontal?size:22, h=horizontal?22:size;
            ctx.fillStyle='#263b49';
            ctx.fillRect(bx,by,w,h); // Continuous corridor floor, no cap.
            const rail='#344a55', light='rgba(156,188,197,.28)';
            const post=(x,y,pw,ph)=>{
                ctx.fillStyle=rail;ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x,y,pw,ph,Math.min(4,pw/2,ph/2));ctx.fill();
                ctx.fillStyle=light;
                if(horizontal)ctx.fillRect(x+1,y+2,Math.max(1,pw-2),2);
                else ctx.fillRect(x+2,y+1,2,Math.max(1,ph-2));
            };
            if(horizontal) {
                post(s-4,by,7,22);post(s+size-3,by,7,22);
            } else {
                post(bx,s-4,22,7);post(bx,s+size-3,22,7);
            }
            ctx.restore();
        }
    }

    renderCorridor(ctx,c) {
        // Keep the navigable corridor geometry deliberately connected to both rooms,
        // but trim the painted bridge to the actual door/wall boundary at each end.
        // This prevents passage floors, shadows and rails from intruding into rooms.
        let {x,y,w,h}=c;
        if(!c.bend && c.link?.length===2) {
            const rooms=c.link.map(id=>this.rooms[id]).filter(Boolean);
            if(rooms.length===2){
                const [A,B]=rooms.map(r=>r.bounds);
                if(c.axis==='h'||c.w>c.h){
                    const [left,right]=A.x<B.x?[A,B]:[B,A];
                    x=left.x+left.w; w=Math.max(0,right.x-x);
                }else{
                    const [top,bottom]=A.y<B.y?[A,B]:[B,A];
                    y=top.y+top.h; h=Math.max(0,bottom.y-y);
                }
            }
        }
        ctx.save();
        if(c.bend){
            // Bézier connectors start inside two rooms to keep collision seamless.
            // Mask those interior sections out of the rendered bridge (not the walkable route).
            ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(0,0,this.width||3400,this.height||2400);
            for(const r of Object.values(this.rooms||{})){
                const b=r.bounds;ctx.rect(b.x,b.y,b.w,b.h);
            }
            ctx.clip('evenodd');
            const b=c.bend, pts=c._samples||(c._samples=this.cubicSamples(b));
            const d=new Path2D(this.corridorPathData(c));
            ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineCap='butt';
            // A soft, continuous passage floor: no outline or transverse end caps.
            ctx.strokeStyle='#182b38';ctx.lineWidth=b.width+7;ctx.stroke(d);
            ctx.strokeStyle='#263b49';ctx.lineWidth=b.width;ctx.stroke(d);
            ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(120,154,166,.04)';ctx.lineWidth=b.width-22;ctx.stroke(d);
            // The two long edge rails follow the bend; the ends simply fade into
            // the adjacent floor. The player sees an opening, not a solid pod.
            for(const sign of [-1,1]){
                ctx.beginPath();
                for(let i=1;i<pts.length-1;i++){
                    const a=pts[i-1],q=pts[i],z=pts[i+1];
                    const vx=z.x-a.x,vy=z.y-a.y,len=Math.hypot(vx,vy)||1;
                    const off=b.width/2-9;
                    const px=q.x+sign*(-vy/len)*off,py=q.y+sign*(vx/len)*off;
                    if(i===1)ctx.moveTo(px,py);else ctx.lineTo(px,py);
                }
                ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,26,37,.40)';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.stroke();
                ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(126,175,194,.20)';ctx.lineWidth=1.3;ctx.stroke();
            }
            // Few small recessed floor joints along the route, never across the doorway.
            for(let i=5;i<pts.length-5;i+=10){
                const q=pts[i],p=pts[i-1],n=pts[i+1],vx=n.x-p.x,vy=n.y-p.y,l=Math.hypot(vx,vy)||1;
                ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(q.x-vx/l*3,q.y-vy/l*3);ctx.lineTo(q.x+vx/l*3,q.y+vy/l*3);
                ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(215,177,134,.40)';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.stroke();
            }
        }else{
            // Straight connectors: open rectangles with only longitudinal rails.
            // Never draw any caps across the direction of travel.
            ctx.fillStyle='#203644';ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
            const horizontal=w>h;
            const grad=horizontal?ctx.createLinearGradient(0,y,0,y+h):ctx.createLinearGradient(x,0,x+w,0);
            grad.addColorStop(0,'rgba(11,28,41,.35)');grad.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(95,137,154,.10)');grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(11,28,41,.35)');
            ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
            for(const side of [-1,1]){
                ctx.beginPath();
                if(horizontal){const ey=y+h/2+side*(h/2-10);ctx.moveTo(x,ey);ctx.lineTo(x+w,ey);}
                else{const ex=x+w/2+side*(w/2-10);ctx.moveTo(ex,y);ctx.lineTo(ex,y+h);}
                ctx.lineCap='butt';ctx.lineWidth=7;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,26,37,.70)';ctx.stroke();
                ctx.lineWidth=1.4;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(132,185,202,.31)';ctx.stroke();
            }
        }
        ctx.restore();
    }

    // Render the complete 2D illustrated LUNA-7 map
    render(ctx, camera) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(-camera.x, -camera.y);

        // 1. Deep Space Cosmic Background
        this.renderStationHull(ctx,camera);

        // 2. Render 10 Room Floors & Interiors
        for (const key in this.rooms) {
            const room = this.rooms[key];
            const b = room.bounds;
            if(b.x > camera.x+camera.w+70 || b.x+b.w < camera.x-70 || b.y > camera.y+camera.h+70 || b.y+b.h < camera.y-70) continue;

            // Room Floor Base
            ctx.fillStyle = room.floorColor;
            ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);

            // High-fidelity illustrated floor tiles, rivets, accents and service tracks.
            this.renderPremiumFloor(ctx, room);

            // The prerendered floor already contains restrained architectural
            // seams. A second overlay grid would make the rooms feel cluttered.

            // Room-Specific Interior Artwork
            this.renderRoomDetails(ctx, room);
            this.renderFurniture(ctx, room);

            // Outer Perimeter Bulkhead Walls
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
            ctx.lineWidth = 16;
            ctx.strokeRect(b.x + 8, b.y + 8, b.w - 16, b.h - 16);

            // Room plaque placed in reserved wall margin; always contained inside a pill.
            ctx.save();
            const label=(room.shortName || room.name).replace(' (ECLSS)','').toUpperCase();
            ctx.font='700 12px "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
            ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
            const plaqueW=Math.min(b.w-66,Math.max(120,ctx.measureText(label).width+27));
            const plaqueX=b.x+32,plaqueY=b.y+23;
            ctx.fillStyle='rgba(8,22,32,.91)';
            ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(plaqueX,plaqueY,plaqueW,30,8);ctx.fill();
            ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(137,175,188,.21)';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.stroke();
            ctx.fillStyle='#d7e8ee';ctx.fillText(label,plaqueX+12,plaqueY+15,plaqueW-24);
            ctx.restore();
        }

        // 3. Sculpted, curved-shoulder passageways. The visual passage matches the
        // logical corridors, with entrance flares blending into the connected rooms.
        for (const c of this.corridors) {
            if(c.x>camera.x+camera.w+70 || c.x+c.w<camera.x-70 || c.y>camera.y+camera.h+70 || c.y+c.h<camera.y-70) continue;
            this.renderCorridor(ctx,c);
        }

        // 4. Cut clear architectural openings wherever a corridor meets a room.
        for (const key in this.rooms) {
            this.renderRoomPortals(ctx, this.rooms[key]);
        }

        // 5. Render Bulkhead Doors only where a real pressure boundary exists.
        for (const door of this.doors) {
            this.renderDoor(ctx, door);
        }

        // 6. Render Interactive Consoles & Task Stations
        for (const item of this.interactables) {
            if(item.x<camera.x-100||item.x>camera.x+camera.w+100||item.y<camera.y-100||item.y>camera.y+camera.h+100) continue;
            this.renderInteractable(ctx, item);
            const target=window.MissionUI?.getTarget();
            if(target&&item.x===target.x&&item.y===target.y){
                const p=Math.sin(this.pulsePhase*2)*5;
                ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='#ffdb9b';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.setLineDash([8,7]);
                ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(item.x,item.y,item.radius+20+p,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
            }
        }

        ctx.restore();
    }

    /** Clean base floor fill while illustrated room art decodes. */
    renderPremiumFloor(ctx, room) {
        const b = room.bounds;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = room.floorColor || '#13202d';
        ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
        ctx.restore();
    }

    renderFurniture(ctx, room) {
        // Legacy procedural furniture removed; rooms use unified illustrated room art.
    }

    renderRoomDetails(ctx, room) {
        // Legacy procedural room details removed; rooms use unified illustrated room art.
    }

    renderDoor(ctx, door) {
        ctx.save();
        const horizontal=door.w>door.h;
        const progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,door.open));
        // Flush mechanical jambs: they sit on the sides of the passage, never
        // across a clear opening. No 'floating pill' or glowing crossbar.
        const jamb=8;
        ctx.fillStyle='#1b303c';
        ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(118,154,167,.52)';
        ctx.lineWidth=1.2;
        if (horizontal) {
            for (const x of [door.x,door.x+door.w-jamb]) {
                ctx.fillRect(x,door.y-7,jamb,door.h+14);
                ctx.strokeRect(x+.5,door.y-7.5,jamb-1,door.h+15);
            }
        } else {
            for (const y of [door.y,door.y+door.h-jamb]) {
                ctx.fillRect(door.x-7,y,door.w+14,jamb);
                ctx.strokeRect(door.x-7.5,y+.5,door.w+15,jamb-1);
            }
        }
        // Two genuinely sliding leaves: closed = full width, open = retracted
        // into their side pockets. The visual shutter matches collision exactly.
        const closed=1-progress;
        if (closed>.008) {
            const total=horizontal?door.w:door.h;
            const leaf=Math.max(0,(total/2-jamb)*closed);
            const paintLeaf=(x,y,w,h,innerEdge)=>{
                if(w<=.1||h<=.1)return;
                const metal=ctx.createLinearGradient(x,y,x+w,y+h);
                metal.addColorStop(0,door.locked?'#8b6156':'#49636f');
                metal.addColorStop(.58,door.locked?'#68453e':'#34505d');
                metal.addColorStop(1,'#253d4a');
                ctx.fillStyle=metal;
                ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
                ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(184,208,211,.35)';ctx.lineWidth=1;
                ctx.strokeRect(x+.5,y+.5,Math.max(0,w-1),Math.max(0,h-1));
                ctx.fillStyle='rgba(223,231,225,.13)';
                if(horizontal){ctx.fillRect(innerEdge?x+w-3:x+1,y+10,2,Math.max(0,h-20));}
                else{ctx.fillRect(x+10,innerEdge?y+h-3:y+1,Math.max(0,w-20),2);}
            };
            if(horizontal){
                paintLeaf(door.x+jamb,door.y,leaf,door.h,true);
                paintLeaf(door.x+door.w-jamb-leaf,door.y,leaf,door.h,false);
            }else{
                paintLeaf(door.x,door.y+jamb,door.w,leaf,true);
                paintLeaf(door.x,door.y+door.h-jamb-leaf,door.w,leaf,false);
            }
        }
        // Small status lamp recessed into a jamb; muted when shut, teal when open.
        const statusX=horizontal?door.x+4:door.x+door.w*.5;
        const statusY=horizontal?door.y+door.h*.5:door.y+4;
        ctx.fillStyle=door.locked?'#c17b69':progress>.76?'#81bda9':'#baa27c';
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(statusX,statusY,2.5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
    }

    renderInteractable(ctx, item) {
        ctx.save();
        const near = !!(window.Engine?.nearbyInteractable?.id === item.id);
        const objective = window.MissionUI?.getTarget();
        const important = !!(objective && Math.abs(objective.x - item.x) < 1 && Math.abs(objective.y - item.y) < 1);
        const cx = Math.round(item.x);
        const cy = Math.round(item.y);
        const r = Math.min(25, Math.max(18, Math.round(item.radius * 0.65)));

        // Perfectly concentric interaction pad: centered glow, base disc, inner ring, outer ring.
        const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, r * 0.35, cx, cy, r + 26);
        glow.addColorStop(0, near ? 'rgba(123, 217, 242, 0.24)' : 'rgba(108, 196, 221, 0.10)');
        glow.addColorStop(0.58, near ? 'rgba(123, 217, 242, 0.12)' : 'rgba(108, 196, 221, 0.05)');
        glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(108, 196, 221, 0)');
        ctx.fillStyle = glow;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, r + 26, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.fillStyle = '#13283a';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(63, 103, 122, 0.95)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, r - 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.strokeStyle = near ? '#f1d4a0' : (important ? '#9fd8ea' : item.color);
        ctx.lineWidth = near ? 4 : 3;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, r + 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();

        if (near || important) {
            const pulse = Math.sin(this.pulsePhase * 2.2) * 2.2;
            ctx.strokeStyle = near ? 'rgba(241, 212, 160, 0.95)' : 'rgba(159, 216, 234, 0.72)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx, cy, r + 13 + pulse, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.stroke();
        }

        const world = this.currentWorld || 'moon';
        const artKey = (world === 'orbit' && item.id === 'task_greenhouse') ? 'planetLine' :
          (world === 'orbit' && item.id === 'task_rover') ? 'satellite' :
          (world === 'mars' && item.id === 'task_gravity') ? 'rover' :
          ({'task_greenhouse':'plant','task_rover':'rover','task_gravity':'moon','terminal_obs':'telescopeLine','task_comms':'satellite','task_water':'water','task_power':'energy'})[item.id];
        const licensedKey = ({task_power:'power',task_scrubber:'air',task_water:'water',task_greenhouse:'plant',task_comms:'radio',task_shelter:'warning',terminal_obs:'telescope',task_thermal:'power'})[item.id];
        const licensed = licensedKey && window.MoonshiftIcons?.canvas?.[licensedKey];
        const vector = (licensed?.complete && licensed.naturalWidth ? licensed : null) || (artKey && window.SpaceArt?.icons?.[artKey]);
        if (vector?.complete && vector.naturalWidth) ctx.drawImage(vector, cx - 15, cy - 15, 30, 30);
        else {
            ctx.font = '22px "Segoe UI Emoji", sans-serif';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#dcecf2';
            ctx.fillText(item.icon, cx, cy);
        }
        ctx.restore();
    }

    /** Planet-specific exterior only. Interior floors and corridors retain their existing art. */
    renderStationHull(ctx, camera) {
        if(window.WorldTerrain){
            window.WorldTerrain.render(ctx,camera,this.currentWorld || 'moon');
            return;
        }
        ctx.fillStyle='#0b1822';
        ctx.fillRect(camera.x,camera.y,camera.w,camera.h);
    }

    renderCosmicBackdrop(ctx, camera) {
        // Render distant stars visible outside station bounds
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
        const seedStars = [
            { x: 100, y: 150, r: 1.5 }, { x: 450, y: 80, r: 2.0 },
            { x: 1200, y: 120, r: 1.0 }, { x: 2100, y: 90, r: 2.2 },
            { x: 3100, y: 250, r: 1.8 }, { x: 3300, y: 800, r: 1.2 },
            { x: 3200, y: 1800, r: 2.0 }, { x: 100, y: 2200, r: 1.5 }
        ];
        for (const s of seedStars) {
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        }
    }
}

// Global map singleton
window.StationMap = new LunaMap();

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT: THE LOST SIGNAL
 * Original 2D Characters & Animated Companion System
 * Pure 2D vector/sprite rendering with expressive animations, spring physics & emotes.
 */

class Player {
    constructor(x, y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.vx = 0;
        this.vy = 0;
        this.speed = 280; // pixels per second
        this.radius = 20;
        this.facing = 'down'; // 'down', 'up', 'left', 'right'
        this.walkTimer = 0;
        this.isMoving = false;
        
        // Customization
        this.suitColor = '#E7E8E4'; // Lunar Ceramic default
        this.accentColor = '#79AEC8'; // Technical Blue
        this.visorColor = '#79C9D8'; // Cyan gold specular
        this.playerName = 'Junior Astronaut';
    }

    update(dt, input, map) {
        let dx = 0;
        let dy = 0;

        if (input.left) dx -= 1;
        if (input.right) dx += 1;
        if (input.up) dy -= 1;
        if (input.down) dy += 1;

        // Virtual joystick / touch input support
        if (input.joystickActive) {
            dx = input.joyX;
            dy = input.joyY;
        }

        const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (len > 0) {
            this.isMoving = true;
            this.walkTimer += dt * 10.0;

            const normX = (dx / len);
            const normY = (dy / len);

            if (Math.abs(normX) > Math.abs(normY)) {
                this.facing = normX > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
            } else {
                this.facing = normY > 0 ? 'down' : 'up';
            }

            const targetVx = normX * this.speed;
            const targetVy = normY * this.speed;

            // Smooth acceleration
            this.vx += (targetVx - this.vx) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 14.0);
            this.vy += (targetVy - this.vy) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 14.0);

            // Footstep audio trigger
            if (Math.sin(this.walkTimer) > 0.9 && !this.stepped) {
                this.stepped = true;
                if (window.Audio) window.Audio.footstep('ceramic');
            } else if (Math.sin(this.walkTimer) < 0) {
                this.stepped = false;
            }
        } else {
            this.isMoving = false;
            // Smooth deceleration / friction
            this.vx *= Math.max(0, 1.0 - dt * 16.0);
            this.vy *= Math.max(0, 1.0 - dt * 16.0);
        }

        // Apply movement with wall collisions (slide along axes)
        const nextX = this.x + this.vx * dt;
        const nextY = this.y + this.vy * dt;

        if (map.isPassable(nextX, this.y, this.radius)) {
            this.x = nextX;
        }
        if (map.isPassable(this.x, nextY, this.radius)) {
            this.y = nextY;
        }
    }

    render(ctx) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(this.x, this.y);

        // Walking bounce
        const bob = this.isMoving ? Math.abs(Math.sin(this.walkTimer)) * 4 : 0;

        // 1. Soft Dynamic Shadow Under Character
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 18, 20, 9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.translate(0, -bob);

        // 2. Life Support Backpack (renders behind or beside based on facing)
        if (this.facing === 'up' || this.facing === 'left' || this.facing === 'right') {
            ctx.fillStyle = '#263644';
            const packX = this.facing === 'left' ? 6 : (this.facing === 'right' ? -20 : -14);
            const packY = -22;
            ctx.fillRect(packX, packY, 14, 28);
            ctx.strokeStyle = this.accentColor;
            ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            ctx.strokeRect(packX, packY, 14, 28);
        }

        // 3. Astronaut Legs / Boots
        const legOffset = this.isMoving ? Math.sin(this.walkTimer) * 6 : 0;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#263644';
        // Left foot
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(-8, 14 + legOffset, 7, 5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        // Right foot
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(8, 14 - legOffset, 7, 5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // 4. Spacesuit Body (Rounded Torso)
        ctx.fillStyle = this.suitColor;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, -2, 18, 20, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        ctx.stroke();

        // NASA Artemis Chest Patch
        ctx.fillStyle = '#79AEC8';
        ctx.fillRect(-6, -6, 12, 6);

        // 5. Helmet & Visor
        ctx.fillStyle = this.suitColor;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, -18, 17, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        ctx.stroke();

        // Visor (Facing aware)
        if (this.facing !== 'up') {
            const vx = this.facing === 'left' ? -7 : (this.facing === 'right' ? 7 : 0);
            ctx.fillStyle = this.visorColor;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(vx, -18, 12, 9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            // Specular Reflection Highlight on Visor
            ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(vx - 3, -21, 5, 2.5, -0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        }

        ctx.restore();
    }
}

class PixCompanion {
    constructor(player) {
        this.player = player;
        this.x = player.x - 40;
        this.y = player.y - 40;
        this.targetX = this.x;
        this.targetY = this.y;
        this.hoverTime = 0;
        this.emotion = 'happy'; // 'happy', 'alert', 'thinking', 'curious'
        this.speechBubble = '';
        this.speechTimer = 0;
        this.blinkTimer = 0;
        this.isBlinking = false;
    }

    update(dt) {
        this.hoverTime += dt * 3.5;
        this.blinkTimer += dt;
        if (this.blinkTimer > 3.0) {
            this.isBlinking = true;
            if (this.blinkTimer > 3.15) {
                this.isBlinking = false;
                this.blinkTimer = 0;
            }
        }

        // Float slightly behind player based on facing direction
        const offsets = {
            down: { x: -38, y: -45 },
            up: { x: 38, y: 35 },
            left: { x: 42, y: -35 },
            right: { x: -42, y: -35 }
        };
        const off = offsets[this.player.facing] || offsets.down;
        this.targetX = this.player.x + off.x;
        this.targetY = this.player.y + off.y + Math.sin(this.hoverTime) * 6;

        // Smooth spring physics follow
        this.x += (this.targetX - this.x) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 5.0);
        this.y += (this.targetY - this.y) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 5.0);

        // Speech bubble timer
        if (this.speechTimer > 0) {
            this.speechTimer -= dt;
            if (this.speechTimer <= 0) {
                this.speechBubble = '';
            }
        }
    }

    say(text, emotion = 'happy', duration = 4.0) {
        if(window.Story?.isDialogueActive){this.speechBubble='';this.speechTimer=0;return;}
        this.speechBubble = text;
        this.speechTimer = duration;
        this.emotion = emotion;
        if (window.Audio) {
            window.Audio.pixChirp(emotion);
        }
    }

    render(ctx) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(this.x, this.y);

        // Soft Thruster Glow underneath
        const thrusterGrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, 14, 2, 0, 18, 16);
        thrusterGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(121, 201, 216, 0.8)');
        thrusterGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(121, 201, 216, 0)');
        ctx.fillStyle = thrusterGrad;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 16, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Spherical Bot Body (Lunar Ceramic White)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E7E8E4';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 0, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        ctx.stroke();

        // Cute Metallic Antenna
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, -16);
        ctx.lineTo(0, -24);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Antenna beacon tip
        ctx.fillStyle = this.emotion === 'alert' ? '#DA7468' : '#79AEC8';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, -25, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Digital Screen Visor (Navy Blue OLED)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0B1520';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 11, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Digital LED Eyes (Expressive Blue / Cyan)
        ctx.fillStyle = this.emotion === 'alert' ? '#DA7468' : '#79C9D8';
        if (this.isBlinking) {
            // Horizontal blink line
            ctx.fillRect(-7, -1, 4, 2);
            ctx.fillRect(3, -1, 4, 2);
        } else if (this.emotion === 'happy') {
            // Joyful upward curving arc eyes (^_^)
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#79C9D8';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(-5, 1, 3.5, Math.PI * 1.1, Math.PI * 1.9);
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(5, 1, 3.5, Math.PI * 1.1, Math.PI * 1.9);
            ctx.stroke();
        } else if (this.emotion === 'alert') {
            // Wide alert circular rings (O_O)
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(-5, 0, 3.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(5, 0, 3.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        } else {
            // Normal friendly oval eyes
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(-5, 0, 2.5, 3.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(5, 0, 2.5, 3.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        }

        // Render Speech Bubble if active
        if (this.speechBubble) {
            ctx.restore();
            this.renderSpeechBubble(ctx);
            return;
        }

        ctx.restore();
    }

    renderSpeechBubble(ctx) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(this.x, this.y - 45);
        ctx.font = 'bold 12.5px "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
        const maxTextW = 224;
        const words = String(this.speechBubble || '').split(/\s+/);
        const lines = [];
        let cur = '';
        for (const w of words) {
            const test = cur ? cur + ' ' + w : w;
            if (ctx.measureText(test).width > maxTextW && cur) {
                lines.push(cur);
                cur = w;
            } else {
                cur = test;
            }
        }
        if (cur) lines.push(cur);
        if (!lines.length) lines.push('');
        const maxLineW = Math.max(...lines.map(l => ctx.measureText(l).width));
        const padX = 14, padY = 9, lineH = 16;
        const bw = Math.min(256, Math.max(90, maxLineW + padX * 2));
        const bh = lines.length * lineH + padY * 2;

        // Bubble Box + Seamless Pointer
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(22, 37, 50, 0.96)';
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#79AEC8';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(-bw / 2, -bh, bw, bh, 10);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-7, -1);
        ctx.lineTo(7, -1);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 8);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-7, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 8);
        ctx.lineTo(7, 0);
        ctx.stroke();

        // Wrapped Text
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E7E8E4';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        const startY = -bh + padY + lineH / 2;
        lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
            ctx.fillText(line, 0, startY + idx * lineH, bw - padX * 2);
        });
        ctx.restore();
    }
}

// Crew NPCs (Rawan, Abdelrahman, Rewaa; Fatema and Eyad provide guidance)
class StationCrew {
    constructor() {
        this.npcs = [
            {
                id: 'maya',
                name: 'Rawan',
                role: 'Lead Astrobiologist',
                x: 1420, y: 1100,
                color: '#79C9D8',
                facing: 'left',
                portrait: '🔬',
                dialogue: "Welcome to LUNA-7! I oversee our closed-loop life support and lunar crops."
            },
            {
                id: 'leo',
                name: 'Abdelrahman',
                role: 'Chief Systems Engineer',
                x: 1350, y: 1850,
                color: '#E9AD65',
                facing: 'up',
                portrait: '🔧',
                dialogue: "Hey Junior! Solar array tracking is my specialty. Keep an eye on battery depth-of-discharge!"
            },
            {
                id: 'sprout',
                name: 'Rewaa',
                role: 'Greenhouse Specialist',
                x: 2750, y: 1750,
                color: '#89BE96',
                facing: 'down',
                portrait: '🤖',
                dialogue: "Hi, I’m Rewaa! Come see how different light choices help our seedlings grow."
            }
        ];
    }

    render(ctx) {
        for (const npc of this.npcs) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(npc.x, npc.y);

            // NPC Shadow
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(0, 18, 18, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            // Body
            ctx.fillStyle = npc.color;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(0, -2, 17, 19, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
            ctx.stroke();

            // Head / Helmet
            ctx.fillStyle = '#E7E8E4';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(0, -18, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#263644';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
            ctx.stroke();

            // Visor / Face
            ctx.fillStyle = '#263644';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(0, -18, 11, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            // Name tag badge above head
            ctx.font = 'bold 12px "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#E7E8E4';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.fillText(npc.name, 0, -42);

            ctx.font = '10px "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#79AEC8';
            ctx.fillText(npc.role, 0, -30);

            ctx.restore();
        }
    }
}

window.PlayerClass = Player;

window.CrewClass = StationCrew;

    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT: THE LOST SIGNAL
 * Tactile 2D Interactive NASA Science Tasks & Minigames
 * Approach -> Interact -> Understand -> Solve -> See Station Impact
 */

class MinigameManager {
    constructor() {
        this.activeMinigame = null;
        this.overlayElement = null;
        this.activeDrillTimer = null;
    }

    init(container) {
        this.container = container;
    }

    startTask(taskId, onComplete) {
        this.onComplete = onComplete;
        this.currentTaskId = taskId;
        if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick();

        if (taskId === 'powerRouting') {
            this.openPowerRouting();
        } else if (taskId === 'co2Scrubber') {
            this.openCO2Scrubber();
        } else if (taskId === 'waterRecovery') {
            this.openWaterRecovery();
        } else if (taskId === 'greenhouseLight') {
            this.openGreenhouseLight();
        } else if (taskId === 'dsnAlign') {
            this.openDSNAlignment();
        } else if (taskId === 'stormShutter') {
            this.openStormShutter();
        } else if (taskId === 'evaMission') {
            this.openEVAMission();
        } else if (taskId === 'gravityLab') {
            this.openGravityLab();
        } else if (taskId === 'roverSurvey') {
            this.openRoverSurvey();
        } else if (taskId === 'thermalBalance') {
            this.openThermalBalance();
        }
    }

    close() {
        if (this.overlayElement && this.overlayElement.parentNode) {
            this.overlayElement.parentNode.removeChild(this.overlayElement);
        }
        this.activeMinigame = null;
        this.overlayElement = null;
        if (this.activeDrillTimer) { clearInterval(this.activeDrillTimer); this.activeDrillTimer = null; }
        if (window.Save) window.Save.schedule();
    }

    createModal(title, subtitle, icon) {
        this.close();
        const overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.className = 'task-overlay';
        overlay.innerHTML = `
            <div class="task-modal polished-task-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${title}" tabindex="-1">
                <div class="task-header">
                    <div class="task-title-group">
                        <span class="task-icon">${window.MoonshiftIcons?.forTask(title,icon) || icon}</span>
                        <div>
                            <h2>${title}</h2>
                            <p class="task-subtitle">${subtitle}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button class="task-close-btn" id="taskCloseBtn">&times;</button>
                </div>
                <div class="task-guide-sheet" id="taskGuideSheet" hidden></div>
                <div class="task-body" id="taskBody"></div>
                <div class="task-footer" id="taskFooter">
                    <span class="task-hint" id="taskHint"></span>
                    <div class="task-footer-actions">
                        <button class="task-secondary-btn" id="taskGuideBtn" aria-controls="taskGuideSheet" aria-expanded="false">✦ HOW IT WORKS</button>
                        <button class="task-action-btn" id="taskActionBtn">ENGAGE SYSTEM</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        this.overlayElement = overlay;
        this.activeMinigame = title;
        overlay.querySelector('.task-modal')?.focus();

        document.getElementById('taskCloseBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick();
            this.close();
        });
        document.getElementById('taskGuideBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick();
            this.toggleGuide();
        });
        const firstTimeKey = `moonshift-guide-${this.currentTaskId}`;
        try {
            if (!localStorage.getItem(firstTimeKey)) {
                this.toggleGuide(true);
                localStorage.setItem(firstTimeKey, '1');
            }
        } catch (e) {}

        return overlay;
    }

    toggleGuide(forceOpen = null) {
        const sheet = document.getElementById('taskGuideSheet');
        const btn = document.getElementById('taskGuideBtn');
        if (!sheet || !btn) return;
        const shouldOpen = forceOpen === null ? sheet.hasAttribute('hidden') : !!forceOpen;
        const modal=this.overlayElement?.querySelector('.task-modal');
        const action=document.getElementById('taskActionBtn');
        const body=document.getElementById('taskBody');
        if (!shouldOpen) {
            sheet.setAttribute('hidden', '');
            body?.removeAttribute('hidden');
            modal?.classList.remove('guide-mode');
            btn.textContent = '✦ HOW IT WORKS';
            btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
            if(action)action.hidden=false;
            return;
        }
        const guide = this.getGuideData(this.currentTaskId);
        if (!guide) return;
        sheet.innerHTML = this.renderGuideHTML(guide);
        sheet.removeAttribute('hidden');
        body?.setAttribute('hidden','');
        modal?.classList.add('guide-mode');
        btn.textContent = 'LET ME TRY →';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
        if(action)action.hidden=true;
    }

    renderGuideHTML(guide) {
        // A focused learning page, never shown on top of the task controls.
        // Game ranges are explicitly identified as fictional rather than NASA standards.
        const steps=(guide.steps||[]).slice(0,4).map((line,i)=>`<li><span class="edu-step-num">${i+1}</span><span>${line}</span></li>`).join('');
        return `<div class="edu-guide">
            <div class="edu-guide-hero"><div class="edu-hero-icon" aria-hidden="true">✦</div><div><span class="edu-kicker">UNDERSTAND THE MISSION</span><h3>${guide.title}</h3><p>${guide.summary}</p></div></div>
            <div class="edu-two-col">
                <section class="edu-tile"><span class="edu-tile-label">01 · THE IDEA</span><h4>What are we helping?</h4><p>${guide.what}</p></section>
                <section class="edu-tile edu-tile-accent"><span class="edu-tile-label">02 · A GOOD START</span><h4>Which numbers make sense?</h4><p>${guide.normal}</p></section>
            </div>
            <section class="edu-compare"><div><span class="edu-dot edu-dot-low"></span><strong>Too little</strong><p>${guide.low}</p></div><div><span class="edu-dot edu-dot-high"></span><strong>Too much</strong><p>${guide.high}</p></div></section>
            <section class="edu-bottom"><div class="edu-steps"><span class="edu-tile-label">03 · YOUR TURN</span><h4>Try it in a few steps</h4><ol>${steps}</ol></div><div class="edu-tip"><span class="edu-tile-label">FATEMA'S TIP</span><p>${guide.tip}</p><small>Numbers labeled “in this game” are learning examples, not official NASA operating limits.</small></div></section>
        </div>`;
    }

    getGuideData(taskId) {

                low: 'If air or heat is too low, the crew may not breathe well or stay comfortable. If radio is too low, you may lose contact.',
                high: 'If you give too much power to everything, the battery drains faster.',

                high: 'High heat feels warmer, but uses more electricity.',
                tip: 'Find a comfortable middle option, not the coldest or hottest one.',
                steps: ['Move the heater slider.', 'Read the temperature and power preview.', 'Compare comfort with electricity use.', 'Set the cabin heating.']
            },
        };
        return guides[taskId];
    }

    // 1. POWER DISTRIBUTION GRID
    openPowerRouting() {
        const modal = this.createModal(
            'POWER RECOVERY · CIRCUIT ENGINEERING',
            'Isolate the shorted bus breaker, reroute backup power, and balance station loads within solar capacity.',
            '⚡'
        );
        const body = modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
        const hint = modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
        const btn = modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');
        const sim = window.Sim;

        let faultIsolated = false;
        let backupRouted = false;
        let lifeKw = Math.max(10, Math.round(sim.powerAllocations.lifeSupport));
        let ghKw = Math.max(2, Math.round(sim.powerAllocations.greenhouse));
        let commKw = Math.max(2, Math.round(sim.powerAllocations.communications));

        const render = () => {
            const solar = sim.solarGeneration;
            const total = lifeKw + ghKw + commKw + sim.powerAllocations.auxiliary;
            const net = +(solar - total).toFixed(1);
            const overload = !faultIsolated || net < -6;
            const ready = faultIsolated && backupRouted && lifeKw >= 12 && commKw >= 4 && net >= -2;

            body.innerHTML = `<div class="ms-mg-shell">
              <div class="ms-mg-stage">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 640 210" role="img" aria-label="Power Distribution Bus">
                  <rect x="14" y="14" width="612" height="182" rx="14" fill="#0d1b28" stroke="rgba(121,174,200,0.3)"/>
                  <!-- Solar Bus -->
                  <rect x="30" y="34" width="120" height="54" rx="10" fill="#163044" stroke="#e9ad65" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="90" y="57" text-anchor="middle" fill="#f3d098" font-size="12" font-weight="700">SOLAR ARRAY</text>
                  <text x="90" y="75" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="13" font-weight="700">${solar.toFixed(1)} kW</text>
                  <!-- Faulted Bus Segment -->
                  <path d="M150 61 L270 61" stroke="${faultIsolated ? '#556d7a' : '#da7468'}" stroke-width="5" class="${faultIsolated ? 'ms-flow-line paused' : 'ms-flow-line'}"/>
                  <rect x="270" y="34" width="126" height="54" rx="10" fill="${faultIsolated ? '#162938' : '#3a1b1e'}" stroke="${faultIsolated ? '#89be96' : '#da7468'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="333" y="56" text-anchor="middle" fill="${faultIsolated ? '#89be96' : '#ff9f94'}" font-size="11" font-weight="700">${faultIsolated ? 'BREAKER ISOLATED' : '⚠ BUS SHORT FAULT'}</text>
                  <text x="333" y="74" text-anchor="middle" fill="#cfe4ec" font-size="11">${faultIsolated ? 'Safe to route' : 'Trip breaker!'}</text>
                  <!-- Backup Bus Route -->
                  <path d="M90 88 L90 148 L270 148" fill="none" stroke="${backupRouted ? '#79c9d8' : '#395264'}" stroke-width="5" class="${backupRouted ? 'ms-flow-line' : 'ms-flow-line paused'}"/>
                  <rect x="270" y="122" width="126" height="54" rx="10" fill="${backupRouted ? '#143344' : '#152533'}" stroke="${backupRouted ? '#79c9d8' : '#4a687a'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="333" y="145" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9fe2ee" font-size="11" font-weight="700">BACKUP BUS B</text>
                  <text x="333" y="163" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="11">${backupRouted ? 'ONLINE' : 'STANDBY'}</text>
                  <!-- Output to Station Systems -->
                  <path d="M396 148 L470 148 L470 61 L488 61" fill="none" stroke="${ready ? '#89be96' : '#e9ad65'}" stroke-width="4" class="${backupRouted ? 'ms-flow-line' : 'ms-flow-line paused'}"/>
                  <rect x="488" y="34" width="122" height="142" rx="10" fill="#13293a" stroke="${ready ? '#89be96' : '#79aec8'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="549" y="58" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">STATION BUS</text>
                  <text x="549" y="88" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="12">ECLSS: ${lifeKw} kW</text>
                  <text x="549" y="114" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="12">Garden: ${ghKw} kW</text>
                  <text x="549" y="140" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="12">Comms: ${commKw} kW</text>
                  <text x="549" y="164" text-anchor="middle" fill="${net >= 0 ? '#89be96' : '#e9ad65'}" font-size="12" font-weight="700">Net: ${net >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${net} kW</text>
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-btn-row">
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${faultIsolated ? 'active' : 'fault'}" id="msPwrTrip">${faultIsolated ? '✓ Fault Breaker Isolated' : '⚡ Trip Shorted Breaker'}</button>
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${backupRouted ? 'active' : ''}" id="msPwrRoute">${backupRouted ? '✓ Backup Bus Connected' : '🔌 Connect Backup Bus B'}</button>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-controls">
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>💨 Life Support (ECLSS)</span><b>${lifeKw} kW</b></div><input type="range" min="10" max="20" value="${lifeKw}" id="msPwrLife"></label>
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>🌱 Greenhouse Arrays</span><b>${ghKw} kW</b></div><input type="range" min="2" max="14" value="${ghKw}" id="msPwrGh"></label>
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>📡 Deep Space Comms</span><b>${commKw} kW</b></div><input type="range" min="2" max="10" value="${commKw}" id="msPwrComm"></label>
              </div>
              <details class="ms-nasa-note"><summary>✦ NASA Power &amp; Load Management Model</summary>Spacecraft electrical power systems isolate faulted buses before reclosing backup ties to protect solar arrays and batteries. See <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/reference/environmental-control-and-life-support-systems-eclss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA ECLSS Reference</a>. Simulation simplifies station kW loads deterministically.</details>
            </div>`;

            body.querySelector('#msPwrTrip').onclick = () => { faultIsolated = !faultIsolated; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msPwrRoute').onclick = () => {
                if (!faultIsolated) { window.Audio?.uiError(); hint.textContent = 'Trip the shorted breaker first before connecting Backup Bus B!'; return; }
                backupRouted = !backupRouted; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render();
            };
            body.querySelector('#msPwrLife').oninput = (e) => { lifeKw = +e.target.value; render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msPwrGh').oninput = (e) => { ghKw = +e.target.value; render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msPwrComm').oninput = (e) => { commKw = +e.target.value; render(); };

            btn.disabled = !ready;
            hint.textContent = overload ? 'Isolate the faulted bus and keep net load balanced.' : ready ? 'Bus stable! Engage circuit configuration.' : 'Connect Backup Bus B and allocate ≥12 kW to ECLSS & ≥4 kW to Comms.';
        };

        btn.textContent = 'LOCK POWER GRID';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            sim.setPowerAllocation('lifeSupport', lifeKw);
            sim.setPowerAllocation('greenhouse', ghKw);
            sim.setPowerAllocation('communications', commKw);
            window.Story.decisions.push({ type: 'power', allocations: { lifeSupport: lifeKw, greenhouse: ghKw, communications: commKw }, missionHour: sim.missionTimeHours });
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
            this.close();
            this.onComplete?.('powerRouting');
        };
        render();
    }

    openCO2Scrubber() {
        const modal = this.createModal(
            'OXYGEN PRODUCTION · NASA ECLSS',
            'Operate the Oxygen Generation Assembly (OGA) and CDRA CO₂ scrubber using real electrolysis stoichiometry.',
            '💨'
        );
        const body = modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
        const hint = modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
        const btn = modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');

        let waterValveOpen = false;
        let cdraBed = 'A';
        let electrolysisAmps = 28;

        const render = () => {
            const res = window.NasaSimModels.simulateECLSS({ crewCount: 4, electrolysisAmps, waterValveOpen, cdraBed });
            body.innerHTML = `<div class="ms-mg-shell">
              <div class="ms-mg-stage">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 640 200" role="img" aria-label="ECLSS Oxygen Generation Assembly">
                  <rect x="14" y="14" width="612" height="172" rx="14" fill="#0d1b28" stroke="rgba(121,174,200,0.3)"/>
                  <rect x="28" y="42" width="128" height="114" rx="12" fill="#132c3d" stroke="#79c9d8" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="92" y="72" text-anchor="middle" fill="#8ad3eb" font-size="11" font-weight="700">H₂O FEED</text>
                  <text x="92" y="102" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="13" font-weight="700">${res.h2oConsumedKgDay} kg/d</text>
                  <text x="92" y="128" text-anchor="middle" fill="${waterValveOpen ? '#89be96' : '#e9ad65'}" font-size="11">${waterValveOpen ? 'VALVE OPEN' : 'VALVE CLOSED'}</text>
                  <path d="M156 99 L250 99" stroke="#5bc0de" stroke-width="5" class="${waterValveOpen ? 'ms-flow-line' : 'ms-flow-line paused'}"/>
                  <rect x="250" y="34" width="150" height="130" rx="12" fill="#152b3b" stroke="${res.safePressure ? '#79aec8' : '#da7468'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="325" y="60" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">OGA ELECTROLYSIS</text>
                  <text x="325" y="88" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="16" font-weight="700">${electrolysisAmps} A</text>
                  <text x="325" y="112" text-anchor="middle" fill="#a9cad6" font-size="11">2H₂O → 2H₂ + O₂</text>
                  <text x="325" y="138" text-anchor="middle" fill="${res.safePressure ? '#89be96' : '#ff9f94'}" font-size="11">${res.safePressure ? 'Pressure Nominal' : '⚠ H₂ Vent High'}</text>
                  <path d="M400 99 L484 99" stroke="#89be96" stroke-width="5" class="${res.o2ProducedKgDay > 0 ? 'ms-flow-line' : 'ms-flow-line paused'}"/>
                  <rect x="484" y="42" width="128" height="114" rx="12" fill="#132c3d" stroke="${res.optimal ? '#89be96' : '#e9ad65'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="548" y="72" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">CABIN O₂</text>
                  <text x="548" y="102" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="15" font-weight="700">${res.o2ProducedKgDay} kg/d</text>
                  <text x="548" y="128" text-anchor="middle" fill="#a9cad6" font-size="11">Need: ${res.o2RequiredKgDay} kg/d</text>
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-kpis">
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${res.o2ProducedKgDay >= res.o2RequiredKgDay ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>O₂ Output</span><strong>${res.o2ProducedKgDay} / ${res.o2RequiredKgDay} kg/d</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi"><span>Power Draw</span><strong>${res.powerKw} kW</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${res.co2Scrubbed ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>CDRA Bed</span><strong>${cdraBed === 'B' ? 'BED B (FRESH)' : 'BED A (SATURATED)'}</strong></div>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-btn-row">
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${waterValveOpen ? 'active' : ''}" id="msO2Valve">${waterValveOpen ? '✓ Water Feed Open' : '🚰 Open H₂O Feed Valve'}</button>
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${cdraBed === 'B' ? 'active' : ''}" id="msO2Bed">${cdraBed === 'B' ? '✓ CDRA Bed B Active' : '🔄 Switch to Fresh Bed B'}</button>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-controls">
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>⚡ Electrolysis Current</span><b>${electrolysisAmps} A</b></div><input type="range" min="15" max="85" value="${electrolysisAmps}" id="msO2Amps"></label>
              </div>
              <details class="ms-nasa-note"><summary>✦ NASA ECLSS Stoichiometry</summary>Per <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/eclss-technical-brief-ochmo.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA ECLSS Technical Brief</a> and <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2016/01/174687main_eclss_facts.pdf?emrc=0ab55e" target="_blank" rel="noopener">ECLSS Fact Sheet</a>, ISS OGA produces 2.3–9 kg O₂/day via water electrolysis (1.125 kg H₂O per kg O₂) for ~0.89 kg O₂/crew-day.</details>
            </div>`;
            body.querySelector('#msO2Valve').onclick = () => { waterValveOpen = !waterValveOpen; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msO2Bed').onclick = () => { cdraBed = cdraBed === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A'; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msO2Amps').oninput = (e) => { electrolysisAmps = +e.target.value; render(); };
            btn.disabled = !res.optimal;
            hint.textContent = res.optimal ? 'ECLSS Oxygen & CO₂ loops nominal!' : 'Open water feed, switch to fresh CDRA Bed B, and set current between 42–78 A.';
        };
        btn.textContent = 'ENGAGE LIFE SUPPORT';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            window.Sim.scrubberOperational = true;
            window.Sim.co2Level = 0.04;
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
            this.close();
            this.onComplete?.('co2Scrubber');
        };
        render();
    }

    openWaterRecovery() {
        const modal = this.createModal(
            'WATER RECYCLING · NASA WRS DIAGNOSIS',
            'Inspect and repair the Urine Processor (UPA) & Water Processor (WPA) loop to reach 99.5%+ potable purity.',
            '💧'
        );
        const body = modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
        const hint = modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
        const btn = modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');

        let brineValveDiverted = false;
        let filterReplaced = false;
        let reactorTempC = 95;
        let tested = false;

        const render = () => {
            const res = window.NasaSimModels.simulateWaterRecovery({ brineValveDiverted, filterReplaced, reactorTempC, tested });
            const pipeColor = res.purityPct >= 99 ? '#79c9d8' : res.purityPct >= 94 ? '#e9ad65' : '#da7468';
            body.innerHTML = `<div class="ms-mg-shell">
              <div class="ms-mg-stage">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 640 185" role="img" aria-label="NASA Water Recovery System">
                  <rect x="14" y="14" width="612" height="157" rx="14" fill="#0d1b28" stroke="rgba(121,174,200,0.3)"/>
                  <rect x="28" y="42" width="122" height="96" rx="10" fill="#152b3b" stroke="${brineValveDiverted ? '#89be96' : '#e9ad65'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="89" y="72" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">1. UPA BRINE</text>
                  <text x="89" y="98" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="12">${brineValveDiverted ? 'Diverted ✓' : 'Bypass Open'}</text>
                  <path d="M150 90 L195 90" stroke="${pipeColor}" stroke-width="5" class="ms-flow-line"/>
                  <rect x="195" y="42" width="122" height="96" rx="10" fill="#152b3b" stroke="${filterReplaced ? '#89be96' : '#da7468'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="256" y="72" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">2. WPA FILTER</text>
                  <text x="256" y="98" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="12">${filterReplaced ? 'Fresh Bed ✓' : 'Clogged Bed'}</text>
                  <path d="M317 90 L362 90" stroke="${pipeColor}" stroke-width="5" class="ms-flow-line"/>
                  <rect x="362" y="42" width="122" height="96" rx="10" fill="#152b3b" stroke="${res.tempOk ? '#89be96' : '#e9ad65'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="423" y="72" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">3. CATALYTIC OX</text>
                  <text x="423" y="98" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="12">${reactorTempC}°C ${res.tempOk ? '✓' : ''}</text>
                  <path d="M484 90 L520 90" stroke="${pipeColor}" stroke-width="5" class="ms-flow-line"/>
                  <rect x="520" y="42" width="92" height="96" rx="10" fill="#152b3b" stroke="${tested ? '#89be96' : '#79aec8'}" stroke-width="2"/>
                  <text x="566" y="72" text-anchor="middle" fill="#9ed2e6" font-size="11" font-weight="700">POTABLE</text>
                  <text x="566" y="98" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="14" font-weight="700">${res.purityPct}%</text>
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-kpis">
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${res.purityPct >= 99.5 ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>Water Purity</span><strong>${res.purityPct}%</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi"><span>Recovery Rate</span><strong>${res.recoveryPct}%</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi"><span>Crew Need</span><strong>${res.crewHydrationLDay} L/day</strong></div>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-btn-row">
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${brineValveDiverted ? 'active' : ''}" id="msWrsBrine">${brineValveDiverted ? '✓ Brine Valve Diverted' : '🔧 Divert UPA Brine'}</button>
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${filterReplaced ? 'active' : ''}" id="msWrsFilter">${filterReplaced ? '✓ Filter Cartridge Swapped' : '🧪 Swap Saturated Filter'}</button>
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${tested ? 'active' : ''}" id="msWrsTest">${tested ? '✓ Conductivity Verified' : '🔬 Run Purity Test'}</button>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-controls">
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>🔥 Catalytic Reactor Temp (Target 125–140°C)</span><b>${reactorTempC}°C</b></div><input type="range" min="80" max="155" value="${reactorTempC}" id="msWrsTemp"></label>
              </div>
              <details class="ms-nasa-note"><summary>✦ NASA ISS Water Recovery Data</summary>Per <a href="https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20240005472/downloads/ICES%202024-317%20Status%20of%20ISS%20Water_Final.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA ICES 2024-317</a>, ISS UPA recovers 85–87% of pretreated urine water while WPA processes ~50.8 kg/day for crew hydration (~2.5 L/crew-day).</details>
            </div>`;
            body.querySelector('#msWrsBrine').onclick = () => { brineValveDiverted = !brineValveDiverted; tested = false; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msWrsFilter').onclick = () => { filterReplaced = !filterReplaced; tested = false; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msWrsTemp').oninput = (e) => { reactorTempC = +e.target.value; tested = false; render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msWrsTest').onclick = () => { tested = true; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            btn.disabled = !res.optimal;
            hint.textContent = res.optimal ? 'Potable water verified at 99.8% purity!' : 'Divert brine, swap filter, set reactor to 125–140°C, then run Purity Test.';
        };
        btn.textContent = 'RESTORE WATER LOOP';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            window.Sim.waterPurityPercent = 99.8;
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
            this.close();
            this.onComplete?.('waterRecovery');
        };
        render();
    }

    // 4. THE MOON GARDEN — a genuine electricity / water / food trade-off.
    openGreenhouseLight() {
        const modal = this.createModal(
            'THE MOON GARDEN · NASA VEGGIE & APH',
            'Balance Red/Blue LED photon flux (PPF), capillary irrigation, and CO₂ enrichment to maximize crop health.',
            '🌱'
        );
        const body = modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
        const hint = modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
        const btn = modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');

        let ppf = Math.round((window.Sim.powerAllocations.greenhouse || 7) / 0.022);
        let waterLPerHr = 2.4;
        let co2Enriched = true;

        const render = () => {
            const res = window.NasaSimModels.simulateGreenhouse({ ppf, waterLPerHr, co2Enriched });
            const leafColor = res.healthPct >= 80 ? '#79c98e' : res.healthPct >= 60 ? '#b7c96b' : '#d69767';
            body.innerHTML = `<div class="ms-mg-shell">
              <div class="ms-mg-stage">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 640 190" role="img" aria-label="NASA Veggie Plant Chamber">
                  <rect x="14" y="14" width="612" height="162" rx="14" fill="#0d1b28" stroke="rgba(121,174,200,0.3)"/>
                  <!-- LED Light Bank -->
                  <rect x="140" y="26" width="360" height="20" rx="6" fill="#231936" stroke="#c084fc"/>
                  <rect x="155" y="31" width="330" height="10" rx="4" fill="rgba(236, 72, 153, ${Math.min(0.95, ppf/600)})"/>
                  <!-- Light cone -->
                  <polygon points="160,46 480,46 530,152 110,152" fill="rgba(192, 132, 252, ${Math.min(0.22, ppf/2200)})"/>
                  <!-- Plants in PONDS root pillows -->
                  ${[210, 320, 430].map(cx => `
                    <rect x="${cx-34}" y="138" width="68" height="18" rx="5" fill="#294354" stroke="#79aec8"/>
                    <path d="M${cx} 138 Q${cx-18} ${105-res.healthPct*0.25} ${cx-28} ${102-res.healthPct*0.18} Q${cx-8} 118 ${cx} 138Z" fill="${leafColor}"/>
                    <path d="M${cx} 138 Q${cx+18} ${105-res.healthPct*0.25} ${cx+28} ${102-res.healthPct*0.18} Q${cx+8} 118 ${cx} 138Z" fill="${leafColor}"/>
