                    <path d="M${cx} 138 Q${cx} ${90-res.healthPct*0.32} ${cx} ${88-res.healthPct*0.28}" stroke="${leafColor}" stroke-width="4" fill="none"/>
                  `).join('')}
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-kpis">
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${res.healthPct >= 82 ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>Crop Health</span><strong>${res.healthPct}%</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${res.lightKw <= 11.5 ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>LED Power</span><strong>${res.lightKw} kW</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi"><span>Biomass Yield</span><strong>${res.biomassRate} g/day</strong></div>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-controls">
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>💡 LED PPF (400–700 nm)</span><b>${ppf} µmol/m²/s</b></div><input type="range" min="100" max="600" step="10" value="${ppf}" id="msGhPpf"></label>
                <label class="ms-mg-ctrl"><div class="ms-mg-ctrl-head"><span>💧 PONDS Irrigation</span><b>${waterLPerHr.toFixed(1)} L/h</b></div><input type="range" min="0.8" max="4.5" step="0.2" value="${waterLPerHr}" id="msGhWater"></label>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-btn-row">
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${co2Enriched ? 'active' : ''}" id="msGhCo2">${co2Enriched ? '✓ CO₂ Enrichment Active (1200 ppm)' : '🌫️ Enable CO₂ Enrichment'}</button>
              </div>
              <details class="ms-nasa-note"><summary>✦ NASA Veggie &amp; APH Reference</summary>Per <a href="https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20210022119/downloads/Stars%20APH%20PONDS%20Talk%202021.pdf?attachment=true" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA APH / PONDS</a> and <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/biological-physical/focus-areas/plant-biology/hardware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA Plant Biology</a>, chambers use Red (630–660 nm) + Blue (400–500 nm) LEDs with controlled PPF and capillary root pillows.</details>
            </div>`;
            body.querySelector('#msGhPpf').oninput = (e) => { ppf = +e.target.value; render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msGhWater').oninput = (e) => { waterLPerHr = +e.target.value; render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msGhCo2').onclick = () => { co2Enriched = !co2Enriched; window.Audio?.uiConfirm(); render(); };
            btn.disabled = !res.optimal;
            hint.textContent = res.optimal ? 'Optimal photosynthesis & water balance!' : 'Target 250–450 µmol/m²/s PPF and 2.0–3.2 L/h irrigation within 11.5 kW.';
        };
        btn.textContent = 'SAVE GARDEN PLAN';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            const res = window.NasaSimModels.simulateGreenhouse({ ppf, waterLPerHr, co2Enriched });
            window.Sim.setPowerAllocation('greenhouse', Math.max(2, Math.round(res.lightKw)));
            window.Sim.greenhouseCropHealth = Math.min(100, Math.max(40, res.healthPct));
            window.Story.decisions.push({ type: 'gardenPlan', powerKW: res.lightKw, waterLitresPerHour: waterLPerHr, missionHour: window.Sim.missionTimeHours });
            window.Save?.schedule();
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
            this.close();
            this.onComplete?.('greenhouseLight');
        };
        render();
    }

    openDSNAlignment() {
        this.createModal(
            'DEEP SPACE NETWORK DISH ALIGNMENT',
            `Target: Earth Ground Station (Goldstone/Madrid). One-way radio transit delay: ${window.Sim.signalDelaySeconds.toFixed(3)}s.`,
            '📡'
        );
        const body = document.getElementById('taskBody');
        const hint = document.getElementById('taskHint');
        const btn = document.getElementById('taskActionBtn');
        hint.textContent = 'Rotate Azimuth and Elevation to center the antenna beam on Earth.';

        body.innerHTML = `
            <div class="radar-scope-wrapper">
                <div class="radar-scope" id="radarScope">
                    <div class="radar-crosshair"></div>
                    <div class="radar-target" id="earthTarget">🌍</div>
                    <div class="radar-beam" id="antennaBeam"></div>
                </div>
                <div class="radar-dials">
                    <div class="slider-row">
                        <div class="slider-label"><span>Dish Azimuth (Degrees)</span><span id="azVal">180°</span></div>
                        <input type="range" min="150" max="210" value="180" id="dishAz" class="nasa-slider">
                    </div>
                    <div class="slider-row">
                        <div class="slider-label"><span>Dish Elevation (Degrees)</span><span id="elVal">40°</span></div>
                        <input type="range" min="20" max="60" value="40" id="dishEl" class="nasa-slider">
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="dsn-stats-box">
                <div>Light Travel Time: <strong>1.282 seconds</strong></div>
                <div>Carrier SNR: <strong id="carrierSNR">8.2 dB</strong></div>
                <div>Link Handshake: <strong id="handshakeStatus" style="color:#E9AD65;">SEARCHING CARRIER...</strong></div>
            </div>
        `;

        const dAz = document.getElementById('dishAz');
        const dEl = document.getElementById('dishEl');
        const beam = document.getElementById('antennaBeam');
        const target = document.getElementById('earthTarget');

        // Earth target is at Az: 184°, El: 43°
        target.style.left = '52%';
        target.style.top = '44%';

        const updateBeam = () => {
            const az = parseInt(dAz.value);
            const el = parseInt(dEl.value);
            document.getElementById('azVal').textContent = `${az}°`;
            document.getElementById('elVal').textContent = `${el}°`;

            const posX = 52 + (az - 184) * 1.5;
            const posY = 44 - (el - 43) * 1.5;
            beam.style.left = `${posX}%`;
            beam.style.top = `${posY}%`;

            const dist = Math.hypot(posX - 52, posY - 44);
            if (dist < 4) {
                document.getElementById('carrierSNR').textContent = '26.4 dB (EXCELLENT)';
                document.getElementById('handshakeStatus').textContent = 'LOCKED TO GOLDSTONE DSN ✓';
                document.getElementById('handshakeStatus').style.color = '#89BE96';
                hint.textContent = 'Signal locked! Radio echo confirms Earth telemetry link established. Keep the communications circuit at 4 kW or more.';
                btn.style.background = '#89BE96';
                btn.textContent = 'TRANSMIT TELEMETRY PACKET';
            } else {
                document.getElementById('carrierSNR').textContent = '6.1 dB';
                document.getElementById('handshakeStatus').textContent = 'SEARCHING CARRIER...';
                document.getElementById('handshakeStatus').style.color = '#E9AD65';
                hint.textContent = 'Adjust azimuth and elevation until the green beam centers directly on Earth.';
                btn.style.background = '#263644';
            }
        };

        dAz.oninput = () => { if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick(); updateBeam(); };
        dEl.oninput = () => { if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick(); updateBeam(); };
        updateBeam();

        btn.onclick = () => {
            const az = parseInt(dAz.value);
            const el = parseInt(dEl.value);
            if (Math.hypot((52 + (az - 184) * 1.5) - 52, (44 - (el - 43) * 1.5) - 44) < 4) {
                // An aligned dish still needs electricity. Recheck even if the player
                // has not waited for the next simulation tick after changing a plan.
                const sim=window.Sim;
                const demand=Object.values(sim.powerAllocations).reduce((a,b)=>a+Number(b),0);
                const shortfall=sim.batteryCharge<=0.001 && sim.solarGeneration<demand;
                const commsAvailable=shortfall
                    ? Math.max(0,Math.min(sim.powerAllocations.communications, sim.solarGeneration-sim.powerAllocations.lifeSupport))
                    : sim.powerAllocations.communications;
                if(commsAvailable<4){
                    hint.innerHTML='📡 Signal locked. The radio needs at least <strong>4 kW</strong> of delivered power. <button type="button" class="v31-inline-repair" id="v31RepairFromDish">⚡ Restore power</button>'; hint.querySelector('#v31RepairFromDish')?.addEventListener('click',()=>window.MoonshiftEnergy?.open());
                    window.MissionUI?.notify('🔋 Power shortage: rebalance electricity before sending the signal.');
                    return;
                }
                if (window.Audio) window.Audio.taskSuccess();
                window.Sim.antennaAligned = true;
                window.Sim.dsnLinkQuality = 99;
                this.close();
                if (this.onComplete) this.onComplete('dsnAlign');
            }
        };
    }

    // 6. RADIATION REGOLITH STORM SHUTTERS
    openStormShutter() {
        const modal = this.createModal(
            'RADIATION SHELTER · NASA DONKI SPACE WEATHER',
            'Lock all 3 radiation storm shutters and select a crew protection strategy for the simulated SEP event.',
            '🛡️'
        );
        const body = modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
        const hint = modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
        const btn = modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');

        let locks = [false, false, false];
        let mode = 'powered';

        const render = () => {
            const locksSecured = locks.filter(Boolean).length;
            const res = window.NasaSimModels.simulateSpaceWeather({ locksSecured, mode });
            body.innerHTML = `<div class="ms-mg-shell">
              <div class="ms-mg-stage">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 640 175" role="img" aria-label="Radiation Shelter Shutters">
                  <rect x="14" y="14" width="612" height="147" rx="14" fill="#0d1b28" stroke="rgba(121,174,200,0.3)"/>
                  <text x="32" y="38" fill="#e9ad65" font-size="11" font-weight="700">SIMULATED DONKI ALERT · CLASS ${res.flareClass} FLARE · PROTON FLUX ${res.protonFluxPfu} pfu (&gt;10 MeV)</text>
                  ${[0,1,2].map(idx => {
                      const x = 42 + idx * 195;
                      const ok = locks[idx];
                      return `<g style="cursor:pointer" data-lock="${idx}">
                        <rect x="${x}" y="54" width="166" height="90" rx="10" fill="${ok ? '#15342b' : '#351c20'}" stroke="${ok ? '#89be96' : '#da7468'}" stroke-width="2.5"/>
                        <text x="${x+83}" y="88" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="13" font-weight="700">SHUTTER ${idx+1}</text>
                        <text x="${x+83}" y="114" text-anchor="middle" fill="${ok ? '#89be96' : '#ff9f94'}" font-size="12" font-weight="700">${ok ? '🔒 LOCKED' : '🔓 CLICK TO LOCK'}</text>
                      </g>`;
                  }).join('')}
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-kpis">
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${res.ready ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>Shelter Integrity</span><strong>${res.shieldPct}%</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi"><span>Energy Cost</span><strong>${res.powerCostKwh} kWh</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi"><span>Time Cost</span><strong>${res.timeCostHr} hr</strong></div>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-btn-row">
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${mode==='powered' ? 'active' : ''}" id="msRadPow">⚡ Active Water-Wall (8 kWh, Fast)</button>
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn ${mode==='manual' ? 'active' : ''}" id="msRadMan">🔧 Manual Bulkhead (0 kWh, 1 hr)</button>
              </div>
              <details class="ms-nasa-note"><summary>✦ NASA DONKI Space Weather Context</summary>Grounded in <a href="https://ccmc.gsfc.nasa.gov/tools/DONKI/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA CCMC DONKI</a> SEP operational threshold (&gt;10 pfu at &gt;10 MeV). Solar flare observations trigger mission posture alerts and do not directly establish local astronaut dose without a radiation transport model.</details>
            </div>`;
            body.querySelectorAll('[data-lock]').forEach(g => {
                g.onclick = () => {
                    const idx = +g.getAttribute('data-lock');
                    locks[idx] = !locks[idx];
                    window.Audio?.uiConfirm();
                    render();
                };
            });
            body.querySelector('#msRadPow').onclick = () => { mode = 'powered'; window.Audio?.uiClick(); render(); };
            body.querySelector('#msRadMan').onclick = () => { mode = 'manual'; window.Audio?.uiClick(); render(); };
            btn.disabled = !res.ready;
            hint.textContent = res.ready ? 'All 3 shutters locked. Confirm your protection plan.' : 'Click all 3 shutter panels above to lock them.';
        };
        btn.textContent = 'ENGAGE SHELTER SHIELDING';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            if (mode === 'powered') {
                window.Sim.batteryKWh = Math.max(0, window.Sim.batteryKWh - 8);
            } else {
                window.Sim.update(3600);
            }
            window.Sim.shelterSealed = true;
            window.Story.decisions.push({ type: 'shelterPlan', mode, missionHour: window.Sim.missionTimeHours });
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
            this.close();
            this.onComplete?.('stormShutter');
        };
        render();
    }

    openEVAMission() {
        this.createModal(
            'LUNAR SURFACE EVA — ICE CORE DRILLING',
            'Depressurize airlock, step onto Shackleton Crater rim, and extract volatile water ice cores.',
            '🧑‍🚀'
        );
        const body = document.getElementById('taskBody');
        const hint = document.getElementById('taskHint');
        const btn = document.getElementById('taskActionBtn');
        hint.textContent = 'Click to activate core drill when indicator is in the green Permanently Shadowed Region.';

        body.innerHTML = `
            <div class="drill-gauge-wrapper">
                <div class="drill-meter-bar">
                    <div class="meter-safe-zone"></div>
                    <div class="meter-needle" id="meterNeedle"></div>
                </div>
                <div class="lunar-sample-preview">
                    <div class="sample-icon">🪨</div>
                    <div>
                        Sample Depth: <strong id="depthVal">0.0 meters</strong><br>
                        H2O Ice Content: <strong id="iceVal" style="color:#79C9D8;">-- %</strong>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;

        let needlePos = 0;
        let dir = 1;
        let drillInterval = setInterval(() => {
            needlePos += dir * 2.5;
            if (needlePos >= 100) dir = -1;
            if (needlePos <= 0) dir = 1;
            const needle = document.getElementById('meterNeedle');
            if (needle) needle.style.left = `${needlePos}%`;
        }, 30);

        this.activeDrillTimer = drillInterval;
        let drilled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'ACTIVATE CORE DRILL';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            clearInterval(drillInterval);
            // Safe green zone is 40% to 65%
            if (needlePos >= 38 && needlePos <= 68) {
                if (window.Audio) window.Audio.taskSuccess();
                drilled = true;
                document.getElementById('depthVal').textContent = '2.4 meters (Subsurface)';
                document.getElementById('iceVal').textContent = '5.8% (FICTIONAL TRAINING ESTIMATE)';
                hint.textContent = 'Great drill! This fictional sample illustrates what researchers hope to investigate. No actual water-ice measurement was made.';
                btn.style.background = '#89BE96';
                btn.textContent = 'STOW SAMPLE IN AIRLOCK';
                btn.onclick = () => {
                    this.close();
                    if (this.onComplete) this.onComplete('evaMission');
                };
            } else {
                if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiError();
                hint.textContent = 'Drill struck dense basalt! Try again when needle enters the green zone.';
                btn.textContent = 'RESET DRILL RPM';
                btn.onclick = () => this.openEVAMission();
            }
        };
    }

    // Optional activity: the same object falls differently under Earth and Moon gravity.
    openGravityLab() {
        this.createModal('THE GRAVITY LAB', 'Same height. Same object. Different gravity. What do you think will happen?', '🌙');
        const body=document.getElementById('taskBody'),hint=document.getElementById('taskHint'),btn=document.getElementById('taskActionBtn');
        body.innerHTML=`<div class="experiment-lead">🔬 <strong>Try a real physics idea:</strong> Gravity on the Moon is only about one-sixth of Earth’s gravity.</div>
        <div class="gravity-lanes"><div class="gravity-lane"><span class="lane-name">🌍 EARTH <small>9.81 m/s²</small></span><div class="fall-track"><span class="fall-ball" id="earthBall">●</span><span class="fall-line"></span></div><span class="fall-time" id="earthTime">Ready to drop</span></div>
        <div class="gravity-lane"><span class="lane-name">🌙 MOON <small>1.62 m/s²</small></span><div class="fall-track"><span class="fall-ball" id="moonBall">●</span><span class="fall-line"></span></div><span class="fall-time" id="moonTime">Ready to drop</span></div></div>
        <div class="science-note">We drop identical objects from a height of 1.8 m, with no air resistance. The animation is slowed down so you can see the difference. This is a simplified physics experiment, not a NASA measurement.</div>`;
        let finished=false, animating=false,raf=0;
        const h=1.8, earth=Math.sqrt(2*h/9.81), moon=Math.sqrt(2*h/1.62);
        hint.textContent='Predict which object will land first, then launch the experiment.';
        btn.textContent='DROP BOTH OBJECTS';
        btn.onclick=()=>{
            if(finished){this.close();this.onComplete?.('gravityLab');return;}
            if(animating)return;
            animating=true;btn.disabled=true;
            const start=performance.now(),slow=2.2;
            const e=body.querySelector('#earthBall'),m=body.querySelector('#moonBall');
            const step=(t)=>{
                if(!this.overlayElement?.isConnected)return;
                const elapsed=(t-start)/1000/slow;
                e.style.top=`${Math.min(1,9.81*elapsed*elapsed/(2*h))*100}%`;
                m.style.top=`${Math.min(1,1.62*elapsed*elapsed/(2*h))*100}%`;
                if(elapsed<moon){raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);} else {
                    finished=true;animating=false;btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='SAVE MY DISCOVERY';
                    body.querySelector('#earthTime').textContent=`Landed after ${earth.toFixed(2)} s`;
                    body.querySelector('#moonTime').textContent=`Landed after ${moon.toFixed(2)} s`;
                    hint.textContent='✨ Same object, slower fall on the Moon! Gravity affects motion. Try predicting the time next time.';
                    window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
                }
            };
            raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
        };
    }

    // Optional mission: choose among real trade-offs in a fictional rover-energy budget.
    openRoverSurvey() {
        const modal = this.createModal(
            'MARS ROVER · TERRAIN NAVIGATION PUZZLE',
            'Plot a safe rover path to collect ≥3 science samples without exceeding the 320 Wh battery budget.',
            '🛞'
        );
        const body = modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
        const hint = modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
        const btn = modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');

        const nodes = {
            base: { x: 65, y: 155, label: 'LANDER', samples: 0 },
            ridge: { x: 185, y: 65, label: 'RIDGE', samples: 1 },
            sand: { x: 245, y: 160, label: 'DUNES', samples: 1 },
            delta: { x: 375, y: 65, label: 'DELTA', samples: 2 },
            rim: { x: 425, y: 155, label: 'CRATER', samples: 1 },
            cache: { x: 565, y: 105, label: 'CACHE', samples: 1 }
        };
        const edges = {
            'base-ridge': { dist: 420, slope: 11, terrain: 'basalt' },
            'base-sand': { dist: 340, slope: 22, terrain: 'sand' },
            'ridge-delta': { dist: 440, slope: 9, terrain: 'basalt' },
            'ridge-sand': { dist: 260, slope: 15, terrain: 'rocky' },
            'sand-rim': { dist: 380, slope: 21, terrain: 'sand' },
            'delta-rim': { dist: 270, slope: 14, terrain: 'rocky' },
            'delta-cache': { dist: 430, slope: 10, terrain: 'basalt' },
            'rim-cache': { dist: 360, slope: 16, terrain: 'rocky' }
        };
        const getEdge = (a, b) => edges[`${a}-${b}`] || edges[`${b}-${a}`] || null;

        let path = ['base'];
        const budgetWh = 320;

        const render = () => {
            let totalWh = 0, totalSamples = 0, hazards = 0;
            const visited = new Set(path);
            visited.forEach(id => { totalSamples += nodes[id].samples; });
            for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i++) {
                const e = getEdge(path[i], path[i+1]);
                if (e) {
                    const leg = window.NasaSimModels.simulateRoverLeg(e.dist, e.slope, e.terrain);
                    totalWh += leg.energyWh;
                    if (leg.hazard) hazards++;
                }
            }
            const cur = path[path.length - 1];
            const remWh = budgetWh - totalWh;
            const reachedEnd = cur === 'cache';
            const success = reachedEnd && remWh >= 0 && totalSamples >= 3;

            body.innerHTML = `<div class="ms-mg-shell">
              <div class="ms-mg-stage">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 640 205" role="img" aria-label="Mars Terrain Route Map">
                  <rect x="14" y="14" width="612" height="177" rx="14" fill="#181210" stroke="rgba(212,155,120,0.35)"/>
                  ${Object.entries(edges).map(([k, e]) => {
                      const [a, b] = k.split('-');
                      const na = nodes[a], nb = nodes[b];
                      const active = path.some((id, idx) => idx < path.length - 1 && ((path[idx]===a && path[idx+1]===b)||(path[idx]===b && path[idx+1]===a)));
                      const leg = window.NasaSimModels.simulateRoverLeg(e.dist, e.slope, e.terrain);
                      const mx = (na.x+nb.x)/2, my = (na.y+nb.y)/2;
                      return `<line x1="${na.x}" y1="${na.y}" x2="${nb.x}" y2="${nb.y}" stroke="${active ? '#89be96' : leg.hazard ? '#da7468' : '#586d78'}" stroke-width="${active ? 5 : 2.5}" stroke-dasharray="${leg.hazard && !active ? '5 4' : 'none'}"/>
                      <rect x="${mx-24}" y="${my-10}" width="48" height="18" rx="5" fill="#1b2630" stroke="${leg.hazard ? '#da7468' : '#4a687a'}"/>
                      <text x="${mx}" y="${my+3}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${leg.hazard ? '#ff9f94' : '#cfe4ec'}" font-size="9.5" font-weight="700">${e.slope}° · ${leg.energyWh}Wh</text>`;
                  }).join('')}
                  ${Object.entries(nodes).map(([id, n]) => {
                      const inPath = visited.has(id);
                      const isCur = cur === id;
                      const canStep = !!getEdge(cur, id) && !inPath;
                      return `<g style="cursor:${canStep?'pointer':'default'}" data-wp="${id}">
                        <circle cx="${n.x}" cy="${n.y}" r="${isCur?21:17}" fill="${inPath ? '#1d4d3e' : canStep ? '#223f54' : '#1b2630'}" stroke="${isCur ? '#f1d4a0' : inPath ? '#89be96' : canStep ? '#79c9d8' : '#586d78'}" stroke-width="2.5"/>
                        <text x="${n.x}" y="${n.y-1}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6f2f6" font-size="9" font-weight="700">${n.label}</text>
                        <text x="${n.x}" y="${n.y+10}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#8ad3eb" font-size="8.5">${n.samples > 0 ? '+'+n.samples+' sci' : 'START'}</text>
                      </g>`;
                  }).join('')}
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-kpis">
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${remWh >= 0 ? 'ok' : 'bad'}"><span>Battery Left</span><strong>${remWh} / ${budgetWh} Wh</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${totalSamples >= 3 ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>Science Samples</span><strong>${totalSamples} / 3+</strong></div>
                <div class="ms-mg-kpi ${hazards === 0 ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"><span>Slope Hazards</span><strong>${hazards}</strong></div>
              </div>
              <div class="ms-mg-btn-row">
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn" id="msRovUndo">↩ Step Back</button>
                <button type="button" class="ms-tactile-btn" id="msRovReset">🔄 Reset Route</button>
              </div>
              <details class="ms-nasa-note"><summary>✦ NASA Mars Rover &amp; Trek Context</summary>Inspired by <a href="https://trek.nasa.gov/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">NASA Mars Trek</a> and <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/mission/mars-2020-perseverance/science-instruments/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Perseverance Rover</a> terrain planning: slopes &gt;18° in loose sand cause wheel slip and steep energy penalties.</details>
            </div>`;
            body.querySelectorAll('[data-wp]').forEach(g => {
                g.onclick = () => {
                    const id = g.getAttribute('data-wp');
                    if (getEdge(cur, id) && !visited.has(id)) {
                        path.push(id);
                        window.Audio?.uiConfirm();
                        render();
                    }
                };
            });
            body.querySelector('#msRovUndo').onclick = () => { if (path.length > 1) { path.pop(); window.Audio?.uiClick(); render(); } };
            body.querySelector('#msRovReset').onclick = () => { path = ['base']; window.Audio?.uiClick(); render(); };
            btn.disabled = !success;
            hint.textContent = success ? 'Safe high-science route plotted!' : remWh < 0 ? 'Battery depleted! Step back and avoid steep sandy dunes.' : 'Click connected waypoints to reach CACHE with ≥3 science samples and ≥0 Wh left.';
        };
        btn.textContent = 'DISPATCH ROVER ROUTE';
        btn.onclick = () => {
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
            this.close();
            this.onComplete?.('roverSurvey');
        };
        render();
    }

    openThermalBalance() {
        this.createModal('KEEP THE CREW COMFORTABLE', 'Heating the habitat uses electricity. Can you pick a sensible setting?', '🌡️');
        const body=document.getElementById('taskBody'),hint=document.getElementById('taskHint'),btn=document.getElementById('taskActionBtn');
        const sim=window.Sim;
        body.innerHTML=`<div class="experiment-lead">🏠 <strong>Inside the habitat:</strong> <span id="cabinReadout">${sim.temperatureC.toFixed(1)}°C</span>. The Moon has almost no atmosphere, so insulation and heating matter.</div><div class="temperature-readout"><span>❄️ COOLER</span><div class="thermo-gradient"><span id="thermalNeedle"></span></div><span>☀️ WARMER</span></div><div class="slider-row"><div class="slider-label"><label for="thermoPower">Cabin heater</label><strong id="thermoPowerLabel">${sim.powerAllocations.thermalHeaters} kW</strong></div><input id="thermoPower" type="range" min="3" max="10" step="1" value="${sim.powerAllocations.thermalHeaters}" class="nasa-slider"></div><div class="choice-intro" id="thermalPreview"></div><p class="science-note">Simplified fictional LUNA-7 heating model. Real spacecraft temperature depends on insulation, equipment heat, sunlight and radiator design.</p>`;
        const input=body.querySelector('#thermoPower');
        const update=()=>{
            const kw=Number(input.value),t=19.7+(kw-3)*.48;
            body.querySelector('#thermoPowerLabel').textContent=`${kw} kW`;
            body.querySelector('#thermalNeedle').style.left=`${(kw-3)/7*100}%`;
            body.querySelector('#thermalPreview').innerHTML=`<strong>${kw<=4?'Energy saver':kw<=7?'Balanced comfort':'Extra warmth'}</strong><p>Approximate cabin target: ${t.toFixed(1)}°C. The heater needs ${kw} kWh per simulated hour. ${kw<5?'Cooler, but leaves more battery power for other jobs.':kw>7?'Warmer, but takes more electricity from the shared supply.':'A middle ground between comfort and electricity.'}</p>`;
            hint.textContent='Compare the temperature with the power cost, then decide.';
        };
        input.oninput=update;update();btn.textContent='SET CABIN HEATING';
        btn.onclick=()=>{
            const kw=Number(input.value);
            sim.setPowerAllocation('thermalHeaters',kw);
            sim.temperatureC=19.7+(kw-3)*.48;
            sim.updateAlerts();
            window.Story.decisions.push({type:'thermal',kilowatts:kw,cabinTempC:sim.temperatureC});
            window.MissionUI?.notify(`🌡️ Heater set to ${kw} kW · Cabin target ${sim.temperatureC.toFixed(1)}°C`);
            window.Audio?.taskSuccess();this.close();this.onComplete?.('thermalBalance');window.Save?.schedule();
        };
    }

}

window.Minigames = new MinigameManager();

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT: THE LOST SIGNAL
 * Complete 6-Act Branching Narrative & Mission Progression Engine
 * Designed for Independent educational prototype · NASA Space Apps Challenge 2026.
 */

class StoryEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.currentAct = 1;
        this.activeObjective = 'Report to Central Habitat and meet the LUNA-7 crew.';
        this.completedTasks = new Set();
        this.dialogueQueue = [];
        this.isDialogueActive = false;
        this.missionScore = 0;
        this.decisions = [];
        this.endingShown = false;
    }

    reset() {
        this.currentAct = 1;
        this.clearDialogueHandlers();
        this.completedTasks.clear();
        window.Voice?.stop();
        this.dialogueQueue = [];
        this.isDialogueActive = false;
        this.endingShown = false;
        this.decisions = [];
        document.getElementById('dialogueBox')?.remove();
    }

    start() {
        this.loadAct(1);
    }

    loadAct(actNum) {
        this.currentAct = actNum;
        if (window.Save) window.Save.schedule();

        if (actNum === 1) {
            // ACT 1: WELCOME TO LUNA-7
            this.activeObjective = 'Find NOVA’s glowing globe in the Central Habitat.';
            this.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "You made it! I'm Fatema, your new robot buddy. I've practiced my welcome speech 47 times!" },
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "Our pretend Moon base is called LUNA-7. Follow the corridor to the big glowing globe!" },
                { speaker: 'Eyad', avatar: '🌐', text: "Eyad here. The station is ready. Walk with WASD or the arrow keys, then press E near something glowing." }
            ]);
        } 
        else if (actNum === 2) {
            // ACT 2: DISCOVERING LIFE ON THE MOON
            this.activeObjective = 'Visit the Moon Garden. Choose how much light and water our plants should receive.';
            this.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: 'Rawan', avatar: '🔬', text: "Hello Junior! Welcome to LUNA-7. I lead our astrobiology and life support research." },
                { speaker: 'Rawan', avatar: '🔬', text: "Our garden needs water and electric lights, just like the air machines need power!" },
                { speaker: 'Rewaa', avatar: '🌱', text: "I care for the seedlings! Let's test a gentle mix of light and water, and see what grows." },
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "Let's give the Moon garden a special light recipe. Come explore the greenhouse!" }
            ]);
        }
        else if (actNum === 3) {
            // ACT 3: THE SOLAR ARRAY ANOMALY
            this.activeObjective = 'Visit the Power Room. Balance electricity and battery reserves.';
            window.Sim.solarEventMultiplier = 0.54; // LUNA-7 scenario: terrain-shadow reduction persists through simulation ticks
            this.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: 'Abdelrahman', avatar: '🔧', text: "Junior, come in! A crater rim has shadowed part of our pretend solar array. We have less power coming in!" },
                { speaker: 'Abdelrahman', avatar: '🔧', text: "The air machines, garden and radio all need electricity. Can you help choose what gets power?" },
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "We'll need a clever plan. Find the Power Room to the southwest!" }
            ]);
        }
        else if (actNum === 4) {
            // ACT 4: CASCADING LIFE SUPPORT EMERGENCY
            this.activeObjective = 'Visit Life Support: repair the air cleaner and the water recycler.';
            window.Sim.scrubberOperational = false;
            window.Sim.co2Level = 880;
            this.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: 'Eyad', avatar: '🌐', text: "WARNING: Atmospheric CO2 rising past 850 ppm. Amine bed saturation detected in Life Support." },
                { speaker: 'Rawan', avatar: '🔬', text: "Junior! Carbon dioxide buildup causes fatigue. We need you to manually cycle the vacuum desorber!" },
                { speaker: 'Rewaa', avatar: '🌱', text: "I can see the air warning in our greenhouse too. Let's help the crew fix the cleaner." },
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "Follow me to Life Support (south corridor)! We've got to flush that CO2 scrubber!" }
            ]);
        }
        else if (actNum === 5) {
            // ACT 5: DONKI SPACE WEATHER STORM WARNING
            this.activeObjective = 'Space-weather training alert! Prepare our shelter to keep the crew safe.';
            // NASA SDO observed an X8.7 flare on 2024-05-14.
            // This is a hypothetical preparedness drill, NOT a lunar dose prediction.
            const event=window.NasaEvidence?.solarFlare2024;
            window.Sim.solarStormActive = !!event?.flareClass?.startsWith('X');
            window.Sim.spaceWeatherTrainingReference = event?.observationDate || null;
            if (window.Audio) window.Audio.startAlarm();
            this.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: 'Eyad', avatar: '🌐', text: "TRAINING ALERT! NASA observed an X8.7 solar flare on May 14, 2024. Imagine we receive a space-weather warning: how should our crew prepare?" },
                { speaker: 'Abdelrahman', avatar: '🔧', text: "A strong space-weather hazard can change plans. We're practicing a shelter drill—not using real Moon radiation readings." },
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "Let's prepare the shelter together! Follow the path north. Our equipment needs power, too." }
            ]);
        }
        else if (actNum === 6) {
            // ACT 6: RESTORING THE LOST SIGNAL & RESOLUTION
            this.activeObjective = 'Find Communications. Point our radio toward Earth to restore the signal.';
            if (window.Audio) window.Audio.stopAlarm();
            window.Sim.antennaAligned = false;
            window.Sim.dsnLinkQuality = 12;
            this.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: 'Rawan', avatar: '🔬', text: "Good work! We prepared the protected area in time for our training scenario." },
                { speaker: 'Abdelrahman', avatar: '🔧', text: "One last problem: the Earth radio link is offline. The crew needs your help!" },
                { speaker: 'Rewaa', avatar: '🌱', text: "Our garden is safe again! I'll look after it while you bring that signal home." },
                { speaker: 'Fatema', avatar: '🤖', text: "To the Communications Room (northeast)! Let's realign the dish, compensate for the 1.28s light delay, and restore The Lost Signal!" }
            ]);
        }
    }

    onTaskCompleted(taskId) {
        // A player may experiment with any console early. Practicing ahead of the story
        // must never make a later required chapter impossible to finish.
        const chapterForTask={stationTelemetry:1,greenhouseLight:2,powerRouting:3,co2Scrubber:4,waterRecovery:4,stormShutter:5,dsnAlign:6};
        if(chapterForTask[taskId] && chapterForTask[taskId] > this.currentAct){
            window.MissionUI?.notify(`Nice practice! This system becomes a story mission in Chapter ${chapterForTask[taskId]}.`);
            return;
        }
        if (this.completedTasks.has(taskId) && taskId !== 'powerRouting') return;
        this.completedTasks.add(taskId);
        window.Beyond?.onLunarTask?.(taskId);
        if (window.Save) window.Save.schedule();
        const discoveries={gravityLab:'🌙 Gravity discovery saved in your Journal!',roverSurvey:'🚙 Rover expedition logged in your Journal!',thermalBalance:'🌡️ Cabin comfort lesson saved!'};
        if (discoveries[taskId]) window.MissionUI?.notify(discoveries[taskId]);

        if (this.currentAct === 1 && this.completedTasks.has('stationTelemetry')) {
            this.loadAct(2);
        } else if (this.currentAct === 2 && this.completedTasks.has('greenhouseLight')) {
            this.loadAct(3);
        } else if (this.currentAct === 3 && this.completedTasks.has('powerRouting')) {
            this.loadAct(4);
        } else if (this.currentAct === 4 && this.completedTasks.has('co2Scrubber') && this.completedTasks.has('waterRecovery')) {
            this.loadAct(5);
        } else if (this.currentAct === 4) {
            this.activeObjective = this.completedTasks.has('co2Scrubber') ? 'Great job! Now repair the water recycler in Life Support.' : 'Repair the air cleaner, then the water recycler in Life Support.';
        } else if (this.currentAct === 5 && this.completedTasks.has('stormShutter')) {
            this.loadAct(6);
        } else if (this.currentAct === 6 && this.completedTasks.has('dsnAlign')) {
            this.activeObjective = 'Communications restored! Great job. Open your Journal, try the Labs, or keep exploring LUNA-7.';
            window.MissionUI?.clearMissionWaypoint?.();
            window.MissionUI?.notify('📡 Signal restored! Mission complete.');
            if (!this.endingShown) { this.endingShown = true; this.showMissionDebrief(); }
        }
    }

    queueDialogue(dialogues) {
        this.dialogueQueue.push(...dialogues);
        if (!this.isDialogueActive) this.displayNextDialogue();
    }

    clearDialogueHandlers() {
        if(this._dialogueAutoTimer){clearTimeout(this._dialogueAutoTimer);this._dialogueAutoTimer=null;}
        if(this._dialogueTimer){clearInterval(this._dialogueTimer);this._dialogueTimer=null;}
        if(this._dialogueAdvance){document.getElementById('dialogueBox')?.removeEventListener('click',this._dialogueAdvance);this._dialogueAdvance=null;}
        if(this._dialogueKeyHandler){document.removeEventListener('keydown',this._dialogueKeyHandler);this._dialogueKeyHandler=null;}
    }

    displayNextDialogue() {
        this.clearDialogueHandlers();
        window.Voice?.stop();
        const box=document.getElementById('dialogueBox');
        if(this.dialogueQueue.length===0){
            this.isDialogueActive=false;
            this._activeLine=null;
            box?.remove();
            return;
        }
        this.isDialogueActive=true;
        const msg=this.dialogueQueue.shift();
        this._activeLine=msg;
        const panel=box||document.createElement('div');
        if(!box){panel.id='dialogueBox';panel.className='dialogue-container';document.body.appendChild(panel);}
        panel.innerHTML=`
          <div class="dialogue-avatar">${this.escapeDialogueHTML(msg.avatar)}</div>
          <div class="dialogue-content">
            <div class="dialogue-speaker">${this.escapeDialogueHTML(msg.speaker)}</div>
            <div class="dialogue-text" id="dialogueTypewriter"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="dialogue-voice-actions">
            <button type="button" class="dialogue-voice-toggle" id="dialogueVoiceToggle" aria-label="Toggle spoken dialogue">${window.Voice?.enabled?'🔊 VOICE ON':'🔇 VOICE OFF'}</button>
            <div class="dialogue-prompt" id="dialoguePrompt" role="status" aria-live="off">SPACE / CLICK TO CONTINUE ▾</div>
          </div>`;
        this.renderDialogueWords(msg.text);
        if(window.Audio){if(msg.speaker==='Fatema')window.Audio.pixChirp('happy');else window.Audio.uiClick();}
        const toggle=panel.querySelector('#dialogueVoiceToggle');
        toggle.addEventListener('click',e=>{
          e.stopPropagation();
          const nowEnabled=window.Voice?.toggle();
          toggle.textContent=nowEnabled?'🔊 VOICE ON':'🔇 VOICE OFF';
          this.clearAutoAdvance();
          this.clearDialogueWordHighlight();
          if(nowEnabled)this.startCurrentDialogueVoice();
          else this.setDialoguePrompt('SPACE / CLICK TO CONTINUE ▾');
        });
        const advance=()=>{
          if(window.Voice?.enabled&&window.Voice?.supported&&window.Voice?.pendingAutoplay){
            window.Voice.activate();
            this.startCurrentDialogueVoice();
            return; // If browser blocked autoplay before any gesture, first interaction starts the line.
          }
          this.displayNextDialogue();
        };
        const keyHandler=e=>{
          if(e.code==='Space'||e.code==='Enter'){
            if(e.target?.closest?.('#dialogueVoiceToggle'))return;
            e.preventDefault();
            if(e.repeat)return;
            advance();
          }
        };
        this._dialogueAdvance=advance;
        this._dialogueKeyHandler=keyHandler;
        panel.addEventListener('click',advance);
        document.addEventListener('keydown',keyHandler);
        this.startCurrentDialogueVoice();
    }

    escapeDialogueHTML(s) {return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}

    renderDialogueWords(text){
      const target=document.getElementById('dialogueTypewriter');if(!target)return;
      this._dialogueWords=[];
      // Keep the complete sentence visible from the beginning. Speech events provide
      // optional word highlighting; no timed typewriter can run ahead of the voice.
      const parts=String(text).matchAll(/\S+|\s+/g);
      for(const part of parts){
        if(/^\s+$/.test(part[0])){target.appendChild(document.createTextNode(part[0]));continue;}
        const span=document.createElement('span');span.className='dialogue-word';span.textContent=part[0];
        target.appendChild(span);this._dialogueWords.push({start:part.index,element:span});
      }
    }

    clearDialogueWordHighlight(){
      if(this._dialogueActiveWord)this._dialogueActiveWord.classList.remove('is-speaking');
      this._dialogueActiveWord=null;
    }
    highlightDialogueAt(index){
      if(!Number.isFinite(index))return;
      const words=this._dialogueWords||[];
      let match=words[0];
      for(const word of words){if(word.start<=index)match=word;else break;}
      if(!match||this._dialogueActiveWord===match.element)return;
      this.clearDialogueWordHighlight();
      this._dialogueActiveWord=match.element;
      match.element.classList.add('is-speaking');
    }
    setDialoguePrompt(value){const prompt=document.getElementById('dialoguePrompt');if(prompt)prompt.textContent=value;}
    clearAutoAdvance(){if(this._dialogueAutoTimer){clearTimeout(this._dialogueAutoTimer);this._dialogueAutoTimer=null;}}

    startCurrentDialogueVoice(){
      const msg=this._activeLine;
      if(!msg)return;
      this.clearAutoAdvance();this.clearDialogueWordHighlight();
      if(!window.Voice?.supported || !window.Voice?.enabled){this.setDialoguePrompt('SPACE / CLICK TO CONTINUE ▾');return;}
      // Do not let world/robot speech interrupt the cinematic introduction.
      if(window.Beyond?.prologueOverlay?.isConnected){this.setDialoguePrompt('FINISH OR SKIP THE INTRODUCTION FIRST');return;}
      const current=msg;
      const ok=window.Voice.speak(msg.speaker,msg.text,{
        onStart:()=>{if(this._activeLine!==current)return;this.setDialoguePrompt('● CREW RECORDING · CLICK TO SKIP ▾');},
        onBoundary:(charIndex)=>{if(this._activeLine===current)this.highlightDialogueAt(charIndex);},
        onEnd:()=>{
          if(this._activeLine!==current)return;
          this.clearDialogueWordHighlight();this.setDialoguePrompt('✓ FINISHED · NEXT LINE ▾');
          if(window.Voice?.autoAdvance){this._dialogueAutoTimer=setTimeout(()=>{this._dialogueAutoTimer=null;if(this._activeLine===current)this.displayNextDialogue();},1000);}
        },
        onBlocked:()=>{if(this._activeLine===current)this.setDialoguePrompt('🔊 TAP / SPACE TO START VOICE');},
        onError:()=>{if(this._activeLine!==current)return;this.clearDialogueWordHighlight();this.setDialoguePrompt('NO RECORDING FOR THIS LINE · CLICK TO CONTINUE ▾');}
      });
      if(ok)this.setDialoguePrompt('● CREW RECORDING · CLICK TO SKIP ▾');
    }

    // Final Mission Debrief & 3 Distinct Endings
    showMissionDebrief() {
        window.Sim.updateAlerts();
        const health = window.Sim.overallHealthScore;
        const batteryPct = Math.round((window.Sim.batteryCharge / window.Sim.batteryCapacityMax) * 100);
        const radio = Math.round(window.Sim.dsnLinkQuality);
        const discoveries=['gravityLab','roverSurvey','thermalBalance'].filter(x=>this.completedTasks.has(x)).length;
        const lastGarden=[...this.decisions].reverse().find(x=>x.type==='gardenPlan');
        const lastPower=[...this.decisions].reverse().find(x=>x.type==='power');
        const lessons=[
            lastGarden?`🌱 Garden: ${lastGarden.powerKW} kW of light and ${lastGarden.waterLitresPerHour} L/hour of water.`:'🌱 Garden: consider how lighting competes with power reserves.',
            lastPower?`⚡ Power: you balanced ${Object.values(lastPower.allocations).reduce((a,b)=>a+Number(b),0)} kW across your chosen systems (plus essentials).`:'⚡ Power: every system needs a share of a limited supply.',
            `🔬 Optional discoveries: ${discoveries} of 3 completed.`
        ];
        
        let rank = '';
        let endingTitle = '';
        let badge = '';
        let reviewText = '';

        if (health >= 85 && batteryPct >= 60) {
            // ENDING 1: BRILLIANT RESCUE
            rank = 'BRILLIANT LUNAR PLANNER';
            endingTitle = '🌟 BRILLIANT RESCUE — LUNA-7 SAFE & OPERATIONAL';
            badge = '🏆';
            reviewText = 'Well planned! Your final station readings show strong reserves and safety. Your choices kept important systems working during a simulated challenge.';
        } else if (health >= 65) {
            // ENDING 2: SUCCESSFUL MISSION WITH COMPROMISES
            rank = 'RESOURCEFUL CREW HELPER';
            endingTitle = '🚀 SUCCESSFUL MISSION WITH COMPROMISES';
            badge = '🥈';
            reviewText = 'You restored communications and completed the mission with some trade-offs. Review your remaining electricity, air, and shelter readiness to plan an even better mission next time.';
        } else {
            // ENDING 3: LEARNING MISSION
            rank = 'CADET IN TRAINING (BACKUP SYSTEMS ENGAGED)';
            endingTitle = '🔍 LEARNING MISSION — VALUABLE SCIENCE LOGGED';
            badge = '🥉';
            reviewText = 'This mission used more reserves than planned. The training simulator gives you another chance to compare strategies and make a more sustainable plan.';
        }

        const debriefModal = document.createElement('div');
        debriefModal.className = 'task-overlay';
        debriefModal.innerHTML = `
            <div class="task-modal debrief-modal">
                <div class="task-header" style="border-bottom: 2px solid #89BE96;">
                    <div class="task-title-group">
                        <span class="task-icon">${badge}</span>
                        <div>
                            <h2>${endingTitle}</h2>
                            <p class="task-subtitle">Independent educational prototype · NASA Space Apps Challenge 2026</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="task-body">
                    <div class="rank-banner">
                        <div>YOUR LUNA-7 MISSION BADGE:</div>
                        <div class="rank-title">${rank}</div>
                    </div>
                    <p style="font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6; margin-bottom: 20px;">${reviewText}</p>
                    <div class="debrief-lessons">${lessons.map(x=>`<p>${x}</p>`).join('')}</div>
                    <div class="power-summary-box">
                        <div>Station Vitality: <strong style="color:#89BE96;">${health}%</strong></div>
                        <div>Battery Reserve: <strong>${batteryPct}%</strong></div>
                        <div>ECLSS O2 Level: <strong style="color:#79C9D8;">${window.Sim.oxygenLevel.toFixed(1)}%</strong></div>
                        <div>Cabin CO2: <strong>${window.Sim.co2Level.toFixed(0)} ppm</strong></div>
                        <div>Water Purity: <strong style="color:#79AEC8;">${window.Sim.waterPurityPercent}%</strong></div>
                        <div>Earth Radio Link: <strong style="color:#89BE96;">${radio}%</strong></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="task-footer">
                    <button class="task-action-btn" id="playAgainBtn" style="background:#79AEC8;">RE-RUN SIMULATION</button>
                    <button class="task-action-btn" id="closeDebriefBtn" style="background:#89BE96;">CONTINUE EXPLORING LUNA-7</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
        document.body.appendChild(debriefModal);

        if (window.Audio) window.Audio.taskSuccess();

        document.getElementById('closeDebriefBtn').onclick = () => {
            debriefModal.remove();
        };

        document.getElementById('playAgainBtn').onclick = () => {
            debriefModal.remove();
            if (window.Save) window.Save.reset();
            else { window.Sim.reset(); window.Story.reset(); window.Story.start(); }
        };
    }
}

window.Story = new StoryEngine();

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT: THE LOST SIGNAL
 * NASA Science Encyclopedia & Mission Journal
 * Real NASA Datasets, Telemetry, Crew Logs & Science Standards.
 */

class MissionJournal {
    constructor() {
        this.isOpen = false;
        this.currentTab = 'objectives';
        this.modalElement = null;
    }

    toggle() {
        if (this.isOpen) {
            this.close();
        } else {
            this.open();
        }
    }

    open() {
        this.isOpen = true;
        if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick();

        const modal = document.createElement('div');
        modal.className = 'task-overlay journal-overlay';
        modal.innerHTML = `
            <div class="task-modal journal-modal">
                <div class="task-header journal-header">
                    <div class="task-title-group">
                        <span class="task-icon">📋</span>
                        <div>
                            <h2>MY ASTRONAUT JOURNAL</h2>
                            <p class="task-subtitle">Explore your mission and the real science behind it</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button class="task-close-btn" id="journalCloseBtn">&times;</button>
                </div>
                <div class="journal-nav">
                    <button class="journal-tab-btn active" data-tab="objectives">MY MISSION</button>
                    <button class="journal-tab-btn" data-tab="telemetry">STATION STATUS</button>
                    <button class="journal-tab-btn" data-tab="science">REAL SPACE SCIENCE</button>
                    <button class="journal-tab-btn" data-tab="crew">MY CREW</button><button class="journal-tab-btn" data-tab="discoveries">DISCOVERIES</button>
                </div>
                <div class="journal-body" id="journalBody"></div>
            </div>
        `;
        document.body.appendChild(modal);
        this.modalElement = modal;

        document.getElementById('journalCloseBtn').onclick = () => this.close();

        const tabs = modal.querySelectorAll('.journal-tab-btn');
        tabs.forEach(tab => {
            tab.onclick = () => {
                tabs.forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
                tab.classList.add('active');
                this.renderTab(tab.dataset.tab);
            };
        });

        this.renderTab('objectives');
    }

    close() {
        this.isOpen = false;
        if (this.modalElement) {
            this.modalElement.remove();
            this.modalElement = null;
        }
    }

    renderTab(tab) {
        this.currentTab = tab;
        const body = document.getElementById('journalBody');
        if (!body) return;

        if (tab === 'objectives') {
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="journal-section">
                    <h3>CURRENT MISSION: ACT ${window.Story.currentAct}</h3>
                    <div class="objective-card">
                        <div class="objective-bullet">▶</div>
                        <div>
                            <strong>${window.Story.activeObjective}</strong>
                            <p style="color:#79AEC8; margin-top:4px;">Consult PIX or your map terminals for room waypoints.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <h3 style="margin-top:25px;">COMPLETED MILESTONES</h3>
                    <div class="milestones-list">
                        ${Array.from(window.Story.completedTasks).map(t => `<div class="milestone-item">✓ ${t}</div>`).join('') || '<p style="color:#888;">No tasks completed yet. Explore LUNA-7!</p>'}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        else if (tab === 'discoveries') {
            const labs=[['gravityLab','🌙','Gravity Drop','Moon gravity makes an object fall more slowly than on Earth.'],['roverSurvey','🚙','Rover Expedition','A longer survey gives more science but uses more stored electricity.'],['thermalBalance','🌡️','Cabin Comfort','Heating protects comfort but competes with air, food, and radio power.'],['lunarObservation','🔭','Telescope Discovery','Earth stays in roughly the same part of the lunar sky at many near-side locations.']];
            body.innerHTML=`<div class="journal-section"><h3>YOUR SCIENCE COLLECTION</h3><p class="guide-lead">Try optional experiments around the station. Each discovery teaches one simple idea.</p><div class="discoveries-grid">${labs.map(([id,emoji,title,lesson])=>`<div class="discovery-card ${window.Story.completedTasks.has(id)?'done':''}"><span>${emoji}</span><strong>${title}</strong><p>${window.Story.completedTasks.has(id)?lesson:'Not discovered yet. Find the activity on your LABS board.'}</p></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
        }
        else if (tab === 'telemetry') {
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="journal-section">
                    <h3>LUNA-7 SIMULATED STATION READINGS</h3>
                    <div class="telemetry-grid">
                        <div class="telem-box">
                            <span class="telem-label">SOLAR GENERATION</span>
                            <span class="telem-value" style="color:#E9AD65;">${window.Sim.solarGeneration.toFixed(1)} kW</span>
                            <span class="telem-sub">Fictional LUNA-7 power array</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="telem-box">
                            <span class="telem-label">BATTERY STORAGE</span>
                            <span class="telem-value" style="color:#89BE96;">${((window.Sim.batteryCharge / window.Sim.batteryCapacityMax) * 100).toFixed(0)}%</span>
                            <span class="telem-sub">${window.Sim.batteryCharge.toFixed(1)} / 250 kWh reserve</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="telem-box">
                            <span class="telem-label">CABIN PRESSURE</span>
                            <span class="telem-value" style="color:#79AEC8;">${window.Sim.cabinPressure.toFixed(1)} kPa</span>
                            <span class="telem-sub">Sea-level standard (1.0 atm)</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="telem-box">
                            <span class="telem-label">OXYGEN FRACTION</span>
                            <span class="telem-value" style="color:#79C9D8;">${window.Sim.oxygenLevel.toFixed(1)}%</span>
                            <span class="telem-sub">Target: 20.8% - 21.2% O2</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="telem-box">
                            <span class="telem-label">CARBON DIOXIDE</span>
                            <span class="telem-value" style="color:${window.Sim.co2Level > 900 ? '#DA7468' : '#89BE96'};">${window.Sim.co2Level.toFixed(0)} ppm</span>
                            <span class="telem-sub">Demo alert starts at 900 ppm</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="telem-box">
                            <span class="telem-label">INTERIOR RADIATION</span>
                            <span class="telem-value" style="color:${window.Sim.interiorRadiation > 50 ? '#DA7468' : '#89BE96'};">${window.Sim.interiorRadiation.toFixed(0)} µSv/hr</span>
                            <span class="telem-sub">Regolith shielding: ${window.Sim.stormShelterShieldLocked ? 'PREPARED' : 'NORMAL'}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        else if (tab === 'science') {
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="journal-section science-docs">
                    <div class="science-doc-item">
                        <h4>🌙 Why things fall slowly on the Moon</h4>
                        <p>Earth's surface gravity is roughly 9.81 m/s², while the Moon's is about 1.62 m/s². For the same drop height without air resistance, an object takes longer to reach the floor on the Moon.</p>
                        <p class="citation">Our optional gravity experiment uses the simplified equation <strong>distance = ½ × gravity × time²</strong>. See NASA's <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/moon/facts/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Moon facts</a> for further context.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="science-doc-item">
                        <h4>🌱 A garden needs electricity</h4>
                        <p>Plants need water and light to grow. The lights and pumps use electricity, so using more for the garden means less for other equipment.</p>
                        <p class="citation">Learn more: <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/missions/station/iss-research/space-station-20th-veggie-a-garden-in-space/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA — Veggie: A Garden in Space</a></p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="science-doc-item">
                        <h4>💨 Our air and water are connected</h4>
                        <p>Space habitats need equipment to manage oxygen, remove carbon dioxide, and recycle water. This machinery needs electricity and maintenance.</p>
                        <p class="citation">Learn more: <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/reference/environmental-control-and-life-support-systems-eclss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA — Environmental Control and Life Support Systems</a></p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="science-doc-item" style="border-left-color:#d6b68a;background:#1c3442">
                        <h4>🔭 A real NASA observation: May 14, 2024</h4>
                        <p>NASA's Solar Dynamics Observatory recorded a strong <strong>X8.7 solar flare</strong>, peaking at <strong>12:51 p.m. EDT</strong>. That class is a real observation; our fictional LUNA-7 emergency uses it only as a reason to practice shelter planning.</p>
                        <p><strong>Important:</strong> An X8.7 flare class is not a lunar astronaut radiation-dose reading. The game's radiation values and shelter scenario are simplified assumptions.</p>
                        <p class="citation">NASA source: <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/blogs/solar-cycle-25/2024/05/14/strong-solar-flare-erupts-from-sun-15/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Solar Dynamics Observatory — Strong Solar Flare, May 14, 2024</a></p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="science-doc-item">
                        <h4>☀️ Space weather can change plans</h4>
                        <p>The Sun sometimes releases energetic particles. During some events, astronauts must consider where they work and how to protect themselves.</p>
                        <p class="citation">Source: <a href="https://api.nasa.gov/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA DONKI — Space Weather Data</a>. Our warning and radiation readings are fictional training values, not live DONKI observations or real astronaut doses.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="science-doc-item">
                        <h4>🌙 The Moon changes how we build</h4>
                        <p>The Moon has almost no atmosphere, and the pattern of sunlight varies by location. Engineers must understand the local terrain before planning solar power.</p>
                        <p class="citation">Learn more: <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/mission/lro/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA — Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter</a>. Our station site and solar availability are simulated, not LRO measurements.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="science-doc-item">
                        <h4>📡 Why can't Earth answer instantly?</h4>
                        <p>Radio waves travel at the speed of light. Across the Moon's average distance of about 384,400 km, a message takes roughly 1.28 seconds to reach Earth. The distance, and therefore the delay, changes.</p>
                        <p class="citation">The basic calculation is distance ÷ speed of light (299,792 km/s). LUNA-7 uses this science in its fictional communication task.</p>
                    </div>
                    <p style="color:#b3c4d0;font-size:12px;margin-top:12px;"><strong>Science transparency:</strong> All displayed LUNA-7 equipment capacities, target thresholds, shielding efficiencies, greenhouse light recipes, and hazard readings are simplified fictional scenarios; they are not NASA flight requirements or measured mission observations.</p>
                </div>`;
        }
        else if (tab === 'crew') {
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="journal-section">
                    <div class="log-entry">
                        <div class="log-header">
                            <strong>LOG 01: ARRIVAL AT SHACKLETON RIM</strong>
                            <span>Rawan, Lead Astrobiologist</span>
                        </div>
                        <p>"We planted our first Mizuna seeds today. The red 660nm diodes give the Greenhouse an ethereal magenta hue. If closed-loop agriculture works here, Mars is next."</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="log-entry">
                        <div class="log-header">
                            <strong>LOG 02: POWER CONDUIT CALIBRATION</strong>
                            <span>Leo, Chief Systems Engineer</span>
                        </div>
                        <p>"Regolith temperature swings between -130°C and +120°C are brutal on thermal couplings. The solid-state battery bank is holding strong at 250 kWh, but we must protect our reserves."</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="log-entry">
                        <div class="log-header">
                            <strong>LOG 03: NOVA CORE INITIALIZATION</strong>
                            <span>Eyad, Station AI</span>
                        </div>
                        <p>"System integrity 99.4%. Life-support electrolysis active. Deep Space Network carrier signal locked with Goldstone antenna station, California."</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
    }
}

window.Journal = new MissionJournal();
