    <script>
/** Local-only, versioned mission saves. No accounts, no tracking. */
class MoonshiftSave {
    constructor() { this.key='moonshift.luna7.v2'; this.pending=false; this.timer=null; }
    capture() {
        const sim=window.Sim, story=window.Story, player=window.Engine?.player;
        if (!sim || !story || !player) return null;
        const allowed=['missionTimeHours','sunIllumination','solarEventMultiplier','solarStormActive','solarStormProgress','solarCapacityMax','solarGeneration','batteryCapacityMax','batteryCharge','powerAllocations','cabinPressure','oxygenLevel','co2Level','temperatureC','relativeHumidity','scrubberOperational','oxygenGeneratorOperational','potableWaterLitres','greywaterLitres','recyclingEfficiency','waterPurityPercent','greenhouseCropHealth','lightSpectrumRatio','moistureLevel','gardenWaterLitresPerHour','harvestProgress','exteriorRadiation','interiorRadiation','stormShelterShieldLocked','earthDistanceKm','lightSpeedKms','signalDelaySeconds','dsnLinkQuality','antennaAzimuth','antennaElevation','antennaAligned'];
        const state={}; for (const k of allowed) state[k]=sim[k];
        return {version:2,savedAt:Date.now(),act:story.currentAct,objective:story.activeObjective,tasks:[...story.completedTasks],decisions:story.decisions,endingShown:story.endingShown,position:{x:player.x,y:player.y},simulation:state};
    }
    save() {
        const data=this.capture(); if (!data) return false;
        try { localStorage.setItem(this.key,JSON.stringify(data)); this.pending=false; return true; }
        catch(e) { console.warn('Local save unavailable:',e); this.pending=true; return false; }
    }
    schedule() { this.pending=true; }
    beginAutoSave() { window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>this.save()); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)this.save()}); this.timer=setInterval(()=>{if(this.pending)this.save()},3000); }
    restore() {
        let data;
        try {data=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.key)||'null');}catch(e){return false;}
        if(!data || data.version!==2 || !Number.isFinite(data.position?.x) || !Number.isFinite(data.position?.y)) return false;
        const sim=window.Sim,story=window.Story;
        if (!data.simulation || typeof data.simulation!=='object' || !Number.isInteger(data.act) || data.act<1 || data.act>6) return false;
        for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(data.simulation)){
            if (!(key in sim)) continue;
            if (typeof sim[key]==='number' && (!Number.isFinite(value))) continue;
            if (typeof value===typeof sim[key] && (typeof value!=='object'||(value!==null&&!Array.isArray(value)))) sim[key]=value;
        }
        sim.batteryCharge=Math.max(0,Math.min(sim.batteryCapacityMax,sim.batteryCharge));
        sim.updateAlerts();
        story.currentAct=data.act;story.activeObjective=String(data.objective||'Explore LUNA-7');
        story.completedTasks=new Set(Array.isArray(data.tasks)?data.tasks.filter(x=>typeof x==='string'):[]);
        story.decisions=Array.isArray(data.decisions)?data.decisions.slice(-50):[];
        story.endingShown=!!data.endingShown;
        if (story.currentAct===6 && story.completedTasks.has('dsnAlign')) {
            story.activeObjective='Communications restored! Explore freely, visit the Labs, or read your Journal.';
            story.endingShown=true;
        }
        window.Engine.player.x=Math.max(200,Math.min(3150,data.position.x));
        window.Engine.player.y=Math.max(350,Math.min(2100,data.position.y));
        window.Engine.camera.x=window.Engine.player.x-window.Engine.camera.w/2;
        window.Engine.camera.y=window.Engine.player.y-window.Engine.camera.h/2;
        return true;
    }
    reset() {
        // Deliberate new mission: clear ONLY this game's saved progress.
        // Keep audio and learning preferences separate from mission history.
        try { localStorage.removeItem(this.key); } catch(e) {}
        window.Audio?.stopAlarm?.();
        window.Minigames?.close?.();
        window.Journal?.close?.();
        document.querySelectorAll('.mission-guide-overlay,.debrief-modal').forEach(el=>{
            const overlay=el.closest('.task-overlay');(overlay||el).remove();
        });
        window.Story.reset();
        window.Beyond?.reset?.();
        window.Sim.reset();
        const engine=window.Engine;
        if(engine?.player){
            Object.assign(engine.player,{x:400,y:1220,vx:0,vy:0,facing:'down',isMoving:false,walkTimer:0});
            engine.activeRoomName='Arrival Dock';engine.nearbyInteractable=null;
            for(const key of ['up','down','left','right','interact','joystickActive'])engine.input[key]=false;
            engine.input.joyX=0;engine.input.joyY=0;
            engine.camera.x=400-engine.camera.w/2;engine.camera.y=1220-engine.camera.h/2;
            if(engine.pix){Object.assign(engine.pix,{x:360,y:1180,targetX:360,targetY:1180,speechBubble:'',speechTimer:0,emotion:'happy'});}
        }
        window.MissionUI?.clearMissionWaypoint?.();
        if(window.MissionUI){window.MissionUI.lastAct=0;window.MissionUI.renderStamp=0;}
        window.Story.start();
        return this.save();
    }
}
window.Save=new MoonshiftSave();

</script>
    <script>
class MoonshiftMissionUI {
    constructor(){ this.lastAct=0;this.lastAlert='';this.waypoint=null;this.renderStamp=0;this.lastSubtitle='';this.difficulty='explorer'; }
    mount(){
        const bar=document.querySelector('.hud-actions-group');
        if(bar){
            bar.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',`<button class="hud-btn" id="missionMapBtn" title="Find your way (M)">🗺 <span>MAP</span></button><button class="hud-btn primary-game-action" id="missionLinksBtn" title="Try a real resource trade-off (G)">⌁ <span>DECIDE</span></button><button class="hud-btn" id="missionMenuBtn" title="Open your crew tablet">☰ <span>MENU</span></button>`);
            document.getElementById('missionMapBtn').onclick=()=>this.showMap();
            document.getElementById('missionLinksBtn').onclick=()=>this.showMissionLinks();
            document.getElementById('missionMenuBtn').onclick=()=>this.showMainMenu();
        }
        const obj=document.querySelector('.hud-objective-bar');
        if(obj){obj.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div id="missionSubline"><span id="chapterChip">CHAPTER 1 / 6</span><span id="conditionChip">☀ Sunlight recharging the battery</span></div>')}
        const hint=document.createElement('div');hint.id='missionToast';hint.setAttribute('role','status');hint.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');document.body.appendChild(hint);
        try{this.difficulty=localStorage.getItem('moonshift.difficulty')||'explorer';}catch(e){this.difficulty='explorer';}
        this.mountTargetHint();
    }
    mountTargetHint(){const e=document.createElement('div');e.id='destinationChip';e.innerHTML='🧭 <strong>Follow the gold arrow</strong><span id="targetDistance"></span>';document.body.appendChild(e);}
    clearMissionWaypoint(){ this.waypoint=null; }
    isMissionComplete(){ const s=window.Story; return !!(s && s.currentAct===6 && s.completedTasks.has('dsnAlign')); }
    getTarget(){
        const s=window.Story;if(!s)return null;
        if(this.waypoint && this.waypoint.act===s.currentAct)return this.waypoint;
        if(this.isMissionComplete()) return null;
        const id=({1:'terminal_hab_holo',2:'task_greenhouse',3:'task_power',4:s.completedTasks.has('co2Scrubber')?'task_water':'task_scrubber',5:'task_shelter',6:'task_comms'})[s.currentAct];
        const item=window.StationMap?.interactables?.find(x=>x.id===id);
        return item?{x:item.x,y:item.y,label:({1:'NOVA GLOBE',2:'MOON GARDEN',3:'POWER ROOM',4:'LIFE SUPPORT',5:'RADIATION SHELTER',6:'COMMUNICATIONS'})[s.currentAct],roomId:item.roomId,act:s.currentAct}:null;
    }
    getNavigationTarget(player){
        const goal=this.getTarget(),map=window.StationMap;
        if(!goal||!player||!map)return goal;
        let goalRoom=goal.roomId || map.getRoomAt(goal.x,goal.y)?.id;
        if(!map.rooms[goalRoom])return goal;
        const links=[['dock','centralHab','dock_hab'],['dock','airlock','dock_airlock'],['centralHab','quarters','hab_quarters'],['centralHab','shelter','hab_shelter'],['centralHab','power','hab_power'],['centralHab','lifeSupport','hab_lifesupport'],['centralHab','observation','hab_obs'],['observation','greenhouse','obs_greenhouse'],['observation','comms','obs_comms'],['shelter','comms','shelter_comms']];
        const route=(start)=>{
            const queue=[[start]],seen=new Set([start]);
            while(queue.length){const path=queue.shift(),last=path.at(-1);if(last===goalRoom)return path;
                for(const [a,b] of links){const next=a===last?b:b===last?a:null;if(next&&!seen.has(next)){seen.add(next);queue.push([...path,next]);}}
            }return null;
        };
        let room=map.getRoomAt(player.x,player.y)?.id;
        if(room==='corridor'){
            const c=map.corridors.find(c=>player.x>=c.x&&player.x<=c.x+c.w&&player.y>=c.y&&player.y<=c.y+c.h);
            const edge=c&&links.find(([, ,id])=>id===c.id);
            if(edge){const pathA=route(edge[0]),pathB=route(edge[1]);
                room=(pathA?.length||99)<=(pathB?.length||99)?edge[0]:edge[1];
                const b=map.rooms[room].bounds;
                return {...goal,x:b.x+b.w/2,y:b.y+b.h/2,via:map.rooms[room].name};
            }
        }
        if(!room||room===goalRoom)return goal;
        const path=route(room);if(!path||path.length<2)return goal;
        const next=path[1];
        const cId=links.find(([a,b])=>(a===room&&b===next)||(a===next&&b===room))?.[2];
        const c=map.corridors.find(c=>c.id===cId);
        if(!c)return goal;
        return {...goal,x:c.x+c.w/2,y:c.y+c.h/2,via:map.rooms[next].name};
    }
    notify(message){const e=document.getElementById('missionToast');if(!e)return;e.textContent=message;e.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(this._toast);this._toast=setTimeout(()=>e.classList.remove('show'),2900);}
    saveNow(){this.notify(window.Save?.save()?'✓ Adventure saved on this device':'Saving is unavailable in this browser.');}
    update(){
        const s=window.Story,sim=window.Sim,engine=window.Engine;if(!s||!sim||!engine?.player)return;
        const now=performance.now(); if(now-this.renderStamp<300)return;this.renderStamp=now;
        if(this.lastAct!==s.currentAct){this.waypoint=null;this.lastAct=s.currentAct;this.notify(`Chapter ${s.currentAct} · ${['Welcome aboard','The Moon Garden','Power puzzle','Breathe easy','Shelter challenge','The lost signal'][s.currentAct-1]}`)}
        const c=document.getElementById('chapterChip'),e=document.getElementById('conditionChip');
        if(c)c.textContent=`✦ CHAPTER ${s.currentAct} / 6`;
        if(e){ e.textContent=sim.powerShortfall?'⚠️ Energy shortage — visit DECIDE':sim.netPower<0?`🔋 Battery discharging · ${Math.abs(sim.netPower).toFixed(1)} kW`:`☀ Solar surplus · ${sim.netPower.toFixed(1)} kW`;e.style.display=(sim.powerShortfall||sim.batteryCharge<65)?'inline-flex':'none';}
        const t=this.getTarget(),nav=this.getNavigationTarget(engine.player),chip=document.getElementById('destinationChip');
        if(chip){
            if(t){const d=Math.round(Math.hypot(engine.player.x-t.x,engine.player.y-t.y));chip.style.display='flex';chip.classList.toggle('is-complete',false);chip.querySelector('strong').textContent=d<100?'You found it! Press E':`GO TO ${t.label}`;chip.querySelector('#targetDistance').textContent=d<100?'':nav?.via?` · via ${nav.via}`:' · ~'+Math.ceil(d/20)+' steps';}
            else if(this.isMissionComplete()){chip.style.display='flex';chip.classList.add('is-complete');chip.querySelector('strong').textContent='Mission complete';chip.querySelector('#targetDistance').textContent=' · Explore freely';}
            else{chip.style.display='none';}
        }
    }
    modal(title,content){
        document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay')?.remove();
        const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='task-overlay mission-guide-overlay';
        overlay.innerHTML=`<section class="task-modal mission-guide" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="guideTitle" tabindex="-1"><div class="task-header"><div class="task-title-group"><span class="task-icon">🌙</span><div><h2 id="guideTitle">${title}</h2><p class="task-subtitle">LUNA-7 • TRAINING ADVENTURE</p></div></div><button class="task-close-btn" id="closeMissionGuide" aria-label="Close">×</button></div><div class="task-body">${content}</div></section>`;
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);overlay.querySelector('section').focus();
        overlay.querySelector('#closeMissionGuide').onclick=()=>overlay.remove();
        overlay.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===overlay)overlay.remove()});
        return overlay;
    }
    showMainMenu(){
        const modal=this.modal('CREW TABLET',`<p class="guide-lead">Everything you need, without covering your Moon adventure.</p><div class="crew-menu-grid">
            <button data-action="worlds" class="crew-menu-featured">✦ <strong>Explore the Universe</strong><small>Moon · Earth Orbit · Mars · New missions</small></button>
            <button data-action="quick" class="crew-menu-featured">🚀 <strong>PIX’s Rescue Drill</strong><small>3 fun choices · about 2 minutes</small></button>
            <button data-action="profile">♜ <strong>Astronaut Profile</strong><small>Mission XP · Badges · Personal bests</small></button>
            <button data-action="voice">🎙 <strong>Character Narration</strong><small>${window.Voice?.supported ? window.Voice.enabled?'On · Crew recordings':'Off · Tap to enable':'Not supported in this browser'}</small></button>
            <button data-action="help">❔ <strong>How to Play</strong><small>Easy controls & hints</small></button>
            <button data-action="labs">🌙 <strong>Science Labs</strong><small>Three optional adventures</small></button>
            <button data-action="journal">📖 <strong>Mission Journal</strong><small>Your discoveries & decisions</small></button>
            <button data-action="save">💾 <strong>Save Progress</strong><small>Only on this device</small></button>
            <button data-action="audio">🎵 <strong>Music & Sounds</strong><small id="menuSoundState">${window.Audio?.muted?'Currently muted':'Currently on'}</small></button>
            <button data-action="science">🔬 <strong>NASA & Science</strong><small>What is real versus simulated</small></button>
            <button data-action="restart" class="crew-menu-restart">↻ <strong>Start Again</strong><small>Restart from Chapter 1 · clears saved progress</small></button>
            </div>`);
        modal.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{
            const action=btn.dataset.action;
            modal.remove();
            if(action==='quick')window.QuickMission?.start();
            if(action==='worlds')window.Beyond?.openHub();
            if(action==='profile')window.Beyond?.openProfile();
            if(action==='voice'){
                const isOn=window.Voice?.toggle();
                this.notify(window.Voice?.supported?isOn?'Character narration enabled':'Character narration disabled':'Recorded audio playback is not supported here. Subtitles remain available.');
            }
            if(action==='help')this.showHelp();
            if(action==='labs')this.showActivities();
            if(action==='journal')window.Journal?.open();
            if(action==='save')this.saveNow();
            if(action==='audio'){
                window.Audio?.init();window.Audio?.toggleMute();
                this.notify(window.Audio?.muted?'Music and sounds muted':'Music and sounds enabled');
            }
            if(action==='science')this.showScienceSources();
            if(action==='restart')this.showRestartConfirmation();
        });
    }
    showRestartConfirmation(){
        const modal=this.modal('START A NEW MISSION',`<div class="restart-question"><span class="restart-illustration">🌙</span><h3>Ready for a fresh adventure?</h3><p>You will return to the Arrival Dock at Chapter 1. Your current mission progress and discoveries on this device will be replaced.</p><p class="restart-safe-note">Music and learning preferences stay the same.</p></div><div class="restart-actions"><button type="button" id="restartCancel" class="restart-cancel">KEEP PLAYING</button><button type="button" id="restartConfirm" class="restart-confirm">↻ YES, START AGAIN</button></div>`);
        modal.querySelector('#restartCancel').onclick=()=>modal.remove();
        modal.querySelector('#restartConfirm').onclick=()=>{
            modal.remove();
            const didReset=window.Save?.reset();
            this.clearMissionWaypoint();
            this.lastAct=0;
            this.renderStamp=0;
            this.notify(didReset?'New adventure started! Meet PIX at the Arrival Dock.':'New adventure started (saving unavailable).');
            setTimeout(()=>window.Beyond?.showPrologue?.(),200);
        };
    }
    showScienceSources(){
        this.modal('THE REAL SCIENCE',`<p class="guide-lead">LUNA-7 is a fictional training base. The ideas are real, but most numbers are simplified for learning.</p>
          <div class="science-proof-grid">
            <article><strong>🔋 Real engineering</strong><p>Power, water recycling, air management and plants compete for limited resources. <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/reference/environmental-control-and-life-support-systems-eclss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA ECLSS research</a></p></article>
            <article><strong>☀️ Real NASA observation</strong><p>NASA recorded an <b>X8.7 solar flare on May 14, 2024</b>. We use this archived observation to introduce a fictional shelter drill, <em>not</em> to predict lunar radiation exposure. <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/blogs/solar-cycle-25/2024/05/14/strong-solar-flare-erupts-from-sun-15/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA's report</a></p></article>
            <article><strong>🌙 Actual gravity values</strong><p>NASA publishes surface gravity of approximately 1.62 m/s² on the Moon, 3.71 m/s² on Mars and 9.81 m/s² on Earth. These power our falling-object HoloLab; its no-air-resistance model is simplified. <a href="https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20210024855/downloads/BVAD_2.15.22-final.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA gravity reference</a></p></article>
            <article><strong>📡 Interplanetary messages</strong><p>NASA explains that one-way Earth–Mars radio delay changes with distance, roughly four to twenty-four minutes. Our Mars relay challenge calculates a sample delay by dividing the chosen distance by the speed of light. <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/centers-and-facilities/goddard/space-communications-7-things-you-need-to-know/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA communications reference</a></p></article>
            <article><strong>🎮 Simulated gameplay</strong><p>LUNA-7's batteries, machinery, water use, crop thresholds and shielding values are authored training assumptions. They are not NASA measurements, mission hardware specifications or a real-time forecast.</p></article>
          </div><p class="science-note">Our objective is to help children understand the <strong>trade-off</strong>, not pretend the game is a certified spacecraft simulator.</p>`);
    }
    showHelp(){const modal=this.modal('MISSION HELPER',`<p class="guide-lead">You're an astronaut on the Moon! Explore, meet your crew and make clever choices to keep the station safe.</p><div class="simple-guide"><div><span>🕹</span><strong>MOVE</strong><p>WASD or arrows. On mobile, use the joystick.</p></div><div><span>✨</span><strong>EXPLORE</strong><p>Follow the gold arrow to an important console.</p></div><div><span>✋</span><strong>INTERACT</strong><p>Get near glowing equipment; press E or USE.</p></div><div><span>🌱</span><strong>EXPERIMENT</strong><p>Try a plan. Watch your power, air and safety change.</p></div></div><div class="learning-mode"><strong>How much help would you like?</strong><div class="learning-options"><button type="button" data-diff="explorer" class="task-action-btn">🌟 Explorer · more hints</button><button type="button" data-diff="specialist" class="task-action-btn">🧠 Specialist · fewer hints</button></div></div><p class="science-note">Try <strong>LABS (L)</strong> for three optional discoveries: gravity, rover planning and cabin heating. LUNA-7 is fictional; its power and resource numbers are simplified teaching scenarios, not NASA measurements.</p><button class="guide-reset" id="resetMissionBtn">Restart adventure</button>`);
        modal.querySelectorAll('[data-diff]').forEach(b=>{b.classList.toggle('selected',b.dataset.diff===this.difficulty);b.onclick=()=>{this.difficulty=b.dataset.diff;try{localStorage.setItem('moonshift.difficulty',this.difficulty);}catch(e){}modal.querySelectorAll('[data-diff]').forEach(n=>n.classList.toggle('selected',n===b));this.notify(this.difficulty==='explorer'?'Explorer hints enabled':'Specialist mode enabled');};});
        modal.querySelector('#resetMissionBtn').onclick=()=>this.showRestartConfirmation();
    }
    showMap(){
        const map=window.StationMap,player=window.Engine?.player;if(!map||!player)return;
        const target=this.getTarget();
        const colors={dock:'#79aec8',airlock:'#d6b68a',centralHab:'#8acdde',quarters:'#cfbc9d',shelter:'#da8e87',power:'#e9ad65',lifeSupport:'#76c7d5',greenhouse:'#91c99d',comms:'#79aec8',observation:'#b7d4de'};
        const short={dock:'ARRIVAL',airlock:'AIRLOCK',centralHab:'CENTRAL HABITAT',quarters:'CREW QUARTERS',shelter:'RADIATION SHELTER',power:'POWER ROOM',lifeSupport:'LIFE SUPPORT',greenhouse:'GREENHOUSE',comms:'COMMUNICATIONS',observation:'OBSERVATION'};const roomRects=Object.values(map.rooms).map(r=>{const b=r.bounds,t=short[r.id]||r.name;const c=colors[r.id]||'#8dbacc';return `<g data-room="${r.id}" class="map-room" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Navigate to ${t}"><rect x="${b.x}" y="${b.y}" width="${b.w}" height="${b.h}" rx="25" fill="${c}" fill-opacity=".16" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="13"/><rect x="${b.x+22}" y="${b.y+22}" width="${b.w-44}" height="${b.h-44}" rx="8" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-opacity=".17" stroke-width="4"/><text x="${b.x+b.w/2}" y="${b.y+b.h/2}" fill="#e9f5ff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Arial,sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="43">${t}</text></g>`}).join('');
        const corridors=map.corridors.map(c=>c.bend?`<path d="${map.corridorPathData(c)}" fill="none" stroke="#355a69" stroke-width="${c.bend.width}" stroke-linecap="butt"/>`:`<path d="${map.corridorPathData(c)}" fill="#30495a" stroke="none"/>`).join('');
        const marker=target?`<circle cx="${target.x}" cy="${target.y}" r="66" fill="rgba(239,186,120,.18)" stroke="#ffd69a" stroke-width="15" stroke-dasharray="32 22"/>`:'';
        const svg=`<svg id="stationMapSvg" viewBox="90 225 3200 2100" role="img" aria-label="Top down station map. Click a room to set your destination." xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><rect width="3400" height="2400" fill="#0d1d2b"/>${corridors}${roomRects}${marker}<circle id="mapPlayerMarker" cx="${player.x}" cy="${player.y}" r="54" fill="#fff" stroke="#55d6e8" stroke-width="18"/><circle cx="${player.x}" cy="${player.y}" r="14" fill="#2a9eb5"/></svg>`;
        const overlay=this.modal('THE LUNA-7 STATION',`<p class="guide-lead">You are the blue-and-white dot. The <strong>gold circle</strong> is your next mission. Tap a room to choose your own destination.</p><div class="map-canvas-frame">${svg}</div><p class="map-caption" id="mapCaption">${target?'Current goal: '+target.label:'Explore the station.'}</p><div class="room-quick-picks">${Object.values(map.rooms).map(r=>`<button class="room-pick" data-room="${r.id}">${short[r.id]}</button>`).join('')}</div><button class="task-action-btn map-go" id="mapGoBtn">LET'S GO!</button>`);
        overlay.querySelectorAll('.map-room,.room-pick').forEach(el=>{const select=()=>{const room=map.rooms[el.dataset.room];this.waypoint={x:room.bounds.x+room.bounds.w/2,y:room.bounds.y+room.bounds.h/2,label:room.name.toUpperCase(),roomId:room.id,act:window.Story.currentAct};overlay.querySelector('#mapCaption').textContent=`Waypoint set: ${room.name}`;overlay.querySelectorAll('.map-room,.room-pick').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('selected'));el.classList.add('selected');};el.onclick=select;el.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();select()}};});
        overlay.querySelector('#mapGoBtn').onclick=()=>overlay.remove();
    }
    showActivities(){
        const experiments=[
            {id:'gravityLab',icon:'🌙',title:'Gravity Drop',tag:'OBSERVATION DOME',text:'Watch objects fall differently on Earth and the Moon.'},
            {id:'roverSurvey',icon:'🚙',title:'Rover Expedition',tag:'AIRLOCK',text:'Choose how to spend scarce rover electricity.'},
            {id:'thermalBalance',icon:'🌡️',title:'Cabin Comfort',tag:'CREW QUARTERS',text:'Keep astronauts comfortable while conserving power.'}
        ];
        const tasks=window.Story?.completedTasks || new Set();
        const modal=this.modal('EXPLORER ACTIVITIES',`<p class="guide-lead">Three little discoveries to try whenever you like. They are optional — your main story stays easy to follow.</p><div class="activity-progress"><span>✦ SCIENCE DISCOVERIES</span><strong>${experiments.filter(x=>tasks.has(x.id)).length} / 3</strong></div><div class="activity-list">${experiments.map(x=>`<button type="button" class="activity-choice" data-task="${x.id}"><span class="activity-icon">${x.icon}</span><span class="activity-copy"><strong>${x.title} ${tasks.has(x.id)?'✓':''}</strong><small>${x.tag} · ${x.text}</small></span><span class="activity-go">${tasks.has(x.id)?'REPLAY':'FIND'} ↗</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="science-note">Completed discoveries appear in your Journal. Rover travel uses fictional mission energy; experiment values are explained in each activity.</p>`);
        modal.querySelectorAll('[data-task]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
            const task=window.StationMap.interactables.find(x=>x.minigameId===button.dataset.task);
            if(!task)return;
            this.waypoint={x:task.x,y:task.y,roomId:task.roomId,label:task.name.toUpperCase(),act:window.Story.currentAct};
            modal.remove();this.notify(`🧭 Follow the gold arrow to ${task.name}`);
        });
    }
    showMissionLinks(){
        if(window.DecisionLab) return window.DecisionLab.open();
        const sim=window.Sim,p=sim.powerAllocations,batt=sim.batteryCharge,cap=sim.batteryCapacityMax,net=sim.netPower;
        this.modal('HOW THE STATION WORKS',`<p class="guide-lead">One source of electricity. Many important jobs. Can you spot why every decision matters?</p><div class="systems-diagram" aria-label="Station systems all share electrical power"><div class="link-central">☀️ <strong>SOLAR PANELS</strong><small>${sim.solarGeneration.toFixed(1)} kW available now</small></div><div class="diagram-connector">↓</div><div class="link-central battery">🔋 <strong>SHARED BATTERY</strong><small>${batt.toFixed(0)} / ${cap.toFixed(0)} kWh stored</small></div><div class="diagram-connector">↓</div><div class="systems-branches"><div>💨<strong>AIR</strong><small>${p.lifeSupport} kW</small></div><div>🌱<strong>PLANTS</strong><small>${p.greenhouse} kW</small></div><div>📡<strong>RADIO</strong><small>${p.communications} kW</small></div><div>🌡<strong>WARMTH</strong><small>${p.thermalHeaters} kW</small></div></div></div><div class="choice-intro"><strong>${net>=0?'☀ Sunlight covers current power needs.':'🔋 The battery is helping right now.'}</strong><p>${net>=0?`The station could store approximately ${net.toFixed(1)} kWh of extra energy per simulated hour, until the battery is full.`:`If these choices stay the same, the station would use about ${Math.abs(net).toFixed(1)} kWh of stored energy per simulated hour.`} This is a simplified training model, not a real NASA forecast.</p></div><button class="task-action-btn" id="goPowerBtn">SHOW ME THE POWER ROOM</button><p class="science-note">In the game, electricity is measured in kW, while stored energy uses kWh. The garden also needs water, and our shelter needs preparation before a hazardous event.</p>`).querySelector('#goPowerBtn').onclick=()=>{this.waypoint={x:1050,y:1750,label:'POWER ROOM',act:window.Story.currentAct};document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay')?.remove();this.notify('Gold arrow now points to the Power Room');};
    }
}
window.MissionUI=new MoonshiftMissionUI();

    <script>
class MissionDecisionLab {
 open(){
  const sim=window.Sim, ui=window.MissionUI;
  if(!sim||!ui)return;
  const art=(n)=>window.MoonshiftIcons?.markup(n)||'';
  const world=window.Story?.world||'moon';
  const place=world==='mars'?'ARES-1 GREEN DOME':world==='orbit'?'ORBITAL BOTANY EXPERIMENT':'LUNA-7 MOON GARDEN';
  const intro=world==='orbit'?'A biology experiment in orbit needs electricity and water. Can we protect the crew and still run it?':world==='mars'?'Plants grow inside a protected dome on Mars. Dust limits solar power, so every watt matters.':'Plants need light and water. Both have a cost. Choose a plan and see what happens next.';
  const baseLight=Number(sim.powerAllocations.greenhouse),baseWater=Number(sim.gardenWaterLitresPerHour);
  const baseDemand=Object.values(sim.powerAllocations).reduce((s,v)=>s+Number(v),0);
  const net0=sim.solarGeneration-baseDemand;
  const html=`
   <section class="v20-intro"><div class="v21-power-illustration" role="img" aria-label="Solar panels supply grow lights, and batteries store any spare electricity"><div class="v21-diagram-cell">${art('solar')}<small>Sunlight</small></div><span class="v21-diagram-link">→</span><div class="v21-diagram-cell">${art('plant')}<small>Crops</small></div><span class="v21-diagram-link">↔</span><div class="v21-diagram-cell">${art('battery')}<small>Battery</small></div></div>
    <div><p class="v20-eyebrow">${place} · RESOURCE CHALLENGE</p><h3>Grow something. Keep the crew safe.</h3><p>${intro}</p></div></section>
   <div class="v20-status" role="status" id="v20Status"><span class="v20-led"></span><strong id="v20StatusTitle">Checking power…</strong><span id="v20StatusHint"></span></div>
   <div class="v20-numbers" aria-label="Current station conditions"><div>${art('solar')}<span><small>Solar electricity</small><strong>${sim.solarGeneration.toFixed(1)} kW</strong></span></div><div>${art('power')}<span><small>All machines need</small><strong>${baseDemand.toFixed(1)} kW</strong></span></div><div>${art('battery')}<span><small>Energy stored</small><strong>${Math.round(sim.batteryCharge)} / ${Math.round(sim.batteryCapacityMax)} kWh</strong></span></div></div>
   <div class="v20-plan-title"><h4>1. Pick a strategy</h4><p>Or use the sliders to create your own.</p></div>
   <div class="v20-preset-list"><button type="button" data-plan="balanced">${art('plant')}<strong>Balanced</strong><small>Plants grow steadily</small></button><button type="button" data-plan="save">${art('battery')}<strong>Save power</strong><small>Protect stored energy</small></button><button type="button" data-plan="grow">${art('solar')}<strong>Grow faster</strong><small>Uses more electricity</small></button></div>
   <div class="v20-controls"><label> ${art('power')} <span><strong>${world==='orbit'?'Experiment lights':'Grow lights'}</strong><small>More light uses more electricity</small></span><output id="labLightVal"></output><input id="labLight" type="range" min="2" max="15" step="1" value="${baseLight}"></label><label>${art('water')}<span><strong>Garden water</strong><small>Water is also needed by the crew</small></span><output id="labWaterVal"></output><input id="labWater" type="range" min="1" max="5" step="1" value="${baseWater}"></label></div>
   <div class="v20-plan-title"><h4>2. See what happens</h4><p>Forecast after two simulated hours. Other systems stay the same.</p></div>
   <div class="v20-result-grid"><div class="v20-result"><small>CURRENT PLAN</small><strong id="labBeforeBattery"></strong><div class="v20-meter"><div id="labBeforeBar"></div></div><span id="labBeforeInfo"></span></div><div class="v20-result v20-result-preview"><small>YOUR NEW PLAN</small><strong id="labAfterBattery"></strong><div class="v20-meter"><div id="labAfterBar"></div></div><span id="labAfterInfo"></span></div></div>
   <p class="v20-insight" id="labInsight" aria-live="polite"></p>
   <details class="v20-learn"><summary>What is the difference between kW and kWh?</summary><p><b>kW</b> tells us how quickly electricity is being used. <b>kWh</b> tells us how much energy the battery holds. If machines need more power than solar panels give, the battery empties. If it reaches zero while there's still a shortfall, the mission fails.</p><p>These numbers describe a <b>fictional training station</b> — they are not NASA measurements. <a href="https://www.nasa.gov/reference/environmental-control-and-life-support-systems-eclss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn about real NASA life support.</a></p></details>
   <div class="v20-actions"><button type="button" id="labApply" class="v20-primary">Use this plan</button></div>`;
  const modal=ui.modal('THE GREENHOUSE CHALLENGE',html);modal.classList.add('v20-lab-overlay');
  const qs=s=>modal.querySelector(s),set=(s,t)=>{const e=qs(s);if(e)e.textContent=t;};
  const light=qs('#labLight'),water=qs('#labWater');
  const clamp=x=>Math.min(sim.batteryCapacityMax,Math.max(0,x));
  const forecast=n=>clamp(sim.batteryCharge+n*2);
  const current=forecast(net0);
  const beforeInfo=net0<0?`${Math.abs(net0).toFixed(1)} kW more needed than available`:`${net0.toFixed(1)} kW spare`;
  set('#labBeforeInfo',beforeInfo);
  set('#labBeforeBattery',`${Math.round(current)} kWh`);
  function bar(s,value){const el=qs(s);el.style.width=`${Math.min(100,Math.max(0,value/sim.batteryCapacityMax*100))}%`;el.classList.toggle('is-low',value<sim.batteryCapacityMax*.2);}
  bar('#labBeforeBar',current);
  function preview(){
    const l=Number(light.value),w=Number(water.value),need=baseDemand-baseLight+l,net=sim.solarGeneration-need;
    const energy=forecast(net),diff=energy-current;
    set('#labLightVal',`${l} kW`);set('#labWaterVal',`${w} L/h`);
    set('#labAfterBattery',`${Math.round(energy)} kWh`);
    set('#labAfterInfo',net<0?`${Math.abs(net).toFixed(1)} kW shortfall`:`${net.toFixed(1)} kW spare`);
    bar('#labAfterBar',energy);
    const crit=sim.batteryCharge<=.001 && net<0;
    const label=qs('#v20Status');label.classList.toggle('is-danger',sim.batteryCharge<=.001 && net<0);label.classList.toggle('is-warn',sim.batteryCharge<sim.batteryCapacityMax*.2 || net<0);
    set('#v20StatusTitle',crit?'Danger — battery empty, power shortfall!':sim.batteryCharge<=.001?'Battery empty — solar still supplies the station':net<0?'Battery is draining':'Solar panels meet current needs');
    set('#v20StatusHint',crit?'This plan cannot run the station. Choose a stronger rescue plan.':net<0?`Battery use: ${Math.abs(net).toFixed(1)} kWh per hour`:`Surplus: ${net.toFixed(1)} kWh per hour`);
    const healthy=l>=7&&w>=2;
    let msg=healthy?'Plants have enough light and water in this learning model.':'Plants may grow more slowly, but you save resources.';
    if(Math.abs(diff)>.5)msg+=diff>0?` You save ${Math.round(diff)} kWh versus the current plan.`:` You use ${Math.round(-diff)} kWh more.`;
    if(energy===0&&net<0)msg+=' This plan still loses the power challenge: essential systems need more electricity.';
    if(net>=0)msg+=' Solar energy now covers the planned loads.';
    set('#labInsight',msg);
    qs('#labApply').disabled=sim.batteryCharge<=.001 && net<0;
    qs('#labApply').textContent=crit?'Not enough electricity':'Use this plan';
  }
  light.addEventListener('input',preview);water.addEventListener('input',preview);
  const plans={balanced:[7,2],save:[2,1],grow:[12,4]};
  modal.querySelectorAll('[data-plan]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{const [l,w]=plans[b.dataset.plan];light.value=l;water.value=w;modal.querySelectorAll('[data-plan]').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('is-selected',x===b));preview();window.Audio?.uiClick?.();}));
  qs('#labApply').addEventListener('click',()=>{
   const l=Number(light.value),w=Number(water.value);
   sim.setPowerAllocation('greenhouse',l);
   sim.gardenWaterLitresPerHour=w;
   sim.moistureLevel=Math.max(35,Math.min(95,40+w*10));
   sim.update(0);
   window.Story?.decisions?.push({type:'decisionLab',powerKW:l,waterLitresPerHour:w,missionHour:sim.missionTimeHours});
   window.Save?.save?.();modal.remove();
   window.MissionUI?.notify?.(`Plan applied: ${l} kW grow lights, ${w} L/h water.`);
   window.Engine?.pix?.say?.('Nice choice! Watch how your battery changes.','happy',4);
   window.Audio?.uiConfirm?.();
  });
  preview();
 }
}
window.DecisionLab=new MissionDecisionLab();

</script>
    <script>
/** MOONSHIFT | PIX's 3-minute rescue drill.
 * Standalone deterministic strategy practice with openly authored training values.
 * This NEVER mutates the main story's resources or invents NASA measurements.
 */
class MoonshiftQuickMission {
  constructor(){ this.round=0; this.history=[]; this.state=null; }
  get rounds(){return [
    {icon:'☀️',name:'THE SHADOW',story:'The Sun slips behind a crater rim. The station has less electricity. What will you protect?',choices:[
      {icon:'🔋',title:'Save the batteries',description:'Reduce non-essential power for now.',change:{battery:10,air:-6,food:-5},reason:'Using less power saves stored energy, but air support and grow lights slow down.'},
      {icon:'💨',title:'Protect the air',description:'Give more power to the air machines.',change:{battery:-14,air:12,food:-6},reason:'Air management improves, but the extra electricity comes from the battery.'},
      {icon:'🌿',title:'Help the plants',description:'Keep the garden lights running brightly.',change:{battery:-17,air:-4,food:17},reason:'The plants do better, but the battery and air machines have less to work with.'}
    ]},
    {icon:'💧',name:'THE WATER PROBLEM',story:'A recycling system needs attention. Your choice changes what the crew can afford next.',choices:[
      {icon:'🫧',title:'Boost the air cleaner',description:'Use extra power to clean cabin air faster.',change:{battery:-13,air:18,food:-3},reason:'Stronger air cleaning needs electricity; fewer resources remain for other systems.'},
      {icon:'💧',title:'Repair the recycler',description:'Spend some energy to reuse more water.',change:{battery:-7,air:6,food:5},reason:'Recycling helps the crew and garden, but the repair still costs power.'},
      {icon:'🔬',title:'Continue plant research',description:'Keep the growing experiment active.',change:{battery:-5,air:-9,food:11},reason:'The experiment helps the garden, but some air-management work waits.'}
    ]},
    {icon:'🛡️',name:'SPACE WEATHER',story:'A practice space-weather alert arrives. Get the crew ready without losing essential systems.',choices:[
      {icon:'⚡',title:'Power the shelter',description:'Prepare quickly using powered equipment.',change:{battery:-18,safety:25},reason:'Fast shielding preparation helps protect the crew, but uses a lot of stored energy.'},
      {icon:'🧰',title:'Prepare it manually',description:'Take longer and use less battery energy.',change:{battery:-5,safety:18,air:-5},reason:'Manual work preserves power, but it takes time and delays some air-management work.'},
      {icon:'🔭',title:'Finish the science trip',description:'Keep researching instead of preparing now.',change:{battery:-11,safety:-15,food:8},reason:'More research is exciting, but shelter readiness falls during a hazard. Safety comes first.'}
    ]}
  ]; }
  start(){
    this.round=0;this.history=[];
    this.state={battery:70,air:72,safety:68,food:54};
    this.render();
  }
  clamp(x){return Math.max(0,Math.min(100,x));}
  applyChange(before,delta){const next={...before};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(delta))next[k]=this.clamp(next[k]+v);return next;}
  choose(index){
    const round=this.rounds[this.round];if(!round||index<0||index>=round.choices.length)return;
    const choice=round.choices[index];
    const previous={...this.state};this.state=this.applyChange(this.state,choice.change);
    this.history.push({name:round.name,choice:choice.title,reason:choice.reason,before:previous,after:{...this.state}});
    window.Audio?.uiConfirm();
    this.renderFeedback(choice);
  }
  badge(label,key){const n=this.state[key];const color=n<40?'is-low':n<62?'is-mid':'is-good';return `<div class="quick-stat ${color}"><span>${label}</span><strong>${n}%</strong><div class="quick-track"><i style="width:${n}%"></i></div></div>`;}
  render(){
    const round=this.rounds[this.round];if(!round){this.renderEnd();return;}
    const body=`<div class="quick-shell"><p class="quick-eyebrow">PIX'S RESCUE DRILL · ROUND ${this.round+1} OF 3</p>
      <div class="quick-progress" aria-label="Round ${this.round+1} of 3"><i style="width:${this.round/3*100}%"></i></div>
      <div class="quick-status" aria-label="Fictional mission status">${this.badge('🔋 Power','battery')}${this.badge('💨 Air','air')}${this.badge('🛡️ Safety','safety')}${this.badge('🌱 Garden','food')}</div>
      <div class="quick-story"><span class="quick-story-icon">${round.icon}</span><div><h3>${round.name}</h3><p>${round.story}</p></div></div>
      <div class="quick-choices">${round.choices.map((c,i)=>`<button type="button" class="quick-choice" data-choice="${i}">
        <span class="quick-choice-icon">${c.icon}</span><span class="quick-choice-copy"><strong>${c.title}</strong><small>${c.description}</small>
        <span class="quick-tradeoff">${Object.entries(c.change).map(([k,v])=>`<span class="${v>=0?'gain':'cost'}">${v>0?'+':''}${v} ${k==='food'?'garden':k}</span>`).join('')}</span></span><span class="quick-chevron">›</span></button>`).join('')}</div>
      <p class="quick-footnote">These percentages are fictional game-training points, not measured NASA values. There's no timer: think, choose, and learn.</p></div>`;
    const modal=window.MissionUI.modal('PIX’S RESCUE DRILL',body);
    modal.classList.add('quick-mission-overlay');
    modal.querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>this.choose(Number(b.dataset.choice)));
  }
  renderFeedback(choice){
    const h=this.history.at(-1),nextRound=this.round+1;
    const rows=Object.keys(h.after).map(k=>{const d=h.after[k]-h.before[k];return `<span class="quick-change ${d>0?'gain':d<0?'cost':''}">${k==='food'?'Garden':k[0].toUpperCase()+k.slice(1)} <b>${d>0?'+':''}${d}</b></span>`;}).join('');
    const body=`<div class="quick-shell quick-feedback"><div class="quick-feedback-icon">${choice.icon}</div><p class="quick-eyebrow">YOUR CHOICE HAS CONSEQUENCES</p><h3>${choice.title}</h3><p class="quick-feedback-summary">${choice.reason}</p><div class="quick-change-grid">${rows}</div>
      <p class="quick-takeaway">💡 Every useful system needs something. A good plan protects what matters most <strong>without forgetting tomorrow.</strong></p>
      <button id="quickNext" type="button" class="quick-next">${nextRound===3?'SEE HOW THE CREW DID':'NEXT CHALLENGE'} <span>→</span></button></div>`;
    const modal=window.MissionUI.modal('MISSION FEEDBACK',body);modal.classList.add('quick-mission-overlay');
    modal.querySelector('#quickNext').onclick=()=>{this.round=nextRound;this.render();};
  }
  renderEnd(){
    const s=this.state;
    const crewSafe=s.battery>=15&&s.air>=50&&s.safety>=65;
    const excellent=crewSafe&&s.food>=50&&s.battery>=35;
    const result=excellent?'Brilliant planning!':crewSafe?'Mission safe!':'A chance to learn more';
    const explain=excellent?'The crew stayed safe AND you left useful reserves for later.':crewSafe?'You protected essential systems. Some resources ran low, so review your choices.':'The crew needs stronger reserves. Try again: protect air and shelter before taking extra research risks.';
    const total=s.battery+s.air+s.safety+s.food;
    window.Beyond?.onQuickMission?.(total,crewSafe);
    let best=0;try{best=Number(localStorage.getItem('moonshift.quick.best'))||0; if(total>best)localStorage.setItem('moonshift.quick.best',String(total));}catch(e){}
    const body=`<div class="quick-shell quick-end"><div class="quick-end-icon">${excellent?'🏆':crewSafe?'🌙':'🧭'}</div><p class="quick-eyebrow">3 CHOICES · 1 MOON BASE</p><h3>${result}</h3><p>${explain}</p><div class="quick-status">${this.badge('🔋 Power','battery')}${this.badge('💨 Air','air')}${this.badge('🛡️ Safety','safety')}${this.badge('🌱 Garden','food')}</div>
      <div class="quick-debrief"><strong>What happened?</strong>${this.history.map((v,i)=>`<p><b>${i+1}. ${v.choice}</b> — ${v.reason}</p>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="quick-actions"><button id="quickRetry" class="quick-next" type="button">↻ TRY ANOTHER PLAN</button><button id="quickExit" class="quick-exit" type="button">BACK TO THE STATION</button></div>
      <p class="quick-footnote">This is a repeatable fictional training scenario, separate from your saved story and NASA observations.</p></div>`;
    const modal=window.MissionUI.modal('RESCUE DRILL COMPLETE',body);modal.classList.add('quick-mission-overlay');
    modal.querySelector('#quickRetry').onclick=()=>this.start();
    modal.querySelector('#quickExit').onclick=()=>modal.remove();
    window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
  }
}
window.QuickMission=new MoonshiftQuickMission();

</script>
    <script>
/** MOONSHIFT Observatory: an actual interactive 2D illustrated learning instrument. */
class LunarObservatory {
 open(){
  const src=window.SpaceArt?.earthSrc;
  if(!src || !window.MissionUI)return;
  const content=`<div class="observatory-intro"><div class="obsv-eyebrow">LUNA-7 · OBSERVATION DOME</div><p>Take a closer look! Choose a view, zoom in, and discover why the Moon and Earth look so different.</p></div>
    <div class="obsv-switch" role="group" aria-label="Choose telescope view"><button id="obsvEarth" class="active" type="button">🌍 Earth</button><button id="obsvMoon" type="button">🌙 Moon</button></div>
    <div class="obsv-viewport" id="obsvViewport"><div class="obsv-stars"></div><div class="obsv-planet" id="obsvPlanet"><img src="${src}" alt="Illustrated Earth with recognizable continents, derived from public-domain Natural Earth geography" draggable="false"></div><svg class="obsv-horizon" viewBox="0 0 680 250" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><path fill="#63717a" d="M0 118 C110 65 220 105 325 95 C453 75 545 122 680 65V250H0Z"/><path fill="#364953" d="M0 167C150 113 240 172 350 129C490 100 565 164 680 113V250H0Z"/><g fill="none" stroke="#9ca7a8" opacity=".46"><ellipse cx="180" cy="160" rx="38" ry="13"/><ellipse cx="482" cy="192" rx="26" ry="8"/><ellipse cx="326" cy="221" rx="42" ry="15"/></g></svg><div class="obsv-reticle"><span></span><span></span></div><div class="obsv-caption" id="obsvCaption">Earth · our home, seen from the Moon</div></div>
    <div class="obsv-controls"><div class="obsv-zoom"><label for="obsvZoom">Zoom</label><input id="obsvZoom" type="range" min="1" max="2" step="0.1" value="1"><strong id="obsvZoomVal">1.0×</strong></div><button id="obsvRecord" class="task-action-btn" type="button">SAVE DISCOVERY</button></div>
    <div class="obsv-fact" id="obsvFact"><strong>Why can we see Earth?</strong><p>The Moon always keeps nearly the same side toward Earth. From many places on that side, Earth stays in roughly the same part of the sky. In this fictional view it appears low above the horizon.</p></div>
    <p class="obsv-credit">Illustrated learning view — not a live telescope feed. Earth land shapes use Natural Earth public-domain data. Learn about <a href="https://science.nasa.gov/resource/image-earthrise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">NASA's real Earthrise image</a>.</p>`;
  const overlay=window.MissionUI.modal('LOOK THROUGH THE TELESCOPE',content);
  overlay.classList.add('observatory-overlay');
  const earth=overlay.querySelector('#obsvEarth'),moon=overlay.querySelector('#obsvMoon'),viewport=overlay.querySelector('#obsvViewport'),zoom=overlay.querySelector('#obsvZoom'),planet=overlay.querySelector('#obsvPlanet'),fact=overlay.querySelector('#obsvFact'),caption=overlay.querySelector('#obsvCaption');
  let mode='earth';
  const paint=()=>{
   earth.classList.toggle('active',mode==='earth'); moon.classList.toggle('active',mode==='moon');
   viewport.classList.toggle('is-moon',mode==='moon');
   const z=+zoom.value;
   planet.style.transform=`translate(-50%,-50%) scale(${z})`;
   overlay.querySelector('#obsvZoomVal').textContent=z.toFixed(1)+'×';
   if(mode==='earth'){
     caption.textContent='Earth · our home, seen from the Moon';
     fact.innerHTML='<strong>Why can we see Earth?</strong><p>The Moon keeps nearly the same side toward Earth. From many places on that side, Earth stays in roughly the same part of the sky. In this fictional view, it appears low above the horizon.</p>';
   }else{
     caption.textContent='Lunar landscape · craters and rocky ridges';
     fact.innerHTML='<strong>Why do craters last so long?</strong><p>The Moon has almost no atmosphere, so there is no wind or rain to wear the craters away like on Earth. Impacts leave marks for very long times.</p>';
   }
  };
  earth.onclick=()=>{mode='earth';paint()};moon.onclick=()=>{mode='moon';paint()};zoom.oninput=paint;
  overlay.querySelector('#obsvRecord').onclick=()=>{
   window.Story?.completedTasks?.add('lunarObservation');
   window.Save?.save();
   window.MissionUI?.notify('🔭 Discovery saved to your Journal.');
   overlay.remove();
  };
  paint();
 }
}
window.Observatory=new LunarObservatory();

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT BEYOND EARTH — WORLD SCENES / v15
 * Original asset-free, prerendered 2D playable environments.
 * World backgrounds and architecture paint ONCE into a cache. The per-frame
 * pass only draws nearby animated instruments, characters and navigation.
 * Space backgrounds and science simulations are intentionally schematic.
 */
class MoonshiftWorldScene {
  constructor(id) {
    this.id=id;
    this.orbit=id==='orbit';
    this.width=2240;this.height=1320;
    this.camera={x:0,y:0};this.clock=0;this.lastPaint=0;
    this.areas=this.orbit?[
      {id:'arrival',x:120,y:445,w:430,h:365,label:'WELCOME MODULE',sub:'Crew arrival · airlock',accent:'#76b5cb'},
      {id:'micro',x:630,y:140,w:450,h:390,label:'MICROGRAVITY BAY',sub:'Floating cargo experiments',accent:'#7fd4dd'},
      {id:'recycle',x:620,y:765,w:470,h:390,label:'LIFE-SUPPORT CORE',sub:'Air recycling and power',accent:'#83bbd1'},
      {id:'cupola',x:1225,y:125,w:480,h:395,label:'EARTH OBSERVATORY',sub:'Cupola research window',accent:'#b1cbd7'},
      {id:'robot',x:1250,y:765,w:475,h:395,label:'ROBOTICS WORKSHOP',sub:'Attitude and navigation',accent:'#bcae91'},
      {id:'dock',x:1760,y:435,w:355,h:420,label:'CARGO DOCK',sub:'Gentle approach & capture',accent:'#8cbec9'}
    ]:[
      {id:'hab',x:105,y:400,w:495,h:435,label:'ARES-1 HABITAT',sub:'Crew safety and shelter',accent:'#dfb296'},
      {id:'solar',x:660,y:122,w:460,h:370,label:'SOLAR FARM',sub:'Clean the array',accent:'#e7b578'},
      {id:'water',x:1320,y:110,w:500,h:380,label:'WATER LAB',sub:'Ice and recycling',accent:'#9bd0ce'},
      {id:'rover',x:680,y:835,w:480,h:365,label:'ROVER GARAGE',sub:'Field exploration',accent:'#dfa080'},
      {id:'dust',x:1250,y:615,w:345,h:305,label:'DUST LOCK',sub:'Keep harmful dust out',accent:'#dda28b'},
      {id:'comms',x:1680,y:790,w:430,h:370,label:'RELAY ANTENNA',sub:'Mars to Earth',accent:'#daa981'}
    ];
    this.corridors=this.orbit?[
      {x:500,y:563,w:1350,h:150},
      {x:785,y:490,w:158,h:165},{x:785,y:679,w:158,h:162},
      {x:1420,y:480,w:162,h:340},
      {x:1046,y:565,w:258,h:150}
    ]:[
      {x:490,y:450,w:520,h:142,angle:-.52},
      {x:485,y:768,w:525,h:152,angle:.40},
      {x:998,y:286,w:425,h:145,angle:0},
      {x:1450,y:456,w:145,h:400,angle:0},
      {x:1550,y:873,w:250,h:136,angle:0}
    ];
    this.boulders=this.orbit?[]:[
      {x:255,y:190,r:33},{x:445,y:200,r:46},{x:620,y:690,r:26},
      {x:1150,y:580,r:30},{x:1885,y:545,r:32},{x:2020,y:300,r:44},
      {x:192,y:1055,r:38},{x:1330,y:1170,r:30},{x:2135,y:1170,r:38}
    ];
    this.doors=this.orbit?[
      {x:535,y:620,vertical:true},{x:861,y:540,vertical:false},
      {x:862,y:758,vertical:false},{x:1498,y:540,vertical:false},
      {x:1498,y:770,vertical:false},{x:1810,y:620,vertical:true}
    ]:[
      {x:590,y:580,vertical:true},{x:736,y:849,vertical:true},
      {x:1534,y:861,vertical:false}
    ];
    for(const d of this.doors){d.open=0;}
    this.buffer=document.createElement('canvas');
    this.buffer.width=this.width;this.buffer.height=this.height;
    this.prerender(this.buffer.getContext('2d',{alpha:false}));
  }
  clamp(v,min,max){return Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));}
  rounded(ctx,x,y,w,h,r,fill,stroke,line=1){
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x,y,w,h,Math.min(r,w/2,h/2));
    if(fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();}
    if(stroke){ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=line;ctx.stroke();}
  }
  circle(ctx,x,y,r,fill,stroke,line=1){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);if(fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();}if(stroke){ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=line;ctx.stroke();}}
  prerender(ctx){
    const W=this.width,H=this.height;
    if(this.orbit){
      const sky=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,W,H);sky.addColorStop(0,'#050f1b');sky.addColorStop(1,'#070d16');ctx.fillStyle=sky;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
      // Quiet, sparse stars, never a distracting grid outside the playable station.
      for(let i=0;i<86;i++){let x=(i*619+101)%W,y=(i*397+211)%H;this.circle(ctx,x,y,i%7===0?1.8:1,'rgba(214,230,244,'+(i%4===0?.35:.18)+')');}
      this.paintEarth(ctx,1950,215,176);
      this.paintSolarWing(ctx,46,460);this.paintSolarWing(ctx,2110,500);
      // Outer ship silhouette follows the interior modules.
      for(const c of this.corridors)this.rounded(ctx,c.x-13,c.y-13,c.w+26,c.h+26,28,'#152638','#263e4b',7);
      for(const room of this.areas)this.rounded(ctx,room.x-15,room.y-15,room.w+30,room.h+30,42,'#172c39','#314c5d',8);
      for(const c of this.corridors)this.paintOrbitFloor(ctx,c,true);
      for(const room of this.areas)this.paintOrbitRoom(ctx,room);
      this.paintOrbitFixtures(ctx);
    }else{
      const sand=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,W,H);sand.addColorStop(0,'#62423b');sand.addColorStop(.55,'#48312e');sand.addColorStop(1,'#372927');ctx.fillStyle=sand;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
      // Broad soft Martian terrain forms with deliberate negative space.
      ctx.fillStyle='#704941';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(350,170,420,120,.1,0,7);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#593a33';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(1700,1220,850,180,-.1,0,7);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#845349';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(1950,240,270,95,.23,0,7);ctx.fill();
      this.paintMarsCrater(ctx,1940,360,126);
      this.paintMarsCrater(ctx,270,1070,116);
      for(let i=0;i<28;i++){
        const x=90+(i*223)%2050,y=60+(i*371)%1190;
        this.circle(ctx,x,y,3+(i%3)*2,'rgba(198,139,102,.15)');
      }
      // Paved paths make each location navigable at a glance.
      const paths=[[[335,615],[900,320]],[[335,615],[920,995]],[[900,320],[1560,290]],[[1560,290],[1430,765]],[[1430,765],[1890,965]],[[920,995],[1430,765]]];
      for(const path of paths){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(...path[0]);ctx.lineTo(...path[1]);ctx.strokeStyle='#674943';ctx.lineWidth=113;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(233,181,135,.22)';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.setLineDash([17,25]);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);}
      for(const room of this.areas)this.paintMarsRoom(ctx,room);
      for(const rock of this.boulders)this.paintRock(ctx,rock);
      this.paintMarsFixtures(ctx);
    }
  }
  paintEarth(ctx,x,y,r){
    ctx.save();const outer=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,r*.55,x,y,r*1.28);outer.addColorStop(0,'rgba(56,142,209,.04)');outer.addColorStop(1,'rgba(56,142,209,0)');this.circle(ctx,x,y,r*1.28,outer);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.3,y-r*.3,r*.12,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,'#5eb9e4');g.addColorStop(.5,'#206ca7');g.addColorStop(1,'#0d2c58');this.circle(ctx,x,y,r,g,'rgba(142,219,244,.6)',4);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r-3,0,7);ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle='#80b9a7';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r*.68,y-r*.44);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-r*.2,y-r*.85,x+r*.02,y-r*.42,x-r*.08,y-r*.05);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+r*.20,y+r*.19,x-r*.14,y+r*.29,x-r*.38,y+r*.46);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-r*.57,y+r*.11,x-r*.8,y-r*.11,x-r*.68,y-r*.44);ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+r*.31,y-r*.1);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+r*.78,y-r*.08,x+r*.83,y+r*.3,x+r*.48,y+r*.66);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+r*.17,y+r*.72,x+r*.02,y+r*.27,x+r*.31,y-r*.1);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(240,248,255,.47)';ctx.lineWidth=r*.095;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r*.65,y-r*.62);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-r*.24,y-r*.44,x+r*.34,y-r*.60,x+r*.68,y-r*.38);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r*.1,y+r*.44);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+r*.2,y+r*.27,x+r*.48,y+r*.55,x+r*.74,y+r*.34);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  }
  paintSolarWing(ctx,x,y){
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,100,405,8,'#192e46','#426879',5);
    for(let row=0;row<8;row++)for(let col=0;col<2;col++)this.rounded(ctx,x+7+col*46,y+9+row*49,40,44,2,'#1f4d78','#426a8c',1);
  }
  paintOrbitFloor(ctx,c,corridor){
    const {x,y,w,h}=c;
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,w,h,corridor?18:24,corridor?'#233b4b':'#223947',corridor?'#4b6672':'#6e8b99',corridor?2:4);
    ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x+6,y+6,w-12,h-12,corridor?12:17);ctx.clip();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(146,177,191,.095)';ctx.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=x+62;px<x+w;px+=96){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px,y+6);ctx.lineTo(px,y+h-6);ctx.stroke();}
    for(let py=y+62;py<y+h;py+=95){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+6,py);ctx.lineTo(x+w-6,py);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
    if(corridor){ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(134,177,187,.25)';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+26,y+h/2);ctx.lineTo(x+w-26,y+h/2);ctx.stroke();}
  }
  paintOrbitRoom(ctx,room){
    this.paintOrbitFloor(ctx,room,false);
    const {x,y,w,h,label,sub,accent}=room;
    this.rounded(ctx,x+20,y+17,Math.min(w-40,340),56,12,'rgba(8,27,39,.73)','rgba(165,205,221,.2)',1);
    ctx.fillStyle=accent;ctx.fillRect(x+34,y+29,4,30);ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.fillStyle='#ecf5f7';ctx.font='700 17px system-ui';ctx.fillText(label,x+49,y+45);
    ctx.fillStyle='#a6becb';ctx.font='12px system-ui';ctx.fillText(sub,x+49,y+63);
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(188,224,232,.20)';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+24,y+h-19);ctx.lineTo(x+w-24,y+h-19);ctx.stroke();
    // Four inset frame lights (restrained, not high-frequency noise)
    for(const px of [x+36,x+w-44])for(const py of [y+90,y+h-55])this.rounded(ctx,px,py,10,10,3,accent);
    if(room.id==='cupola')this.paintCupolaWindow(ctx,x+310,y+250,113);
    if(room.id==='micro')this.paintCargo(ctx,x+45,y+235);
    if(room.id==='recycle')this.paintRecycler(ctx,x+75,y+252);
    if(room.id==='robot')this.paintRobotics(ctx,x+265,y+230);
    if(room.id==='dock')this.paintDockingRing(ctx,x+222,y+252);
    if(room.id==='arrival')this.paintCrewDesk(ctx,x+80,y+252);
  }
  paintCargo(ctx,x,y){
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const px=x+i*67,py=y+(i%2)*24;
      this.rounded(ctx,px,py,49,45,8,'#456171','#96b3ba',2);
      ctx.strokeStyle='#87a5af';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px+12,py+11);ctx.lineTo(px+36,py+34);ctx.moveTo(px+36,py+11);ctx.lineTo(px+12,py+34);ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  paintRecycler(ctx,x,y){
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,172,83,12,'#152b38','#63949e',3);
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){this.rounded(ctx,x+16+i*50,y+18,38,48,10,'#2a586b','#75a7b9',1);this.circle(ctx,x+35+i*50,y+42,9,'#4f98a9');}
    this.rounded(ctx,x+204,y-4,86,76,10,'#203d4e','#6996a6',2);
    for(let j=0;j<4;j++)this.rounded(ctx,x+217,y+10+j*13,60,6,3,'#4f8296');
  }
  paintCupolaWindow(ctx,x,y,r){
    this.circle(ctx,x,y,r+19,'#0b1c2c','#668b9c',8);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.3,y-r*.6,20,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,'#183b5e');g.addColorStop(1,'#061422');this.circle(ctx,x,y,r,g,'#7bbbcf',5);
    ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r-5,0,7);ctx.clip();
    this.paintEarth(ctx,x+15,y-3,r*.55);ctx.restore();
    for(let a=0;a<6;a++){const angle=a*Math.PI/3;ctx.strokeStyle='#5c8396';ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+Math.cos(angle)*(r-15),y+Math.sin(angle)*(r-15));ctx.lineTo(x+Math.cos(angle)*(r+15),y+Math.sin(angle)*(r+15));ctx.stroke();}
  }
  paintRobotics(ctx,x,y){
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,128,82,15,'#32495a','#9ba6a4',3);
    this.circle(ctx,x+30,y+45,15,'#172f40','#87b4c0',3);this.circle(ctx,x+97,y+45,15,'#172f40','#87b4c0',3);
    ctx.strokeStyle='#bda784';ctx.lineWidth=12;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+55,y+40);ctx.lineTo(x+70,y-20);ctx.lineTo(x+112,y-35);ctx.stroke();this.circle(ctx,x+70,y-20,8,'#d4be98');
  }
  paintDockingRing(ctx,x,y){
    this.circle(ctx,x,y,104,'#132839','#8bb3c3',11);this.circle(ctx,x,y,83,'#0b1f30','#526c79',4);
    this.circle(ctx,x,y,53,null,'rgba(154,207,222,.52)',3);
    for(let a=0;a<8;a++){const t=a*Math.PI/4;ctx.strokeStyle='#afbdc1';ctx.lineWidth=6;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+Math.cos(t)*88,y+Math.sin(t)*88);ctx.lineTo(x+Math.cos(t)*102,y+Math.sin(t)*102);ctx.stroke();}
  }
  paintCrewDesk(ctx,x,y){
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,180,78,14,'#435765','#94aab4',2);
    this.rounded(ctx,x+17,y+10,104,45,7,'#112938','#75b8cc',2);
    this.rounded(ctx,x+136,y+14,26,26,5,'#caad85');
  }
  paintOrbitFixtures(ctx){
    // A few wall cabinets and storage hatches; mostly clear negative space.
    this.rounded(ctx,133,735,90,50,8,'#405867','#748f9b',2);
    this.rounded(ctx,995,835,65,70,9,'#335b69','#6a9caa',2);
    this.rounded(ctx,1680,930,35,130,9,'#3e5968','#7299a7',2);
  }
  paintMarsCrater(ctx,x,y,r){
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(-.14);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(49,27,29,.32)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,r*1.12,r*.69,0,0,7);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(222,160,124,.47)';ctx.lineWidth=17;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,r,r*.60,0,0,7);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(15,9,14,.16)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(10,14,r*.80,r*.44,0,0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
  }
  paintRock(ctx,rock){
    const {x,y,r}=rock;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(32,19,20,.20)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x+5,y+r*.62,r*1.25,r*.42,0,0,7);ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='#9a6a55';ctx.strokeStyle='#be8566';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r,y+r*.35);ctx.lineTo(x-r*.7,y-r*.52);ctx.lineTo(x,y-r);ctx.lineTo(x+r*.9,y-r*.43);ctx.lineTo(x+r,y+r*.34);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(238,183,139,.20)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r*.7,y-r*.46);ctx.lineTo(x,y-r);ctx.lineTo(x+r*.2,y-r*.15);ctx.lineTo(x-r*.25,y+r*.22);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
  }
  paintMarsRoom(ctx,room){
    const {x,y,w,h,label,sub,accent,id}=room;
    this.rounded(ctx,x-8,y-8,w+16,h+16,42,'rgba(27,25,31,.24)','rgba(223,173,131,.25)',7);
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,w,h,34,'#574e4b','#bb937c',5);
    this.rounded(ctx,x+16,y+16,w-32,h-32,26,'#665d57','rgba(245,196,149,.20)',2);
    // Habitat plaques are short and remain clear of the science console.
    this.rounded(ctx,x+22,y+22,Math.min(w-44,295),63,13,'#382e31','rgba(238,185,152,.24)',1);
    this.rounded(ctx,x+35,y+35,5,37,3,accent);
    ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.fillStyle='#f8e6d7';ctx.font='700 18px system-ui';ctx.fillText(label,x+54,y+51);
    ctx.fillStyle='#e2c4b3';ctx.font='12px system-ui';ctx.fillText(sub,x+54,y+70);
    if(id==='hab')this.paintCrewDesk(ctx,x+100,y+260);
    if(id==='solar')this.paintMarsSolar(ctx,x+50,y+192);
    if(id==='water')this.paintWaterRig(ctx,x+235,y+210);
    if(id==='rover')this.paintRover(ctx,x+250,y+223);
    if(id==='dust')this.paintMarsFilter(ctx,x+200,y+167);
    if(id==='comms')this.paintMarsAntenna(ctx,x+240,y+224);
  }
  paintMarsSolar(ctx,x,y){
    for(let j=0;j<2;j++)for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const px=x+i*69,py=y+j*88;this.rounded(ctx,px,py,58,67,4,'#273c52','#a6aaad',2);
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++)this.rounded(ctx,px+5+k*17,py+6,14,55,1,'#3f668a','rgba(173,215,243,.2)',.6);
    }
  }
  paintWaterRig(ctx,x,y){
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
      this.rounded(ctx,x+i*76,y,61,101,22,'#828f8b','#d2c9b8',3);
      this.rounded(ctx,x+9+i*76,y+16,44,55,12,'#457988','#8bc6d2',2);
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle='#88b8bb';ctx.lineWidth=7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+60,y+48);ctx.lineTo(x+78,y+48);ctx.stroke();
  }
  paintRover(ctx,x,y){
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,145,82,22,'#d0b39a','#f0d6b4',3);
    this.rounded(ctx,x+20,y+15,100,44,12,'#5d7580','#bddee3',3);
    for(const px of [x+12,x+132])for(const py of [y+1,y+73])this.circle(ctx,px,py,12,'#29282a','#b7a191',3);
    ctx.strokeStyle='#e4c4a0';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+73,y+5);ctx.lineTo(x+92,y-36);ctx.stroke();this.circle(ctx,x+92,y-36,7,'#9bd7e0');
  }
  paintMarsFilter(ctx,x,y){
    this.rounded(ctx,x,y,102,108,19,'#677d78','#bbad96',3);
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++)this.rounded(ctx,x+12,y+13+i*17,78,8,3,'#29464c');
    this.circle(ctx,x+84,y+91,7,'#d4b088');
  }
  paintMarsAntenna(ctx,x,y){
    ctx.strokeStyle='#d2c4af';ctx.lineWidth=8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y+90);ctx.lineTo(x,y);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='#c5baa7';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-18,y-12,71,37,-.36,0,7);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#f5dbc0';ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-18,y-12,71,37,-.36,0,7);ctx.stroke();
    this.circle(ctx,x+1,y-26,7,'#75aaba');
  }
  paintMarsFixtures(ctx){
    // Sparse outpost markers make traversing the sand legible, without clutter.
    for(const [x,y] of [[650,580],[1150,760],[1650,710],[575,940]]){
      this.circle(ctx,x,y,9,'#d8ad80','#efd1a0',2);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(247,221,177,.32)';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y-4);ctx.lineTo(x,y-30);ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  isWalkable(x,y,r=18){
    if(x<45||y<48||x>this.width-45||y>this.height-45)return false;
    if(!this.orbit){
      return !this.boulders.some(b=>Math.hypot(x-b.x,y-b.y)<b.r+r*.65);
    }
    return [...this.areas,...this.corridors].some(s=>x>=s.x+Math.min(r,20)&&x<=s.x+s.w-Math.min(r,20)&&y>=s.y+Math.min(r,20)&&y<=s.y+s.h-Math.min(r,20));
  }
  move(m,dt,pressed){
    let dx=Number(pressed.has('right'))-Number(pressed.has('left'));
    let dy=Number(pressed.has('down'))-Number(pressed.has('up'));
    const mag=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(mag){dx/=mag;dy/=mag;}
    if(m.active){dx=0;dy=0;}
    let speed=m.movement||{vx:0,vy:0};m.movement=speed;
    const acceleration=this.orbit?650:920,drag=this.orbit?2.8:10,maxSpeed=this.orbit?225:250;
    speed.vx+=(dx*acceleration-speed.vx*drag)*dt;
    speed.vy+=(dy*acceleration-speed.vy*drag)*dt;
    const vel=Math.hypot(speed.vx,speed.vy);
    if(vel>maxSpeed){speed.vx*=maxSpeed/vel;speed.vy*=maxSpeed/vel;}
    if(Math.abs(speed.vx)<.6)speed.vx=0;if(Math.abs(speed.vy)<.6)speed.vy=0;
    const nx=m.player.x+speed.vx*dt,ny=m.player.y+speed.vy*dt;
    if(this.isWalkable(nx,m.player.y))m.player.x=nx;else speed.vx=0;
    if(this.isWalkable(m.player.x,ny))m.player.y=ny;else speed.vy=0;
    this.clock+=dt;
    for(const d of this.doors){
      const distance=Math.hypot(m.player.x-d.x,m.player.y-d.y);
      const target=distance<165?1:0;
      d.open+=((target-d.open)*Math.min(1,dt*6.8));
    }
  }
  render(ctx,m,nodes,next){
    const VW=960,VH=540,px=m.player.x,py=m.player.y;
    const zoom=window.innerWidth<=650?1.55:1;
    const viewHalfW=VW/(2*zoom),viewHalfH=VH/(2*zoom);
    const targetX=this.clamp(px,viewHalfW,this.width-viewHalfW),targetY=this.clamp(py,viewHalfH,this.height-viewHalfH);
    if(!this.camera.ready){this.camera.x=targetX;this.camera.y=targetY;this.camera.ready=true;}
    else{const sm=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,this.frameDt||.016)*7.2);this.camera.x+=(targetX-this.camera.x)*sm;this.camera.y+=(targetY-this.camera.y)*sm;}
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,VW,VH);ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(VW/2-this.camera.x*zoom,VH/2-this.camera.y*zoom);
    ctx.scale(zoom,zoom);
    const cropX=this.clamp(this.camera.x-viewHalfW-30,0,this.width),cropY=this.clamp(this.camera.y-viewHalfH-30,0,this.height);
    const cropW=Math.min(this.width-cropX,viewHalfW*2+60),cropH=Math.min(this.height-cropY,viewHalfH*2+60);
    ctx.drawImage(this.buffer,cropX,cropY,cropW,cropH,cropX,cropY,cropW,cropH);
    // Calm, inexpensive animated world features (only when near viewport).
    if(this.orbit){
      this.spinFan(ctx,833,979,28,1);
      this.spinFan(ctx,904,979,28,-1);
      // drifting tethered calibration orb in Microgravity Bay
      const f=Math.sin(this.clock*.85);
      this.circle(ctx,738+f*18,462+Math.cos(this.clock*.85)*12,14,'#e4b889','#f4d4ad',2);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(205,222,225,.35)';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(712,478);ctx.lineTo(738+f*18,462+Math.cos(this.clock*.85)*12);ctx.stroke();
    }else{
      // Lightweight dust stream: visual hazard changes the environment.
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(241,189,147,.12)';
      for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const x=(((i*203)+(this.clock*28))%(this.width+190))-60,y=(i*137)%this.height;this.rounded(ctx,x,y,28+i%3*15,2,1,'rgba(239,188,148,.11)');}
    }
    this.paintDynamicDoors(ctx);
    for(let i=0;i<nodes.length;i++){
      const n=nodes[i];if(Math.abs(n.x-this.camera.x)>VW*.7||Math.abs(n.y-this.camera.y)>VH*.72)continue;
      this.paintConsole(ctx,n,m.progress.has(n.id),n===next,Math.hypot(n.x-px,n.y-py)<105);
    }
    this.paintAstronaut(ctx,px,py,m);
    ctx.restore();
    // Small, noninvasive edge cue when the selected station is outside the viewport.
    if(next){
      const dx=next.x-px,dy=next.y-py,distance=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
      if(distance>170){
        const angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx),cx=VW/2+Math.cos(angle)*210,cy=VH/2+Math.sin(angle)*166;
        ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.fillStyle=this.orbit?'#bce7ec':'#f6d4ad';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(13,0);ctx.lineTo(-6,-9);ctx.lineTo(-6,9);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
        this.rounded(ctx,VW/2-155,VH-51,310,34,14,'rgba(5,16,25,.75)','rgba(132,180,192,.25)',1);
        ctx.fillStyle='#e7f0f2';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.font='600 14px system-ui';ctx.fillText(`${next.name} · ${Math.round(distance/13)} steps`,VW/2,VH-29);
      }
    }
  }
  spinFan(ctx,x,y,r,dir){
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(this.clock*.88*dir);ctx.strokeStyle='#8fb8c3';ctx.lineWidth=8;ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=i*Math.PI*2/3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*4,Math.sin(a)*4);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  paintDynamicDoors(ctx){
    if(!this.orbit)return;
    for(const d of this.doors){
      // Split panels retreat into walls: no opaque capsule over the path.
      ctx.save();const open=d.open;
      if(d.vertical){
        const width=12+(1-open)*29;
        this.rounded(ctx,d.x-5,d.y-56,width,48,8,'#a0b6be','#dae8e7',2);
        this.rounded(ctx,d.x-5,d.y+8,width,48,8,'#a0b6be','#dae8e7',2);
      }else{
        const width=45*(1-open)+6;
        this.rounded(ctx,d.x-48,d.y-7,width,17,7,'#9fb4b9','#c4d3da',2);
        this.rounded(ctx,d.x+42-width,d.y-7,width,17,7,'#9fb4b9','#c4d3da',2);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }
  paintConsole(ctx,n,completed,target,near){
    const c=completed?'#8dbba7':this.orbit?'#8dc9d6':'#e5bd93';
    ctx.save();
    // Hardware resembles a real work terminal rather than glowing rings.
    this.rounded(ctx,n.x-46,n.y-41,92,74,18,this.orbit?'#1c3849':'#564943',c,near?3:2);
    this.rounded(ctx,n.x-34,n.y-29,68,46,9,this.orbit?'#122b3c':'#2e3037',null,0);
    ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.font='29px system-ui';ctx.fillStyle='#f4f8f8';ctx.fillText(completed?'✓':n.icon,n.x,n.y-7);
    if(target&&!completed){
      ctx.strokeStyle='#eed8a7';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.setLineDash([7,8]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(n.x,n.y-4,58+Math.sin(this.clock*2)*2,0,7);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }
    // Context labels appear when near or when this is the objective.
    if(near||target){
      ctx.font='700 13px system-ui';const tw=ctx.measureText(n.name).width;
      this.rounded(ctx,n.x-tw/2-13,n.y+44,tw+26,30,12,'rgba(7,19,28,.94)','rgba(156,191,204,.20)',1);
      ctx.fillStyle='#eaf4f4';ctx.fillText(n.name,n.x,n.y+59);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  paintAstronaut(ctx,x,y,m){
    const {vx=0,vy=0}=m.movement||{};
    const bob=this.orbit?Math.sin(this.clock*2)*3:(Math.hypot(vx,vy)>10?Math.sin(this.clock*11)*2:0);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y+bob);
    this.circle(ctx,2,30,24,'rgba(1,13,22,.22)');
    this.rounded(ctx,-19,-20,38,50,16,'#e5e9e7','#28404d',4);
    this.circle(ctx,3,-13,16,'#f5f8f5','#2c4d60',3);
    ctx.fillStyle=this.orbit?'#75bfd7':'#d79c7e';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(8,-14,12,8,-.2,0,7);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#dae8ed';ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-14,6);ctx.lineTo(-26,12);ctx.moveTo(14,6);ctx.lineTo(25,11);ctx.stroke();
    // PIX follows at a polite distance.
    const fx=-49+Math.sin(this.clock*1.35)*7,fy=22+Math.sin(this.clock*2.5)*5;
    this.circle(ctx,fx,fy,15,'#152b3b','#8fd1e1',3);this.circle(ctx,fx,fy+23,16,'rgba(71,148,173,.09)');
    ctx.fillStyle='#b7f0fb';ctx.fillRect(fx-8,fy-3,6,4);ctx.fillRect(fx+2,fy-3,6,4);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
window.MoonshiftWorldScene=MoonshiftWorldScene;

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT: BEYOND EARTH — self-contained 2D expansion.
 * Keeps the LUNA-7 story and simulation untouched while adding a playable
 * two-world expedition vertical slice, science hologram, progression, and opening story.
 * The points/resources in expedition scenarios are authored game values, not NASA measurements.
 */
class MoonshiftBeyond {
  constructor(){
    this.key='moonshift.beyond.v1';
    this.worldSaveKey='moonshift.expedition.checkpoint.v15';
    this.state={version:1,xp:0,awarded:[],moon:false,orbit:false,mars:false,best:{},prologueSeen:false};
    this.read();this.prologueIndex=0;this.prologueOverlay=null;
    this.mission=null;this._frame=0;this._last=0;this._pressed=new Set();this._activeTouch=null;
    this.worlds={
      moon:{id:'moon',eyebrow:'01 · LUNAR CHAPTER',name:'LUNA-7',subtitle:'The Lost Signal',gravity:1.62,icon:'☾',color:'#98cbdc',blurb:'Save your crew, protect the air and restore communications.'},
      orbit:{id:'orbit',eyebrow:'02 · ORBITAL CHAPTER',name:'THE ORBITAL LAB',subtitle:'The Silent Experiment',gravity:null,icon:'◉',color:'#8ebeea',blurb:'Explore an orbiting laboratory and learn why floating objects still have momentum.'},
      mars:{id:'mars',eyebrow:'03 · MARS CHAPTER',name:'ARES-1',subtitle:'The Red Planet Mystery',gravity:3.71,icon:'◌',color:'#d88e72',blurb:'Help a Mars outpost through dust, scarce power and long radio delays.'}
    };
    this.nodes={
      orbit:[
        {id:'orbitDrift',icon:'▣',name:'Microgravity Lab',x:838,y:337,desc:'Stop drifting cargo with a gentle push.'},
        {id:'orbitAir',icon:'✳',name:'Recycler Console',x:850,y:957,desc:'Balance fresh air with limited electricity.'},
        {id:'orbitCupola',icon:'◉',name:'Earth Observatory',x:1380,y:351,desc:'Plan science observations while protecting crew time.'},
        {id:'orbitGyro',icon:'⌁',name:'Robotics Attitude Lab',x:1442,y:974,desc:'Align a satellite using careful turning.'},
        {id:'orbitDock',icon:'⟁',name:'Docking Ring',x:1874,y:636,desc:'Approach slowly enough for a safe capture.'}
      ],
      mars:[
        {id:'marsSolar',icon:'☀',name:'Solar Farm',x:863,y:322,desc:'Restore sunlight to dusty solar panels.'},
        {id:'marsWater',icon:'◈',name:'Water Recovery Lab',x:1465,y:308,desc:'Choose how to recover water without wasting power.'},
        {id:'marsDust',icon:'≋',name:'Dust Airlock',x:1392,y:739,desc:'Keep drifting dust out of the habitat.'},
        {id:'marsRover',icon:'⦿',name:'Rover Workshop',x:863,y:1007,desc:'Collect samples while protecting the rover battery.'},
        {id:'marsSignal',icon:'⌁',name:'Relay Antenna',x:1872,y:981,desc:'Plan commands across a long radio delay.'}
      ]
    };
    this.prologue=[
      {who:'Fatema',label:'THE JOURNEY BEGINS',text:'Far from home, our crew is learning how to live beyond Earth. Today, you are joining us.'},
      {who:'Eyad',label:'APPROACHING THE MOON',text:'LUNA-7, this is Eyad at Mission Control. Our new astronaut is almost here.'},
      {who:'Fatema',label:'A FRIEND IS WAITING',text:'Wait! That’s our new astronaut? I practiced my welcome speech forty-seven times!'},
      {who:'Rewaa',label:'A MESSAGE FROM THE GREENHOUSE',text:'Hi, Rewaa here! Our first Moon seedlings are growing. I saved a little experiment for you.'},
      {who:'Eyad',label:'A STRANGE SIGNAL',text:'Incoming message... That signal is not coming from Mission Control.'},
      {who:'Fatema',label:'YOUR FIRST DECISION',text:'Something interesting is happening. Ready to investigate together?'}
    ];
  }
  read(){try{const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.key)||'null');if(raw?.version===1){this.state={...this.state,...raw,awarded:Array.isArray(raw.awarded)?raw.awarded:[],best:raw.best&&typeof raw.best==='object'?raw.best:{}};}}catch(_){} }
  save(){try{localStorage.setItem(this.key,JSON.stringify(this.state));return true;}catch(_){return false;}}
  award(id,points){if(this.state.awarded.includes(id))return false;this.state.awarded.push(id);this.state.xp+=Math.max(0,Math.floor(points));this.save();this.updateHud();return true;}
  onLunarTask(taskId){
    const values={stationTelemetry:45,greenhouseLight:75,powerRouting:95,co2Scrubber:85,waterRecovery:85,stormShutter:95,dsnAlign:120,gravityLab:45,roverSurvey:45,thermalBalance:45};
    if(values[taskId])this.award('moon-'+taskId,values[taskId]);
    if(taskId==='dsnAlign')this.completeMoon();
  }
  completeMoon(){
    if(this.state.moon)return;
    this.state.moon=true;this.award('moon-complete',200);this.save();
    this.toast('✦ LUNA-7 complete! The Orbital Lab is now unlocked.');
  }
  onQuickMission(score,safe){
    this.state.best.rescue=Math.max(this.state.best.rescue||0,Math.round(score));
    if(safe)this.award('rescue-safe',90);
    this.save();
  }
  sync(){
    const tasks=window.Story?.completedTasks;
    if(tasks){tasks.forEach(id=>this.onLunarTask(id));if(tasks.has('dsnAlign'))this.completeMoon();}
    this.updateHud();
  }
  rank(){const n=this.state.xp;return n>=1150?'Mission Commander':n>=850?'Mars Pathfinder':n>=550?'Orbital Specialist':n>=300?'Lunar Engineer':n>=120?'Junior Explorer':'Space Cadet';}
  toast(message){window.MissionUI?.notify?.(message);}
  mount(){
    const bar=document.querySelector('.hud-actions-group');
    if(bar && !document.getElementById('beUniverseBtn')){
      const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='hud-btn be-hub-button';btn.id='beUniverseBtn';btn.title='Destinations and astronaut progress';btn.innerHTML='✦ <span>WORLDS</span>';btn.onclick=()=>this.openHub();
      bar.insertBefore(btn,document.getElementById('missionMenuBtn'));
    }
    this.sync();
    if(!this.state.prologueSeen){setTimeout(()=>{if(!this.state.prologueSeen)this.showPrologue();},450);}
  }
  updateHud(){const e=document.getElementById('beRankChip');if(e)e.textContent=`✦ ${this.rank()} · ${this.state.xp} XP`;}
  closeAll(){document.querySelectorAll('.be-prologue,.be-hub-overlay,.be-expedition-overlay,.be-hololab-overlay').forEach(n=>n.remove());this.stopMission();window.Voice?.stop();}
  showPrologue(replay=false){
    window.Voice?.stop();
    if(this.prologueOverlay?.isConnected)this.prologueOverlay.remove();
    this.prologueIndex=0;
    const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='task-overlay be-prologue';overlay.setAttribute('role','dialog');overlay.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');overlay.setAttribute('aria-label','Beyond Earth opening story');
