    overlay.innerHTML=`<div class="be-cosmos" aria-hidden="true"><span class="be-cosmos-glow"></span><div class="be-celestial-earth"><span class="be-earth-continent one"></span><span class="be-earth-continent two"></span><span class="be-cloud"></span></div><div class="be-celestial-moon"></div><div class="be-cosmos-ship">✧<span class="be-ship-trail"></span></div></div>
      <div class="be-intro-ui"><div class="be-top-brand"><span class="be-logo">✦ MOONSHIFT</span><span>BEYOND EARTH · PROLOGUE</span></div>
      <div class="be-prologue-bottom"><div class="be-prologue-progress" id="bePrologueProgress"></div><p class="be-eyebrow" id="beIntroLabel"></p><h1 class="be-intro-title" id="beIntroTitle">Beyond Earth</h1><p class="be-intro-line" id="beIntroLine"></p><div class="be-intro-actions"><button type="button" class="be-cta" id="beIntroNext">BEGIN THE JOURNEY <span>→</span></button><button type="button" class="be-text-btn" id="beIntroSkip">Skip introduction</button><button type="button" class="be-text-btn" id="beVoiceToggle">Voice: ${window.Voice?.enabled?'On':'Off'}</button></div><p class="be-intro-caption">Original illustrated story · no account needed · no personal data collected</p></div></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);this.prologueOverlay=overlay;
    overlay.querySelector('#beIntroNext').onclick=()=>this.advancePrologue();
    overlay.querySelector('#beIntroSkip').onclick=()=>this.endPrologue();
    overlay.querySelector('#beVoiceToggle').onclick=()=>{window.Audio?.init();window.Voice?.toggle();overlay.querySelector('#beVoiceToggle').textContent=`Voice: ${window.Voice?.enabled?'On':'Off'}`;this.cancelPrologueSpeechTimer();if(window.Voice?.enabled)this.speakPrologue();};
    this.paintPrologue();overlay.querySelector('#beIntroNext').focus();
  }
  paintPrologue(){
    const overlay=this.prologueOverlay;if(!overlay?.isConnected)return;
    const scene=this.prologue[this.prologueIndex];
    overlay.querySelector('#beIntroLabel').textContent=`${String(this.prologueIndex+1).padStart(2,'0')} / ${String(this.prologue.length).padStart(2,'0')} · ${scene.label}`;
    overlay.querySelector('#beIntroTitle').textContent=scene.who;
    overlay.querySelector('#beIntroLine').textContent=scene.text;
    overlay.querySelector('#beIntroNext').innerHTML=(window.Voice?.enabled&&window.Voice?.supported&&!window.Voice?.activated?'TAP TO START WITH VOICE':this.prologueIndex===this.prologue.length-1?'START EXPLORING':'CONTINUE STORY')+' <span>→</span>';
    overlay.querySelector('#bePrologueProgress').innerHTML=this.prologue.map((_,i)=>`<i class="${i<=this.prologueIndex?'is-current':''}"></i>`).join('');
    overlay.dataset.step=String(this.prologueIndex);
    this.speakPrologue();
  }
  cancelPrologueSpeechTimer(){if(this._prologueSpeechTimer){clearTimeout(this._prologueSpeechTimer);this._prologueSpeechTimer=null;}}
  speakPrologue(){
    this.cancelPrologueSpeechTimer();
    const scene=this.prologue[this.prologueIndex];if(!scene)return;
    const index=this.prologueIndex;
    window.Voice?.speak(scene.who,scene.text,{
      onEnd:()=>{
        if(this.prologueIndex!==index||!this.prologueOverlay?.isConnected)return;
        this._prologueSpeechTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
          this._prologueSpeechTimer=null;
          if(this.prologueIndex===index&&this.prologueOverlay?.isConnected)this.advancePrologue();
        },1150);
      }
    });
  }
  advancePrologue(){
    window.Audio?.init();
    if(window.Voice?.supported&&window.Voice?.enabled&&!window.Voice?.activated){
      window.Voice.activate();this.paintPrologue();return;
    }
    this.cancelPrologueSpeechTimer();window.Voice?.stop();
    if(this.prologueIndex>=this.prologue.length-1){this.endPrologue();return;}
    this.prologueIndex++;this.paintPrologue();
  }
  endPrologue(){
    this.cancelPrologueSpeechTimer();window.Voice?.stop();
    // Skipping the intro is itself a valid first user gesture to enable speech.
    if(window.Voice?.enabled&&!window.Voice?.activated)window.Voice.activate();
    this.state.prologueSeen=true;this.save();this.prologueOverlay?.remove();this.prologueOverlay=null;
    // The Moon story was created before the player could interact, so play its
    // current line now that the prologue supplied a trusted user gesture.
    if(window.Story?.isDialogueActive)window.Story.startCurrentDialogueVoice?.();
  }
  planetSvg(id){
    if(id==='moon' && window.SpaceArt?.urls?.moon) return `<img src="${window.SpaceArt.urls.moon}" class="be-planet" alt="" draggable="false">`;
    if(id==='mars')return `<svg viewBox="0 0 200 200" class="be-planet" aria-hidden="true"><defs><radialGradient id="beMarsG"><stop stop-color="#efae8a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#914939"/></radialGradient></defs><circle cx="100" cy="100" r="72" fill="url(#beMarsG)"/><path d="M36 95 Q65 70 104 88 T164 85 M35 123 Q85 109 111 131 T155 130" fill="none" stroke="#70382e" stroke-opacity=".33" stroke-width="17"/><path d="M67 54 Q100 38 128 56" fill="none" stroke="#ffc6a3" stroke-opacity=".32" stroke-width="8"/></svg>`;
    if(window.SpaceArt?.earthSrc) return `<img src="${window.SpaceArt.earthSrc}" class="be-planet" alt="" draggable="false">`;
    return `<svg viewBox="0 0 200 200" class="be-planet" aria-hidden="true"><defs><radialGradient id="beEarthG"><stop stop-color="#71cdf6"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#124d96"/></radialGradient></defs><circle cx="100" cy="100" r="72" fill="url(#beEarthG)"/><path d="M72 31 Q120 30 129 55 L114 81 L89 86 L69 111 L53 88 L59 57 Z" fill="#84cbb0"/><path d="M117 118 Q155 103 156 136 L136 166 L112 154 L102 130 Z" fill="#8fcda4"/><path d="M39 93 Q63 74 86 92 M109 55 Q146 49 158 75" fill="none" stroke="#d8f5ff" stroke-width="11" opacity=".55"/></svg>`;
  }
  openHub(){
    this.sync();this.stopMission();document.querySelector('.be-hub-overlay')?.remove();
    const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='task-overlay be-hub-overlay';overlay.setAttribute('role','dialog');overlay.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');overlay.setAttribute('aria-label','Beyond Earth universe map');
    const cards=Object.values(this.worlds).map(w=>{
      const unlocked=w.id==='moon'||this.state.moon&&w.id==='orbit'||this.state.orbit&&w.id==='mars';
      const done=w.id==='moon'?this.state.moon:this.state[w.id];
      return `<button type="button" class="be-world-card ${unlocked?'is-unlocked':'is-locked'}" data-world="${w.id}" ${unlocked?'':'disabled'}>
        <div class="be-world-planet">${this.planetSvg(w.id)}</div><span class="be-world-eyebrow">${w.eyebrow}</span><h3>${w.name}</h3><p class="be-world-subtitle">${w.subtitle}</p><p class="be-world-blurb">${w.blurb}</p><span class="be-world-state">${done?'✓ Completed · Replay':unlocked?w.id==='moon'?'Continue Moon Mission':'Explore this world':'🔒 Complete the previous world'}</span></button>`;
    }).join('');
    overlay.innerHTML=`<div class="be-hub-shell"><header class="be-hub-header"><div><p class="be-eyebrow">YOUR ADVENTURE BEYOND EARTH</p><h2>The Universe Atlas</h2><p>One skill, three very different worlds. Finish the Moon to unlock Earth orbit, then Mars.</p></div><button class="be-round-close" type="button" aria-label="Close universe map">×</button></header>
      <div class="be-hub-score"><div><span class="be-score-label">YOUR RANK</span><strong>${this.rank()}</strong></div><div><span class="be-score-label">EXPERIENCE</span><strong>${this.state.xp} XP</strong></div><div><span class="be-score-label">WORLDS COMPLETE</span><strong>${['moon','orbit','mars'].filter(id=>this.state[id]).length} / 3</strong></div></div>
      <section class="be-world-grid">${cards}</section><div class="be-hub-foot"><button type="button" class="be-secondary" id="beHoloLabBtn">✧ Open the Gravity HoloLab</button><button type="button" class="be-secondary" id="beProfileBtn">♜ Astronaut profile & best scores</button><button type="button" class="be-secondary" id="beReplayIntroBtn">▶ Replay the opening story</button></div><p class="be-provenance">Illustrated schematic—not to scale. Lunar and Martian gravity values come from NASA references. Scenario scores and power budgets are game-training assumptions, not NASA mission readings.</p></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    overlay.querySelector('.be-round-close').onclick=()=>overlay.remove();
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-world]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const id=btn.dataset.world;overlay.remove();if(id!=='moon')this.startWorld(id);});
    overlay.querySelector('#beHoloLabBtn').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.openGravityLab();};
    overlay.querySelector('#beProfileBtn').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.openProfile();};
    overlay.querySelector('#beReplayIntroBtn').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.showPrologue(true);};
    overlay.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===overlay)overlay.remove();});
  }
  openProfile(){
    const wins=[['moon','Moon Station'],['orbit','Orbital Lab'],['mars','Mars Expedition']].map(([id,name])=>`<div class="be-profile-achievement"><b>${this.state[id]?'✦':'○'}</b><span>${name}</span><strong>${this.state[id]?'COMPLETED':'NOT YET'}</strong></div>`).join('');
    const entries=Object.entries(this.state.best||{}).map(([k,score])=>`<div class="be-profile-achievement"><b>★</b><span>${({orbit:'Orbital rescue',mars:'Mars expedition',rescue:'PIX Rescue Drill'})[k]||k}</span><strong>${score} pts</strong></div>`).join('');
    const overlay=window.MissionUI.modal('YOUR ASTRONAUT PROFILE',`<div class="be-profile-header"><div class="be-profile-icon">✦</div><div><p class="be-eyebrow">LUNA-7 ASTRONAUT</p><h3>${this.rank()}</h3><p>${this.state.xp} XP earned by exploring, learning and completing missions.</p></div></div><div class="be-profile-list"><h4>Mission badges</h4>${wins}</div><div class="be-profile-list"><h4>Personal best scores</h4>${entries||'<p>Finish an expedition to record your first personal best.</p>'}</div><p class="be-provenance">Scores stay on this device. No public ranking, account, school name or personal data is required.</p><button class="be-cta" id="beBackWorlds">Back to Universe Atlas →</button>`);
    overlay.querySelector('#beBackWorlds').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.openHub();};
  }
  startWorld(id){
    if(id!=='orbit' && id!=='mars')return;
    if(id==='orbit'&&!this.state.moon || id==='mars'&&!this.state.orbit){this.toast('Complete the previous world to unlock this adventure.');return;}
    this.stopMission();
    window.Audio?.setAmbientMood?.(id);
    const w=this.worlds[id];
    this._simulationTime=0;this._lastPaintTime=0;this._readyToComplete=false;
    this.mission={id,progress:new Set(),player:id==='mars'?{x:340,y:625}:{x:327,y:642},movement:{vx:0,vy:0},score:0,energy:id==='mars'?85:80,air:85,water:id==='mars'?55:70,science:0,safe:75,active:false,startedAt:performance.now(),scene:new window.MoonshiftWorldScene(id),waypoint:null};
    try{
      const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.worldSaveKey+'.'+id)||'null');
      if(saved?.id===id && saved.version===1 && Array.isArray(saved.progress) && saved.progress.length<this.nodes[id].length){
        this.mission.progress=new Set(saved.progress.filter(task=>this.nodes[id].some(n=>n.id===task)));
        for(const key of ['energy','air','water','science','safe','score'])if(Number.isFinite(saved[key]))this.mission[key]=Math.max(0,Math.min(key==='score'?1000:100,saved[key]));
        if(saved.player && this.mission.scene.isWalkable(saved.player.x,saved.player.y))this.mission.player={x:saved.player.x,y:saved.player.y};
        this.mission.waypoint=saved.waypoint||null;
      }
    }catch(_){}
    const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className=`task-overlay be-expedition-overlay be-world-${id}`;overlay.setAttribute('role','dialog');overlay.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');overlay.setAttribute('aria-label',w.name+' expedition');
    overlay.innerHTML=`<div class="be-expedition-shell"><header class="be-expedition-top"><div><p class="be-eyebrow">${w.eyebrow}</p><h2>${w.name} <span>${w.subtitle}</span></h2></div><div class="be-expedition-stats"><div>⚡ <span id="beEnergy">${this.mission.energy}</span><small>ENERGY</small></div><div>💨 <span id="beAir">${this.mission.air}</span><small>AIR</small></div>${id==='mars'?`<div>💧 <span id="beWater">${this.mission.water}</span><small>WATER</small></div>`:''}<div>✦ <span id="beScience">0</span><small>SCIENCE</small></div></div><button class="be-round-close" type="button" id="beExitWorld" aria-label="Return to universe map">×</button></header>
      <div class="be-expedition-content"><div class="be-map-zone"><div class="be-world-hint" aria-live="polite"><span class="be-hint-dot"></span><span>${id==='orbit'?'FREE-FLOAT RESEARCH STATION · LIVE PLAY':'MARTIAN OUTPOST · LIVE PLAY'}</span><span class="be-hint-keys">WASD · E</span></div><canvas id="beWorldCanvas" width="960" height="540" aria-label="Scrollable 2D playable world of ${w.name}, not an overview map"></canvas><div class="be-expedition-controls"><div class="be-dpad"><button type="button" data-dir="up" aria-label="Move up">↑</button><div><button type="button" data-dir="left" aria-label="Move left">←</button><button type="button" data-dir="down" aria-label="Move down">↓</button><button type="button" data-dir="right" aria-label="Move right">→</button></div></div><button type="button" id="beInteract" class="be-interact">✧ INTERACT</button></div><button type="button" class="be-mobile-missions" id="beMissionListToggle" aria-expanded="false">☷ MISSION LIST · 0/5 <span>↓</span></button></div>
      <aside class="be-mission-aside"><p class="be-eyebrow">YOUR ADVENTURE</p><h3 id="beMissionHeading">${id==='orbit'?'The Silent Experiment':'The Red Planet Mystery'}</h3><p id="beMissionPrompt">${id==='orbit'?'A missing research signal. Five rooms, five discoveries. Follow the highlighted objective, or explore freely.':'A dust emergency at the new Mars outpost. Plan energy, air, and science before the relay goes silent.'}</p><div class="be-checklist">${this.nodes[id].map((n,i)=>`<button type="button" class="be-check" data-check="${n.id}" data-target="${n.id}" aria-label="Show direction to ${n.name}"><span>0${i+1}</span><div><strong>${n.name}</strong><small>${n.desc}</small></div><b>○</b></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="be-expedition-note">WASD / arrows to move · E to interact · tap any mission in the list to get directions. No timer, and mistakes are safe.</p></aside></div>
      <div class="be-activity" id="beWorldActivity" hidden></div></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);this.mission.overlay=overlay;
    if(window.innerWidth<=650){const c=overlay.querySelector('#beWorldCanvas');c.width=768;c.height=432;}
    overlay.querySelector('#beExitWorld').onclick=()=>{this.stopMission();this.openHub();};
    overlay.querySelector('#beInteract').onclick=()=>this.interact();
    overlay.querySelector('#beMissionListToggle')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const open=overlay.classList.toggle('be-show-log');
      const toggle=overlay.querySelector('#beMissionListToggle');
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));
      toggle.querySelector('span').textContent=open?'↑':'↓';
    });
    const setDir=(dir,on)=>{if(on)this._pressed.add(dir);else this._pressed.delete(dir);};
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-dir]').forEach(btn=>{const dir=btn.dataset.dir;btn.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();try{btn.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){/* synthetic and older touch events */}setDir(dir,true)});btn.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>setDir(dir,false));btn.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>setDir(dir,false));btn.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>setDir(dir,false));});
    this._worldKeyDown=e=>{if(this.mission?.active)return;const map={KeyW:'up',ArrowUp:'up',KeyS:'down',ArrowDown:'down',KeyA:'left',ArrowLeft:'left',KeyD:'right',ArrowRight:'right'};if(map[e.code]){e.preventDefault();this._pressed.add(map[e.code]);}if(e.code==='KeyE'||e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();this.interact();}};
    this._worldKeyUp=e=>{const map={KeyW:'up',ArrowUp:'up',KeyS:'down',ArrowDown:'down',KeyA:'left',ArrowLeft:'left',KeyD:'right',ArrowRight:'right'};if(map[e.code])this._pressed.delete(map[e.code]);};
    window.addEventListener('keydown',this._worldKeyDown,true);window.addEventListener('keyup',this._worldKeyUp,true);
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-target]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{const n=this.nodes[id].find(x=>x.id===button.dataset.target);if(n){this.mission.waypoint=n.id;overlay.classList.remove('be-show-log');overlay.querySelector('#beMissionListToggle')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');this.refreshMission();this.toast('Route set · Follow the small arrow toward '+n.name);}});
    this._worldBlur=()=>{this._pressed.clear();if(this.mission?.movement){this.mission.movement.vx=0;this.mission.movement.vy=0;}};
    window.addEventListener('blur',this._worldBlur);
    this._last=performance.now();this._frame=requestAnimationFrame(t=>this.tick(t));this.refreshMission();
    this.showWorldBrief(id);

  }
  saveMissionCheckpoint(){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;
    try{localStorage.setItem(this.worldSaveKey+'.'+m.id,JSON.stringify({version:1,id:m.id,progress:[...m.progress],player:{x:m.player.x,y:m.player.y},energy:m.energy,air:m.air,water:m.water,science:m.science,safe:m.safe,score:m.score,waypoint:m.waypoint}));}catch(_){}
  }
  stopMission(){
    this.saveMissionCheckpoint();
    if(this._frame)cancelAnimationFrame(this._frame);this._frame=0;
    if(this._worldKeyDown){window.removeEventListener('keydown',this._worldKeyDown,true);window.removeEventListener('keyup',this._worldKeyUp,true);}
    if(this._worldBlur)window.removeEventListener('blur',this._worldBlur);this._worldBlur=null;
    window.Voice?.stop?.();
    this._worldKeyDown=null;this._worldKeyUp=null;this._pressed.clear();
    this.mission?.overlay?.remove();this.mission=null;
    window.Audio?.setAmbientMood?.('moon');
  }
  tick(t){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;
    const dt=Math.min(Math.max(0,(t-this._last)/1000),.045);this._last=t;
    // Save CPU/battery when hidden; the mission never keeps running in the background.
    if(document.hidden){this._frame=requestAnimationFrame(time=>this.tick(time));return;}
    const interval=window.innerWidth<=650?32:16;
    if(this._lastPaintTime&&t-this._lastPaintTime<interval){this._frame=requestAnimationFrame(time=>this.tick(time));return;}
    this._lastPaintTime=t;
    if(!m.active)m.scene.move(m,Math.min((t-(this._simulationTime||t))/1000,.06)||dt,this._pressed);
    this._simulationTime=t;
    this.drawWorld();
    this._frame=requestAnimationFrame(time=>this.tick(time));
  }
  drawWorld(){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;
    const canvas=m.overlay.querySelector('#beWorldCanvas'),ctx=canvas?.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});if(!ctx)return;
    const next=this.nodes[m.id].find(n=>n.id===m.waypoint&&!m.progress.has(n.id))||this.nodes[m.id].find(n=>!m.progress.has(n.id));
    const renderScale=canvas.width/960;ctx.setTransform(renderScale,0,0,renderScale,0,0);
    m.scene.frameDt=Math.min((performance.now()-(this._simulationTime||performance.now()))/1000,.04)||.016;
    m.scene.render(ctx,m,this.nodes[m.id],next);
  }
  showWorldBrief(id){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;
    const orbit=id==='orbit';
    const words=orbit?{
      heading:'A STATION IN FREE FALL',speaker:'Eyad',
      line:'Welcome aboard! Everything is floating, but every object still has momentum. We lost a science signal. Help the crew keep air flowing, control our machines, and dock a supply capsule.',
      note:'In orbit, astronauts and their station fall around Earth together. That is why they seem weightless. No gravity switch is turned off.'
    }:{
      heading:'WELCOME TO MARS',speaker:'Fatema',
      line:'Whoa. A whole new planet! Dust is covering our solar panels, and we have a rover to rescue. We need water, power, and a smart plan because Earth is far away.',
      note:'Mars has 3.71 m/s² surface gravity, a thin atmosphere, and long Earth communication delays. Game energy numbers are simplified training values.'
    };
    m.active=true;this._pressed.clear();
    const panel=m.overlay.querySelector('#beWorldActivity');panel.hidden=false;
    panel.innerHTML=`<div class="be-activity-card be-brief-card"><button type="button" class="be-activity-close" id="beBriefSkip" aria-label="Skip introduction">×</button><p class="be-eyebrow">${orbit?'ORBITAL LABORATORY':'ARES-1 SURFACE OUTPOST'} · STORY</p><h3>${words.heading}</h3><div class="be-brief-voice"><span>✦</span><div><strong>${words.speaker}</strong><p>${words.line}</p></div></div><div class="be-explain"><strong>One thing to know</strong><p>${words.note}</p></div><p class="be-help">Explore five connected stations. Every decision changes the mission. Tap an item in the mission log to select it as your destination.</p><button type="button" class="be-cta" id="beBriefBegin">LET'S EXPLORE →</button></div>`;
    const close=()=>{window.Voice?.stop();panel.hidden=true;panel.innerHTML='';m.active=false;};
    panel.querySelector('#beBriefSkip').onclick=close;panel.querySelector('#beBriefBegin').onclick=close;
    if(window.Voice?.enabled)window.Voice.speak(words.speaker,words.line);
  }

  interact(){const m=this.mission;if(!m||m.active)return;let nearest=null,dist=Infinity;for(const n of this.nodes[m.id]){const d=Math.hypot(n.x-m.player.x,n.y-m.player.y);if(d<dist){dist=d;nearest=n;}}if(dist>112){this.toast('Go nearer to a glowing science console, or select it from the mission log.');return;}this.showActivity(nearest);}
  showActivity(node){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;m.active=true;this._pressed.clear();m.movement.vx=0;m.movement.vy=0;
    const panel=m.overlay.querySelector('#beWorldActivity');panel.hidden=false;
    let content='';
    const intro=`<div class="be-activity-top"><p class="be-eyebrow">${m.id==='orbit'?'THE ORBITAL LAB':'ARES-1'} · SCIENCE CHALLENGE</p><h3>${node.name}</h3><p>${node.desc}</p></div>`;
    if(node.id==='orbitDrift') content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">🌍 <strong>Learn:</strong> In orbit, things still have momentum. A floating box keeps drifting unless a force changes its speed.</div><label class="be-control-label" for="beImpulse">Tiny push (left is negative)</label><input type="range" id="beImpulse" min="-1" max="0.2" step="0.1" value="0"/><div class="be-value-line"><span>Box begins drifting right at <b>+0.6 m/s</b></span><strong id="beImpulseValue">Final: +0.6 m/s</strong></div><p class="be-help">Try to make the final speed as close to 0 as possible. No timer or penalty for experimenting.</p><button class="be-cta" id="beCommitActivity">APPLY THE PUSH</button>`;
    else if(node.id==='orbitDock')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">🛰 <strong>Learn:</strong> Docking depends on relative speed. Too fast can damage equipment; slowing down takes time and energy.</div><label class="be-control-label" for="beDockSpeed">Choose approach speed</label><input type="range" id="beDockSpeed" min="0.05" max="0.6" step="0.05" value="0.3"/><div class="be-value-line"><span>Target for this training scenario</span><strong id="beDockValue">0.30 m/s</strong></div><p class="be-help">In this simplified drill, approach speeds at or below 0.15 m/s count as careful docking.</p><button class="be-cta" id="beCommitActivity">BEGIN DOCKING</button>`;
    else if(node.id==='orbitAir') content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">💨 <strong>Learn:</strong> The crew needs healthy air. Extra experiments also use the station's shared electricity.</div>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'💨',title:'Air first',desc:'Safer air, but spend 16 energy.',info:'Air +13 · Energy −16'},{id:1,icon:'⚖',title:'Balanced plan',desc:'Keep air healthy and continue careful research.',info:'Air +7 · Energy −10 · Science +16'},{id:2,icon:'🔬',title:'Research first',desc:'Many samples, but air protection drops.',info:'Air −18 · Energy −7 · Science +30'}])}`;
    else if(node.id==='marsSolar')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">☀ <strong>Learn:</strong> Mars has a thin, dusty atmosphere. Dust on solar panels can reduce the electricity they produce.</div>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'🧽',title:'Clean the panels',desc:'Spend 8 energy now; gain 18 through improved solar generation in the next simulated period.',info:'Net energy +10 · Science +18'},{id:1,icon:'🔋',title:'Conserve energy',desc:'Avoid extra electricity use now, keep reserves for later.',info:'Energy saved vs. cleaning · Air −6 · Science +6'},{id:2,icon:'🔬',title:'Study the dust',desc:'Gain science, but postpone maintenance.',info:'Energy −11 · Air −11 · Science +28'}])}`;
    else if(node.id==='marsRover')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">🚙 <strong>Learn:</strong> Longer rover journeys collect more observations, but batteries and distance limit what is safe.</div>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'🪨',title:'Nearby crater',desc:'Short, safe visit with fewer samples.',info:'Energy −6 · Science +13'},{id:1,icon:'🧭',title:'Ridge survey',desc:'Moderate energy for a useful sample trip.',info:'Energy −17 · Science +27'},{id:2,icon:'⛰',title:'Distant canyon',desc:'More discoveries, high battery cost.',info:'Energy −37 · Science +39 · Safety −14'}])}`;
    else if(node.id==='orbitGyro')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">🧭 <strong>Try:</strong> A spacecraft can rotate without turning like a car. Small reaction-control thrusters help it point the right way. The target angle in this fictional training scenario is <b>35°</b>.</div><div class="be-sim-figure"><div class="be-sim-angle" id="beGyroDrawing"><span>✦</span></div><div><strong>Target 35°</strong><small>Turn only as much as you need. More correction uses extra propellant.</small></div></div><label class="be-control-label" for="beGyroAngle">Choose a pointing angle</label><input type="range" id="beGyroAngle" min="0" max="90" step="5" value="0"/><div class="be-value-line"><span>Pointing error</span><strong id="beGyroValue">35° away</strong></div><button class="be-cta" id="beCommitActivity">ALIGN THE SPACECRAFT</button>`;
    else if(node.id==='orbitCupola')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">🌍 <strong>Discover:</strong> From a cupola, astronauts photograph Earth and record environmental changes. Science instruments use energy and crew time.</div><div class="be-sim-earth">${this.planetSvg('earth')}<div><strong>Earth below the station</strong><small>This is a stylized illustration, not a live camera feed.</small></div></div>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'📷',title:'Short observation',desc:'Take a small photo series, preserving battery for other work.',info:'Energy −5 · Science +10'},{id:1,icon:'🔭',title:'Focused observation',desc:'Collect a useful set of observations and keep life support on schedule.',info:'Energy −10 · Science +23'},{id:2,icon:'🌌',title:'All-night imaging',desc:'More photos, but much more power and reduced safety reserve.',info:'Energy −25 · Safety −13'}])}`;
    else if(node.id==='marsWater')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">💧 <strong>Learn:</strong> Water on Mars may be found as ice. Getting useful clean water takes energy and treatment. How much should we process today?</div><div class="be-sim-pipeline"><span>🧊 ICE</span><b>→</b><span>⚙️ RECOVERY</span><b>→</b><span>💧 CLEAN WATER</span></div>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'🫗',title:'Small careful batch',desc:'Use a little energy for a modest water reserve.',info:'Energy −6 · Water +6'},{id:1,icon:'💧',title:'Balanced recovery',desc:'Give the crew useful water without draining the battery.',info:'Energy −13 · Water +20 · Science +16'},{id:2,icon:'♨️',title:'Maximum recovery',desc:'Recover more, but at a high energy cost.',info:'Energy −27 · Water +30 · Science +20'}])}`;
    else if(node.id==='marsDust')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">🌬 <strong>Learn:</strong> Mars dust is fine and can cling to equipment. Keeping it out of the habitat helps protect machines and breathing systems.</div><div class="be-sim-pipeline"><span>🏜 DUST</span><b>→</b><span>🚪 AIRLOCK</span><b>→</b><span>🌿 CLEAN CABIN</span></div>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'🧹',title:'Quick manual sweep',desc:'Save power, but some dust still reaches the filters.',info:'Energy −3 · Air −6'},{id:1,icon:'🧼',title:'Use the dust-removal cycle',desc:'Spend moderate power to protect the living area.',info:'Energy −11 · Air +12 · Safety +10'},{id:2,icon:'⚡',title:'Run every cleaning system',desc:'Very thorough, but spends a lot of power.',info:'Energy −24 · Air +14 · Safety +12'}])}`;
    else if(node.id==='marsSignal')content=`${intro}<div class="be-explain">📡 <strong>Learn:</strong> A radio message travels at light speed. Earth–Mars messages take minutes, not seconds, so astronauts and rovers must plan ahead.</div><label class="be-control-label" for="beMarsDistance">Earth–Mars distance in this scenario</label><input type="range" id="beMarsDistance" min="75" max="405" step="1" value="225"/><div class="be-value-line"><span>Distance: <b id="beDistanceLabel">225 million km</b></span><strong id="beDelayLabel">≈ 12.5 minutes one way</strong></div><p class="be-help">NASA describes roughly 4–24 minutes one-way depending on distance. Which instruction is safest when replies are delayed?</p>${this.choicesHTML([{id:0,icon:'🔴',title:'Send one short steering turn',desc:'Requires a new command soon after.',info:'Weak plan for a long delay'},{id:1,icon:'🛑',title:'Send a safe autonomous route',desc:'Rover avoids hazards without waiting for Earth.',info:'Good planning +23 science'},{id:2,icon:'⚡',title:'Keep driving fast',desc:'No plan for obstacles while messages travel.',info:'Riskier during communication delays'}])}`;
    const done=m.progress.has(node.id);
    panel.innerHTML=`<div class="be-activity-card"><button type="button" class="be-activity-close" aria-label="Close science challenge">×</button>${content}<p class="be-provenance">Scenario power, air, scores and target speeds are invented educational parameters. NASA source references are available in the game journal.</p>${done?'<p class="be-already-done">✓ Challenge completed. Replay is for learning; XP is awarded once.</p>':''}</div>`;
    panel.querySelector('.be-activity-close').onclick=()=>this.closeActivity();
    if(node.id==='orbitDrift'){
      const r=panel.querySelector('#beImpulse'),v=panel.querySelector('#beImpulseValue');const update=()=>{const sum=.6+Number(r.value);v.textContent=`Final: ${sum>0?'+':''}${sum.toFixed(2)} m/s`;v.classList.toggle('is-safe',Math.abs(sum)<=.12);};r.oninput=update;update();
      panel.querySelector('#beCommitActivity').onclick=()=>{const s=Math.abs(.6+Number(r.value));const pts=s<=.12?95:s<=.32?68:35;this.finishActivity(node,pts, s<=.12?'Brilliant! Your small push almost stopped the drift.':`The box still moves at ${s.toFixed(2)} m/s. A smaller final speed would be safer.`,{energy:-8,air:0,science:s<=.12?20:8,safe:0});};
    } else if(node.id==='orbitDock'){
      const r=panel.querySelector('#beDockSpeed'),v=panel.querySelector('#beDockValue');r.oninput=()=>v.textContent=Number(r.value).toFixed(2)+' m/s';
      panel.querySelector('#beCommitActivity').onclick=()=>{const speed=Number(r.value),pts=speed<=.15?92:speed<=.30?55:20;this.finishActivity(node,pts,speed<=.15?'Careful docking! Slower relative motion reduces impact risk.':'That approach was a little fast. The crew wants a gentler docking plan.',{energy:speed<=.15?-12:-5,air:0,science:pts>=90?20:8,safe:speed<=.15?8:-12});};
    } else if(node.id==='orbitGyro'){
      const r=panel.querySelector('#beGyroAngle'),v=panel.querySelector('#beGyroValue'),draw=panel.querySelector('#beGyroDrawing');
      const update=()=>{const error=Math.abs(Number(r.value)-35);v.textContent=`${error}° away`;v.classList.toggle('is-safe',error<=5);draw.style.setProperty('--angle',r.value+'deg');};r.oninput=update;update();
      panel.querySelector('#beCommitActivity').onclick=()=>{const error=Math.abs(Number(r.value)-35),pts=error<=5?96:error<=15?70:37;this.finishActivity(node,pts,error<=5?'Excellent! A small, accurate turn aligned the spacecraft.':'The craft is still misaligned. Careful pointing saves fuel and avoids repeated corrections.',{energy:error<=5?-8:-15,air:0,science:error<=5?23:8,safe:error<=5?7:-8});};
    } else {
      panel.querySelectorAll('[data-be-choice]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const choice=Number(btn.dataset.beChoice);this.resolveChoice(node,choice);});
      if(node.id==='marsSignal'){const r=panel.querySelector('#beMarsDistance'),d=panel.querySelector('#beDistanceLabel'),v=panel.querySelector('#beDelayLabel');r.oninput=()=>{const km=Number(r.value)*1000000;d.textContent=`${r.value} million km`;v.textContent=`≈ ${(km/299792.458/60).toFixed(1)} minutes one way`;};}
    }
  }
  choicesHTML(choices){return `<div class="be-choice-list">${choices.map(c=>`<button type="button" class="be-choice" data-be-choice="${c.id}"><span class="be-choice-icon">${c.icon}</span><span><strong>${c.title}</strong><small>${c.desc}</small><em>${c.info}</em></span><b>→</b></button>`).join('')}</div>`;}
  resolveChoice(node,c){
    const opts={
      orbitAir:[{s:75,d:{energy:-16,air:13,science:5,safe:5},text:'Safer air, but the extra electricity reduces stored energy.'},{s:95,d:{energy:-10,air:7,science:16,safe:4},text:'Good balance! You protected breathing and kept research moving.'},{s:42,d:{energy:-7,air:-18,science:30,safe:-12},text:'More research, but air protection suffered. Not every point is worth the risk.'}],
      marsSolar:[{s:95,d:{energy:10,air:0,science:18,safe:8},text:'Cleaning costs 8 units now but adds 18 units from improved generation during the next simulated period: net +10. A fictional teaching scenario.'},{s:74,d:{energy:0,air:-6,science:6,safe:1},text:'Conserving energy avoids extra spending today, but it does not create electricity or remove the dust.'},{s:38,d:{energy:-11,air:-11,science:28,safe:-11},text:'You learned about the dust, but left the power problem unresolved.'}],
      marsRover:[{s:80,d:{energy:-6,air:0,science:13,safe:8},text:'Safe and efficient. Fewer samples, but lots of battery left.'},{s:95,d:{energy:-17,air:0,science:27,safe:5},text:'Great compromise between exciting science and energy reserves.'},{s:46,d:{energy:-37,air:0,science:39,safe:-14},text:'More samples, but the rover spent too much reserve for a comfortable return.'}],
      orbitCupola:[{s:72,d:{energy:-5,air:0,science:10,safe:4},text:'A quick observation preserves the battery, but gathers fewer measurements.'},{s:96,d:{energy:-10,air:0,science:23,safe:6},text:'Great observation plan! Useful science, with power left for the crew.'},{s:42,d:{energy:-25,air:0,science:32,safe:-13},text:'More images, but long imaging sessions can compete with other priorities.'}],
      marsWater:[{s:76,d:{energy:-6,air:0,water:6,science:8,safe:3},text:'A small water batch preserves energy but brings less water for the crew.'},{s:96,d:{energy:-13,air:0,water:20,science:16,safe:8},text:'Balanced water recovery: useful supplies without emptying your batteries.'},{s:62,d:{energy:-27,air:0,water:30,science:20,safe:-2},text:'Extra water, but high energy demand leaves less margin for an emergency.'}],
      marsDust:[{s:55,d:{energy:-3,air:-6,science:3,safe:-7},text:'A quick sweep saves electricity but leaves the air filters with extra dust.'},{s:96,d:{energy:-11,air:12,science:12,safe:10},text:'Smart! You reduced dust and protected the cabin without wasting power.'},{s:64,d:{energy:-24,air:14,science:14,safe:12},text:'Very clean, but electricity is precious on Mars. Use only what you need.'}],
      marsSignal:[{s:35,d:{energy:-5,air:0,science:7,safe:-7},text:'Tiny commands are hard to coordinate when Earth is many light-minutes away.'},{s:98,d:{energy:-9,air:0,science:23,safe:10},text:'Exactly! Autonomous safety rules help when an answer from Earth takes minutes.'},{s:25,d:{energy:-17,air:0,science:12,safe:-16},text:'High speed without autonomous obstacle planning increases danger during radio delay.'}]
    };
    const chosen=opts[node.id]?.[c];if(!chosen)return;
    this.finishActivity(node,chosen.s,chosen.text,chosen.d);
  }
  finishActivity(node,score,message,delta){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;
    const first=!m.progress.has(node.id);
    if(first){m.progress.add(node.id);m.score+=score;}
    if(first){for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(delta))m[k]=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,m[k]+v));}
    this.saveMissionCheckpoint();
    window.Audio?.uiConfirm();
    const panel=m.overlay.querySelector('#beWorldActivity');
    panel.innerHTML=`<div class="be-activity-card be-activity-result"><p class="be-eyebrow">${first?'DISCOVERY UNLOCKED':'PRACTICE REPLAY'}</p><div class="be-result-mark">${score>=85?'✦':'✓'}</div><h3>${score>=85?'Excellent space thinking!':'A useful discovery!'}</h3><p>${message}</p><div class="be-result-score"><span>Decision score</span><strong>${score} / 100</strong></div><p class="be-help">Your decision changes what the crew can afford next. A strong mission balances safety, energy and useful science.</p><button type="button" class="be-cta" id="beActivityContinue">BACK TO THE MAP →</button></div>`;
    panel.querySelector('#beActivityContinue').onclick=()=>this.closeActivity();
    this.refreshMission();
    if(first&&m.progress.size===this.nodes[m.id].length){this._readyToComplete=true;}
  }
  closeActivity(){const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;m.active=false;const panel=m.overlay.querySelector('#beWorldActivity');panel.hidden=true;panel.innerHTML='';if(this._readyToComplete){this._readyToComplete=false;this.completeWorld();}}
  refreshMission(){const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;const toggle=m.overlay.querySelector('#beMissionListToggle');if(toggle)toggle.innerHTML=`☷ MISSION LIST · ${m.progress.size}/5 <span>${m.overlay.classList.contains('be-show-log')?'↑':'↓'}</span>`;for(const [label,key] of [['beEnergy','energy'],['beAir','air'],['beWater','water'],['beScience','science']]){const el=m.overlay.querySelector('#'+label);if(el)el.textContent=Math.round(m[key]);}m.overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-check]').forEach(el=>{const done=m.progress.has(el.dataset.check);el.classList.toggle('is-done',done);el.classList.toggle('is-target',el.dataset.check===m.waypoint);el.querySelector('b').textContent=done?'✓':'○';});const prompt=m.overlay.querySelector('#beMissionPrompt');if(prompt){const todo=this.nodes[m.id].filter(n=>!m.progress.has(n.id));prompt.textContent=todo.length?`${todo.length} science mission${todo.length===1?'':'s'} remaining. Select a mission below, follow the in-world arrow, and make your decisions. No timer.`:'All five experiments complete! Your expedition report is ready.';}}
  completeWorld(){
    const m=this.mission;if(!m)return;
    const base=Math.round(m.score/this.nodes[m.id].length);
    const reservePenalty=(m.energy<12?12:0)+(m.air<45?15:0)+(m.safe<55?12:0)+(m.id==='mars'&&m.water<20?10:0);
    const score=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,base-reservePenalty));
    const id=m.id,prev=this.state.best[id]||0,first=!this.state[id];
    this.state.best[id]=Math.max(score,prev);this.state[id]=true;
    this.award(`world-${id}`,220);
    this.save();this.stopMission();
    try{localStorage.removeItem(this.worldSaveKey+'.'+id);}catch(_){}
    window.Audio?.taskSuccess();
    const overlay=window.MissionUI.modal(id==='orbit'?'ORBITAL MISSION COMPLETE':'MARS MISSION COMPLETE',`<div class="be-completion"><div class="be-completion-icon">${id==='orbit'?'🌍':'🪐'}</div><p class="be-eyebrow">NEW WORLD COMPLETED</p><h3>${id==='orbit'?'The Silent Experiment':'The Red Planet Mystery'}</h3><p>${reservePenalty?'You completed the expedition, but some supplies are low. Replay with safer reserves to improve your score.':'Excellent planning! Your five discoveries helped keep the crew safe and the mission running.'}</p><div class="be-result-score"><span>Mission strategy score</span><strong>${score} / 100</strong></div><p>Energy remaining: <b>${Math.round(m.energy)}</b> · Air: <b>${Math.round(m.air)}</b>${id==='mars'?` · Water: <b>${Math.round(m.water)}</b>`:''} · Science: <b>${Math.round(m.science)}</b></p><div class="be-completion-actions"><button type="button" class="be-cta" id="beGoHub">${id==='orbit'?'UNLOCK MARS EXPEDITION':'VIEW ALL WORLDS'} →</button><button type="button" class="be-secondary" id="beReplayWorld">↻ REPLAY FOR A BETTER SCORE</button></div><p class="be-provenance">This is an authored game scenario using real physics concepts; point totals are not NASA measurements.</p></div>`);
    overlay.querySelector('#beGoHub').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.openHub();};
    overlay.querySelector('#beReplayWorld').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.startWorld(id);};
    if(id==='orbit'&&first)this.toast('Mars unlocked! Your next adventure is waiting.');
  }
  openGravityLab(){
    document.querySelector('.be-hololab-overlay')?.remove();
    const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='task-overlay be-hololab-overlay';
    overlay.innerHTML=`<div class="be-hololab-shell"><header class="be-hub-header"><div><p class="be-eyebrow">HOLOLAB · GRAVITY EXPERIMENT</p><h2>One object. Three worlds.</h2><p>Drop the same ball from 10 meters. Watch how gravity changes the fall.</p></div><button type="button" class="be-round-close" id="beCloseHolo">×</button></header><div class="be-gravity-grid">${[{id:'earth',title:'EARTH',g:9.81,c:'#8dccf3'},{id:'moon',title:'MOON',g:1.62,c:'#e7e4d7'},{id:'mars',title:'MARS',g:3.71,c:'#dba48b'}].map(p=>`<div class="be-gravity-col"><strong>${p.title}</strong><span>${p.g} m/s²</span><div class="be-gravity-track"><div class="be-gravity-ball" id="beBall${p.id}" style="--ball:${p.c}"></div><div class="be-gravity-ground"></div></div><small id="beTime${p.id}">Ready to drop</small></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="be-holo-foot"><button type="button" class="be-cta" id="beDropBtn">DROP ALL THREE BALLS ↓</button><button type="button" class="be-secondary" id="beHoloBack">BACK TO UNIVERSE ATLAS</button></div><p class="be-provenance">Constant-gravity, no-air-resistance classroom model: h = 10m, fall time = √(2h / g). Earth, Moon and Mars gravity values are published by NASA. The orbiting lab differs because everything there is in orbital free fall.</p></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    overlay.querySelector('#beCloseHolo').onclick=()=>overlay.remove();
    overlay.querySelector('#beHoloBack').onclick=()=>{overlay.remove();this.openHub();};
    let frame=0;
    overlay.querySelector('#beDropBtn').onclick=()=>{
      if(frame)cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
      const ballData=[{id:'earth',g:9.81},{id:'moon',g:1.62},{id:'mars',g:3.71}];
      const start=performance.now();
      const update=(now)=>{
        if(!overlay.isConnected)return;
        const time=(now-start)/1000;let all=true;
        for(const p of ballData){const t=Math.sqrt(20/p.g),ratio=Math.min(1,p.g*time*time/20);if(ratio<1)all=false;
          const ball=overlay.querySelector('#beBall'+p.id);ball.style.transform=`translateY(${Math.round(ratio*210)}px)`;
          overlay.querySelector('#beTime'+p.id).textContent=ratio<1?`${time.toFixed(1)}s falling`:`Landed in ${t.toFixed(2)}s`;
        }
        if(!all)frame=requestAnimationFrame(update);else{frame=0;this.award('gravity-compare',65);}
      };
      frame=requestAnimationFrame(update);
    };
  }
  reset(){this.stopMission();try{localStorage.removeItem(this.worldSaveKey+'.orbit');localStorage.removeItem(this.worldSaveKey+'.mars');}catch(_){}this.state={version:1,xp:0,awarded:[],moon:false,orbit:false,mars:false,best:{},prologueSeen:false};this.save();this.updateHud();}
}
window.Beyond=new MoonshiftBeyond();
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(()=>window.Beyond.mount(),100));

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT: THE LOST SIGNAL
 * Master 2D Engine, Camera, HUD & Game Loop
 * Pure 2D Top-Down WebGL/Canvas Architecture.
 */

class MoonshiftEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.canvas = null;
        this.ctx = null;
        this.lastTime = 0;
        this.camera = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 1280, h: 720 };
        this.zoom = 1.22; // crisp, readable 2D sprites; screen-space HUD remains unscaled
        
        // Input state
        this.input = {
            up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false,
            interact: false,
            joyX: 0, joyY: 0, joystickActive: false
        };

        this.player = null;
        this.pix = null;
        this.crew = null;
        this.nearbyInteractable = null;
        this.screenShake = 0;

        this.activeRoomName = 'Arrival Dock';
    }

    init() {
        this.canvas = document.getElementById('gameCanvas');
        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');

        this.resize();
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => this.resize());

        // Spawn player at Arrival Dock (x: 400, y: 1220)
        this.player = new window.PlayerClass(400, 1220);
        this.pix = new window.PixClass(this.player);
        this.crew = new window.CrewClass();

        // Setup Input Listeners
        this.setupKeyboard();
        this.setupTouchControls();

        // Initialize Audio on first user interaction
        const startAudio = () => {
            if (window.Audio) { window.Audio.init(); window.Audio.resume(); }
            if (window.Audio?.ctx?.state === 'running') {
                window.removeEventListener('keydown', startAudio);
                window.removeEventListener('click', startAudio);
                window.removeEventListener('touchstart', startAudio);
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener('keydown', startAudio);
        window.addEventListener('click', startAudio);
        window.addEventListener('touchstart', startAudio);

        // Returning to a game tab must not leave movement keys stuck.
        window.addEventListener('blur', () => this.clearMovementInput());
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (document.hidden) this.clearMovementInput(); });

        // Resume a previous mission or start fresh.
        if (!window.Save || !window.Save.restore()) window.Story.start();
        if (window.Save) window.Save.beginAutoSave();

        // Start 60 FPS Game Loop
        this.lastTime = performance.now();
        requestAnimationFrame((t) => this.loop(t));
    }

    resize() {
        const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        this.canvas.width = window.innerWidth * dpr;
        this.canvas.height = window.innerHeight * dpr;
        this.ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
        this.camera.w = window.innerWidth / this.zoom;
        this.camera.h = window.innerHeight / this.zoom;
    }

    clearMovementInput() {
        for (const k of ['up','down','left','right','interact','joystickActive']) this.input[k]=false;
        this.input.joyX=0; this.input.joyY=0;
        if (this.player) { this.player.vx=0; this.player.vy=0; this.player.isMoving=false; }
    }

    setupKeyboard() {
        window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.repeat) return;
            const code = e.code;
            const overlayOpen = !!document.querySelector('.task-overlay, .mission-guide-overlay');
            if (['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Space'].includes(code) && !overlayOpen) e.preventDefault();
            if (code==='Escape') {
                if (document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay')) { document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay')?.remove(); return; }
                if (window.Minigames?.activeMinigame) { window.Minigames.close(); return; }
                if (window.Journal?.isOpen) { window.Journal.close(); return; }
            }
            if (overlayOpen || window.Journal?.isOpen) return;
            if (e.target?.matches?.('input,textarea,select,button')) return;
            if (code === 'KeyW' || code === 'ArrowUp') this.input.up = true;
            if (code === 'KeyS' || code === 'ArrowDown') this.input.down = true;
            if (code === 'KeyA' || code === 'ArrowLeft') this.input.left = true;
            if (code === 'KeyD' || code === 'ArrowRight') this.inp
